# Spark Cloud 计划 2：Agent 网关与节点 Agent Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 让实例状态机在生产中真正被驱动：平台侧新增 gRPC + mTLS 的 Agent 网关（内置 CA、一次性令牌接入、指令投递与补推、心跳指标、StateReport 对账、指令超时），机器侧交付单二进制 `spark-agent`（docker CLI 运行时、loop 镜像数据盘、iptables/tc 隔离与限速、指标采集、步骤级幂等），后台增加「机器接入」界面；端到端测试改走真实网关 + 步进式进程内 fake Agent，最后在 LAN 上的 DGX Spark（192.168.1.221）完成真机验收。

**Architecture:** 平台仍是 Go 单体：新增 `internal/pki`（CA 与证书）、`internal/agentgw`（gRPC 网关，`:9443`，多副本正确性由 `machines.agent_conn_id` 保证）、`internal/agentpb`（protoc 生成代码，入库）；`fleet` 增加连接归属 / 指令认领 / 接入令牌的数据层，`rental` 增加对账 `Reconcile`、按实例恢复计时的 `ResumeMachine`、指令超时 `FailTimedOutCommands`。机器侧 `internal/agent/{cmdrun,state,metrics,runtime,disk,netfilter,executor,client,fakeagent}` + `cmd/spark-agent`：所有系统操作都 exec 命令（docker / iptables / tc / losetup / mkfs），通过可注入的 `cmdrun.Runner` 在 macOS 上做命令快照单测；`runtime/disk/netfilter` 各有 fake 实现，供集成测试、e2e-server（步进模式）与 `spark-agent run --fake` 使用。

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27（`~/.local/go/bin`）、pgx v5、chi v5、embedded-postgres；新增 `google.golang.org/grpc v1.84.0`、`google.golang.org/protobuf v1.36.12`（模块缓存已有）；`protoc 36.2` + `protoc-gen-go v1.36.12` + `protoc-gen-go-grpc v1.6.2`（`~/.local/bin`，只在 `make proto` 时用）；`golang.org/x/crypto/ssh`（已有依赖，用于上游 ed25519 密钥与容器主机密钥）；前端 Vue 3 + Element Plus + Vitest + Playwright（计划 5 已有工具链）。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-18-plan2-agent-design.md`（总设计 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-spark-cloud-mvp-design.md` §1、§2、§3、§4、§10；e2e 规范与覆盖表见 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-plan5-frontend-design.md` §7.3、§7.5）。

**分支：** `feat/plan2-agent`（设计文档已提交 8990200）。

**本计划不包含：** sshpiper 与 Traefik（计划 3）、`UpdatePorts`（随计划 3）、档期预订、机器证书续期、上游 SSH 密钥轮换、容器可写层限额、时序监控、Agent 自动升级（均列入遗留）。

## Global Constraints

- 仓库根目录 `/Users/rifuyiri/Documents/claude/机房搭建/spark-cloud`；所有命令前置 `export PATH=$HOME/.local/go/bin:$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH`。Go 模块名 `spark-cloud`。开发 Mac 没有 Docker，也不能 sudo；测试用 embedded-postgres。
- 协议定义 `api/proto/agent/v1/agent.proto`，生成代码 `internal/agentpb/` **提交进仓库**；`make proto` 重新生成；日常 `go build` / `go test` 不需要 protoc。
- 新端口 `SPARK_AGENT_ADDR`（默认 `:9443`），与 8080 / 8081 分开监听；TLS 最低 1.3，`ClientAuth = VerifyClientCertIfGiven`：`Enroll` 不要求客户端证书，`Connect` 必须有并通过校验，否则 `Unauthenticated`。
- 根 CA：ECDSA P-256，20 年；私钥用 `secretbox`（`SPARK_SECRET_KEY`）加密存 `platform_pki` 单行，`INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` 再读取。服务端证书 1 年、由 `SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS` 决定 SAN、不落盘、每日检查剩余 < 30 天自动重签。机器证书 CN = `machines.serial`，5 年；**指纹 = 证书 DER 的 SHA-256 小写十六进制**；`cert_fingerprint = ''` 的机器一律拒绝；吊销靠数据库，不做 CRL。
- 接入令牌：32 字节随机，展示 `spk1_<base64url>`，只存 SHA-256 哈希；有效 24 小时；同机新令牌使旧未用令牌失效；明文不进日志、不进审计 diff（审计只记 `token_id`）。`Enroll` 按来源 IP 每分钟 10 次限流；令牌类失败一律返回「令牌无效」。
- 投递规则（设计 §3.1）逐条照办：每秒一个 tick；认领 SQL 用 `m.agent_conn_id = ANY($1)`；`sent` 补推只补「不在该流已推送集合」里的；收到并处理完第一条 StateReport 前不投递；Agent 单队列顺序执行实例指令，`PullImage` 单独低优先级队列；超时判定只在清扫器（`FailTimedOutCommands`），常量 create 15m / start 5m / stop 5m / wipe 30m / pull 60m，只对 `agent_online` 的机器计时。
- `rental.CreateTimeout` 改为 **20 分钟**；`ResumeMachine` 签名改为 `ResumeMachine(ctx, machineID int64, runningIDs []string) (int, error)`。
- 所有 `RaiseAlertOnce` 文案必须稳定：不带百分比、温度等数值，形如「机器 {serial} 根分区使用率超过 90%」；数值到 `last_metrics` 里看。
- 加锁顺序沿用：`instance → agent_commands → orders → wallet；machine → wallet`。对账动作每个独立事务，有实例行的先 `lockInstance`，只有该实例**没有任何** `pending / sent` 指令（`HasAnyOpenCommand`）才入队 Stop / Wipe。
- 机器侧假设每台机器同一时间只有一个实例（`instances_machine_active_uniq`）：SSH 端口固定 2222、`SPARK-FWD` 不区分容器、`data_disk_used` 只有一个值；**实现里不要出现多容器分支**。
- 机器侧一律 exec 系统命令（`docker`、`iptables`、`tc`、`losetup`、`mkfs.ext4`、`mountpoint`、`findmnt`、`nsenter`、`ip`、`df`、`blkid`、`chattr`、`fstrim`、`nvidia-smi`），不引入 Docker SDK / netlink 库；全部通过 `cmdrun.Runner` 接口执行，单测用 `cmdrun.Recorder` 做命令快照断言；**不使用 build tag**（所有包在 macOS 上可编译可测）。
- 容器规格：`--gpus all`、不用 `--ipc=host`、`--shm-size = 内存一半`、`--memory = 内存 − 8GiB`（`--memory-swap` 同值）、`--pids-limit 32768`、`--oom-score-adj 500`、`--cap-add IPC_LOCK,SYS_NICE,SYS_PTRACE`、`--ulimit memlock=-1:-1 --ulimit stack=67108864:67108864`、专用网桥 `spark0`（`10.200.0.0/24`，`enable_icc=false`，IPv6 关闭）、`-p <lan_ip>:2222:22`、`--dns <AgentConfig.dns_servers>`、`--dns-search .`、`--restart no`、入口 `/.spark/init.sh`。
- 数据盘：每实例一个稀疏镜像 `data/<id>.img`（`AgentConfig.data_size_gib`，默认 2048），`mkfs.ext4 -E lazy_itable_init=1,nodiscard` → `losetup` → 挂到 `mnt/<id>`（`nosuid,nodev`，目录 `chattr +i`）→ bind 到容器 `/root/data`；创建前根分区剩余 ≥ 数据盘大小 + 100GiB；`docker start` 前必须 `mountpoint -q` 且挂载源是该实例的 loop 设备，否则失败「数据盘未挂载」并在 StateReport 报 `missing_data`。
- Agent 失败原因 = 命令 + stderr 末尾 500 字节；平台不可达期间 Agent 不自主停机；重连指数退避 1s 起、上限 60s、带抖动。
- 时间一律 `clock.Clock`；金额单位分；错误用 `apperr`；生产禁止 mock。`SPARK_ENV=prod` 下 `SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS`、`SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE` 必填，缺失则启动失败。
- 端到端规范沿用计划 5（设计 §7.3）：用例标题以覆盖表 ID 开头、`data-testid` 命名 `页面-元素`、禁止 `waitForTimeout`、`workers: 1`；`web/e2e/coverage.json` 与覆盖表一致，`npm run e2e:coverage` 必须通过；现有 Playwright 用例必须保持全绿。
- 每个任务结束前必须实际运行并通过：`go vet ./... && go test ./...`；涉及前端的任务再加 `cd web && npm test && npm run build`；页面任务再加该任务列出的 Playwright 用例。证据是命令输出。
- 提交信息结尾空一行加 `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>`。

## Review Focus

1. **令牌粘贴时带首尾空白或换行**（从后台复制到终端、通过 `curl | sh` 传参）：去掉空白后正常接入；缺 `spk1_` 前缀或内容被改则「令牌无效」→ Task 1 `TestConsumeEnrollTokenTrimsAndRequiresPrefix`。
2. **StateReport 里出现不合法的实例标识**（Agent 状态文件损坏、人为在机器上起了带 `spark.instance` 标签的容器）：不能把任意字符串写进 `agent_commands.instance_id`，而是记一条稳定告警并跳过 → Task 5 `TestReconcileInvalidInstanceIDIsAlertedAndSkipped`。
3. **`nvidia-smi` 输出 `[N/A]` 或指标为 NaN / Inf**（GB10 统一内存下显存就是 `[N/A]`）：Agent 侧解析不报错、心跳照发；网关侧 JSON 编码不能因 NaN 失败而断流 → Task 11 `TestParseNvidiaSMIHandlesNA`、Task 9 `TestHeartbeatSanitizesNonFiniteMetrics`。
4. **Hello 里本机 IP 列表为空**（Agent 读网卡失败）：无法核对登记 IP 时也要告警 `lan_ip_mismatch`，不能静默通过 → Task 9 `TestLanIPMismatchAlert`（空列表分支）。
5. **Agent 回报平台不认识的指令 ID**（机器被重装、平台库被重置、状态文件残留）：忽略并记日志，流保持可用，后续指令照常投递 → Task 9 `TestUnknownCommandResultIsIgnored`。

## 已做出的裁定（规格未写明或与代码核对后确定的细节）

- **Create 指令的扩展字段由网关在投递时填充**：`rental` 入队的 `CreateInstancePayload` 保持 `{image_ref}` 不变（`place.go` / `migrate.go` 不改），网关把 `upstream_pubkey`（平台配置的上游公钥）、`bandwidth_mbps`（`SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS`，默认 200）、`ssh_port = 2222`、`data_size_gib`（`SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB`，默认 2048）填进 proto `CreateInstance`。规格里的「后台按机型可改」数据盘大小本计划用环境变量实现，按机型配置列入遗留。
- **孤儿 wipe 失败告警在 `rental.HandleCommandResult` 内实现**（规格写「网关单独处理」）：`HandleCommandResult` 在 `cmd.Type = wipe && !ok && inst.State != wiping` 时 `RaiseAlertOnce(critical, orphan_wipe_failed)`；网关只负责调用。效果与规格一致，且能在 `rental` 单测里直接覆盖。`HandleCommandResult` 同时放宽：指令的 `instance_id` 在 `instances` 表里不存在时不再报错（孤儿清理可能针对平台从未见过的 ID）。
- **孤儿容器告警级别用 `warning`**：规格写「info 告警」，但 `alerts.level` 的 CHECK 只允许 `warning / critical`，前端也只有这两档；不为此改表。
- **`unknown` 阶段不触发任何对账动作**：Agent 在 `docker inspect` 出错时上报 `unknown`，网关只记日志，避免误判。
- **AgentConfig 的来源**：`lan_ip` 取 `machines.lan_ip`；`heartbeat_interval_s = 10`；`allow_cidrs` / `dns_servers` / `bandwidth_mbps` / `data_size_gib` 来自平台环境变量 `SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS`（默认空）、`SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS`（默认 `223.5.5.5,119.29.29.29`）、`SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS`、`SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB`。
- **安装命令的平台地址**：`SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS` 的第一项 + `SPARK_AGENT_ADDR` 的端口（`agentgw.Config.AdvertiseAddr`，由 `app.Build` 计算）。
- **Agent 配置文件 `/etc/spark-agent/config.yaml` 只用 `key: value` 行**（`platform`、`server_name`），由 Agent 自己按行解析，不引入 YAML 库。
- **机器列表 / 详情的 JSON 增加**：`enrolled`、`fingerprint_prefix`（前 16 位）、`connected`（`agent_conn_id IS NOT NULL`）、`agent_version`、`agent_connected_at`、`state_reported_at`、`last_metrics`（无指标时 `null`）；`cert_fingerprint` 全文不输出。
- **e2e 步进式 fake Agent 的同步方式**：`/hooks/agent` 先等该机器所有 `pending` 指令被真实网关投递到 fake Agent（轮询，最多 10 秒真实时间），再让 fake Agent 执行队列并发 StateReport，然后等网关处理完该次报告（`agentgw.Server.ReportCount(machineID)` 增加）才返回；`/hooks/sweep` 的 `heartbeat=true` 让每个 fake Agent 发一次心跳并等 `HeartbeatCount` 增加。对尚未接入的机器（后台 API 登记的）调用 `/hooks/agent` 会先为它接入并建流（现有用例 A-MCH-01 依赖这一点）。步进模式下 fake Agent 不自动心跳、不自动定时上报，假时钟因此保持确定性。
- **e2e 的指令超时副作用**：fake Agent 不自动执行，所以用例若在下发指令后推进假时钟超过该指令的超时值再清扫，指令会按超时失败（例如 wipe 30 分钟后 `wipe_failed`、机器 faulty）。现有用例基本都在下单 / 释放后立即 `hooks.agent()`；Task 21 跑全量 Playwright 时若遇到这类失败，改用例让它先 `hooks.agent()` 再推进时间，不改生产逻辑。
- **`DisableRateLimits` 同样关闭 `Enroll` 限流**（e2e 一次造 50 台机器都从 127.0.0.1 接入）。
- **`agentgw` 的投递 tick 间隔可注入**（`app.Options.AgentTick`）：生产 1 秒；e2e-server 与集成测试 20～50ms。tick 与 gRPC 超时用真实时间，数据库时间戳（`sent_at`、`agent_connected_at`、`state_reported_at`、心跳）用注入的时钟。
- **hooks 用例里 `/hooks/machines` 的 300 次循环**保留（序列号唯一性），每次真实接入约 30ms，整个测试约 10 秒。

## 任务依赖

Task 1 → 2（fleet 数据层）；3（pki）；4 → 5（rental）；6（proto）；7（配置与上游密钥）；8 → 9 → 10（网关，依赖 1～7）；11 → 12 → 13 → 14 → 15 → 16 → 17（Agent：16 客户端，17 fakeagent 与 spark-agent 二进制；11 只依赖 6）；18 → 19（组装与集成测试矩阵，依赖 10 与 17）；20（后台 API，依赖 18）；21（e2e-server，依赖 20）；22（前端，依赖 21）；23（部署产物与镜像规范，依赖 17）；24（真机验收，依赖 23 与 20）；25（收尾）。按编号顺序执行，共 25 个任务。

---
### Task 1: 迁移 00017、机器新字段、连接归属 / 指标 / StateReport 存取、接入令牌与撤销

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00017_agent.sql`
- Modify: `internal/fleet/machines.go`（`Machine` 结构、`machineCols`、`scanMachine`）
- Create: `internal/fleet/agent.go`、`internal/fleet/enroll.go`
- Test: `internal/fleet/agent_test.go`、`internal/fleet/enroll_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 现有 `fleet.Service`（`pool`、`clock`）、`audit.Actor.Entry` / `audit.Record`、`randx.Token()`（43 字符 base64url）、`db.WithTx`、`apperr`。
- Produces（后续任务按此签名调用）：
  - `fleet.Machine` 新增字段：`CertFingerprint string \`json:"-"\``、`Enrolled bool \`json:"enrolled"\``、`FingerprintPrefix string \`json:"fingerprint_prefix"\``、`Connected bool \`json:"connected"\``、`AgentVersion string \`json:"agent_version"\``、`AgentConnectedAt *time.Time \`json:"agent_connected_at"\``、`StateReportedAt *time.Time \`json:"state_reported_at"\``、`LastMetrics json.RawMessage \`json:"last_metrics"\``。
  - `const PhaseRunning = "running"; PhaseStopped = "stopped"; PhaseMissingData = "missing_data"; PhaseUnknown = "unknown"`；`type InstanceState struct { InstanceID string \`json:"instance_id"\`; Phase string \`json:"phase"\` }`；`type StateReport struct { Instances []InstanceState \`json:"instances"\` }`；`func (r StateReport) RunningIDs() []string`。
  - `type ConnOwner struct { ConnID, Fingerprint string }`
  - `func (s *Service) MachineBySerial(ctx, serial string) (Machine, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) AttachAgentConn(ctx, machineID int64, connID string) error`（设置 `agent_conn_id`、`agent_connected_at = now`；机器不存在 → `apperr.ErrNotFound`）
  - `func (s *Service) DetachAgentConn(ctx, machineID int64, connID string) error`（只清自己的）
  - `func (s *Service) ConnOwners(ctx, ids []int64) (map[int64]ConnOwner, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) RecordMetrics(ctx, machineID int64, version string, metrics json.RawMessage) error`（`metrics == nil` 时保留旧值）
  - `func (s *Service) RecordStateReport(ctx, machineID int64, r StateReport) error`（写 `last_state_report`、`state_reported_at = now`）
  - `func (s *Service) ReportsFor(ctx, ids []int64) (map[int64]StateReport, error)`（只返回 `agent_online AND state_reported_at >= agent_connected_at` 的机器）
  - `const EnrollTokenPrefix = "spk1_"`、`const EnrollTokenTTL = 24 * time.Hour`、`var ErrEnrollToken = apperr.New("invalid_token", "令牌无效", 400)`
  - `type EnrollToken struct { ID int64; Plain string; ExpiresAt time.Time }`、`type EnrollTokenRow struct { ID, MachineID, IssuedBy int64; Serial string }`
  - `func (s *Service) IssueEnrollToken(ctx, actor audit.Actor, machineID int64) (EnrollToken, error)`（审计 `machine.enroll_token`，diff `{token_id, expires_at}`）
  - `func (s *Service) ConsumeEnrollToken(ctx, tx pgx.Tx, plain string) (EnrollTokenRow, error)`
  - `func (s *Service) SetCertFingerprint(ctx, q db.Querier, machineID int64, fp string) error`（同时 `agent_conn_id = NULL`）
  - `func (s *Service) RevokeEnrollment(ctx, actor audit.Actor, machineID int64) error`（未接入 → `apperr.ErrInvalidState`；审计 `machine.revoke_enrollment`，diff `{fingerprint}`）

- [ ] **Step 2: 写失败的测试**

`internal/fleet/agent_test.go`：

```go
package fleet_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
)

func TestAttachDetachConnAndOwners(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, clk := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	a := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	b := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-B")
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, a, "conn-1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, 9999, "conn-x"); !errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("attach unknown = %v", err)
	}
	m, _ := svc.Get(ctx, pool, a)
	if !m.Connected || m.AgentConnectedAt == nil || !m.AgentConnectedAt.Equal(clk.Now()) || m.Enrolled {
		t.Fatalf("after attach = %+v", m)
	}
	owners, err := svc.ConnOwners(ctx, []int64{a, b})
	if err != nil || owners[a] != (fleet.ConnOwner{ConnID: "conn-1"}) || owners[b] != (fleet.ConnOwner{}) {
		t.Fatalf("owners = %+v, %v", owners, err)
	}
	// 只清自己的：别的连接 id 不生效
	if err := svc.DetachAgentConn(ctx, a, "conn-2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if m, _ = svc.Get(ctx, pool, a); !m.Connected {
		t.Fatal("detach with other conn id must not clear")
	}
	if err := svc.DetachAgentConn(ctx, a, "conn-1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if m, _ = svc.Get(ctx, pool, a); m.Connected {
		t.Fatal("detach must clear own conn")
	}
	if got, _ := svc.MachineBySerial(ctx, "SN-B"); got.ID != b {
		t.Fatalf("by serial = %+v", got)
	}
	if _, err := svc.MachineBySerial(ctx, "nope"); !errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown serial = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestRecordMetricsAndStateReport(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, clk := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	id := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	if err := svc.RecordMetrics(ctx, id, "0.1.0", json.RawMessage(`{"gpu_temp_c":55}`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := svc.RecordMetrics(ctx, id, "0.1.1", nil); err != nil { // nil 保留旧指标
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m, _ := svc.Get(ctx, pool, id)
	if m.AgentVersion != "0.1.1" || string(m.LastMetrics) != `{"gpu_temp_c": 55}` {
		t.Fatalf("metrics = %q version = %q", m.LastMetrics, m.AgentVersion)
	}
	if err := svc.RecordMetrics(ctx, id, "", json.RawMessage(`{"gpu_temp_c":60}`)); err != nil { // 空版本保留旧版本
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if m, _ = svc.Get(ctx, pool, id); m.AgentVersion != "0.1.1" || string(m.LastMetrics) != `{"gpu_temp_c": 60}` {
		t.Fatalf("after empty version: metrics = %q version = %q", m.LastMetrics, m.AgentVersion)
	}
	rep := fleet.StateReport{Instances: []fleet.InstanceState{{InstanceID: "ins-aaaaaaaa", Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning}, {InstanceID: "ins-bbbbbbbb", Phase: fleet.PhaseStopped}}}
	if got := rep.RunningIDs(); len(got) != 1 || got[0] != "ins-aaaaaaaa" {
		t.Fatalf("running ids = %v", got)
	}
	// 没有连接时间 → 不可用于兜底恢复
	if err := svc.RecordStateReport(ctx, id, rep); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.ReportsFor(ctx, []int64{id}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("reports without connection = %v", got)
	}
	// 报告早于连接时间 → 不可用；报告晚于或等于连接时间 → 可用
	clk.Advance(time.Second)
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, id, "c1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.ReportsFor(ctx, []int64{id}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("stale report = %v", got)
	}
	if err := svc.RecordStateReport(ctx, id, rep); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got, err := svc.ReportsFor(ctx, []int64{id})
	if err != nil || len(got) != 1 || len(got[id].Instances) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("reports = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	m, _ = svc.Get(ctx, pool, id)
	if m.StateReportedAt == nil || !m.StateReportedAt.Equal(clk.Now()) {
		t.Fatalf("state_reported_at = %v", m.StateReportedAt)
	}
	// 离线机器不返回
	if _, err := svc.MarkOfflineStale(ctx, 0); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.ReportsFor(ctx, []int64{id}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("offline report = %v", got)
	}
}
```

`internal/fleet/enroll_test.go`：

```go
package fleet_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
)

func consume(t *testing.T, svc *fleet.Service, pool interface {
	Begin(context.Context) (pgx.Tx, error)
}, plain string) (fleet.EnrollTokenRow, error) {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := context.Background()
	tx, err := pool.Begin(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer tx.Rollback(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
	row, err := svc.ConsumeEnrollToken(ctx, tx, plain)
	if err != nil {
		return row, err
	}
	return row, tx.Commit(ctx)
}

func TestEnrollTokenLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, clk := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	id := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	actor := audit.Actor{AdminID: 7, IP: "10.0.0.1"}
	tok, err := svc.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, actor, id)
	if err != nil || !strings.HasPrefix(tok.Plain, "spk1_") || len(tok.Plain) != 5+43 || !tok.ExpiresAt.Equal(clk.Now().Add(24*time.Hour)) {
		t.Fatalf("token = %+v, %v", tok, err)
	}
	if _, err := svc.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, actor, 9999); !errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("unknown machine = %v", err)
	}
	// 审计只记 token_id，不含明文
	var diff string
	_ = pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT diff::text FROM audit_logs WHERE action = 'machine.enroll_token'`).Scan(&diff)
	if !strings.Contains(diff, `"token_id"`) || strings.Contains(diff, tok.Plain[5:]) {
		t.Fatalf("audit diff = %s", diff)
	}
	// 新令牌使旧令牌失效
	tok2, _ := svc.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, actor, id)
	if _, err := consume(t, svc, pool, tok.Plain); !errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		t.Fatalf("replaced token = %v", err)
	}
	row, err := consume(t, svc, pool, tok2.Plain)
	if err != nil || row.MachineID != id || row.IssuedBy != 7 || row.Serial != "SN-A" || row.ID != tok2.ID {
		t.Fatalf("consume = %+v, %v", row, err)
	}
	if _, err := consume(t, svc, pool, tok2.Plain); !errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		t.Fatalf("second use = %v", err)
	}
	// 过期
	tok3, _ := svc.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, actor, id)
	clk.Advance(24*time.Hour + time.Second)
	if _, err := consume(t, svc, pool, tok3.Plain); !errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		t.Fatalf("expired = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestConsumeEnrollTokenTrimsAndRequiresPrefix(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, _ := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	id := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	tok, _ := svc.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, audit.Actor{}, id)
	if _, err := consume(t, svc, pool, strings.TrimPrefix(tok.Plain, "spk1_")); !errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		t.Fatalf("no prefix = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := consume(t, svc, pool, "spk1_"+strings.Repeat("x", 43)); !errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		t.Fatalf("garbage = %v", err)
	}
	if row, err := consume(t, svc, pool, "  "+tok.Plain+"\n"); err != nil || row.MachineID != id {
		t.Fatalf("trimmed = %+v, %v", row, err)
	}
}

func TestSetFingerprintAndRevoke(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, _ := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	id := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	if err := svc.RevokeEnrollment(ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, id); !errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrInvalidState) {
		t.Fatalf("revoke unenrolled = %v", err)
	}
	fp := strings.Repeat("ab", 32)
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, id, "c1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := db.WithTx(ctx, pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error { return svc.SetCertFingerprint(ctx, tx, id, fp) }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m, _ := svc.Get(ctx, pool, id)
	if !m.Enrolled || m.FingerprintPrefix != fp[:16] || m.CertFingerprint != fp || m.Connected {
		t.Fatalf("after set = %+v", m)
	}
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, id, "c2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := svc.RevokeEnrollment(ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, id); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m, _ = svc.Get(ctx, pool, id)
	if m.Enrolled || m.Connected || m.FingerprintPrefix != "" {
		t.Fatalf("after revoke = %+v", m)
	}
	var n int
	_ = pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM audit_logs WHERE action = 'machine.revoke_enrollment' AND diff->>'fingerprint' = $1`, fp).Scan(&n)
	if n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("revoke audit = %d", n)
	}
}
```

`Machine` 结构改为：

```go
type Machine struct {
	ID          int64      `json:"id"`
	Serial      string     `json:"serial"`
	Rack        string     `json:"rack"`
	Slot        string     `json:"slot"`
	LanIP       string     `json:"lan_ip"`
	ProductID   int64      `json:"product_id"`
	State       string     `json:"state"`
	AgentOnline bool       `json:"agent_online"`
	LastSeenAt  *time.Time `json:"last_seen_at"`
	PDUID       *int64     `json:"pdu_id"`
	PDUOutlet   *int       `json:"pdu_outlet"`
	CreatedAt   time.Time  `json:"created_at"`

	// 计划 2：Agent 接入与连接状态。指纹全文不输出，只给前 16 位。
	CertFingerprint   string          `json:"-"`
	Enrolled          bool            `json:"enrolled"`
	FingerprintPrefix string          `json:"fingerprint_prefix"`
	Connected         bool            `json:"connected"`
	AgentVersion      string          `json:"agent_version"`
	AgentConnectedAt  *time.Time      `json:"agent_connected_at"`
	StateReportedAt   *time.Time      `json:"state_reported_at"`
	LastMetrics       json.RawMessage `json:"last_metrics"`
}
```

`machineCols` 与 `scanMachine`：

```go
const machineCols = `id, serial, rack, slot, lan_ip, product_id, state, agent_online, last_seen_at, pdu_id, pdu_outlet, created_at,
	cert_fingerprint, agent_conn_id, agent_version, agent_connected_at, state_reported_at, last_metrics`

func scanMachine(row pgx.Row) (Machine, error) {
	var m Machine
	var connID *string
	var metrics []byte
	err := row.Scan(&m.ID, &m.Serial, &m.Rack, &m.Slot, &m.LanIP, &m.ProductID, &m.State, &m.AgentOnline, &m.LastSeenAt,
		&m.PDUID, &m.PDUOutlet, &m.CreatedAt, &m.CertFingerprint, &connID, &m.AgentVersion, &m.AgentConnectedAt, &m.StateReportedAt, &metrics)
	if db.IsNoRows(err) {
		return Machine{}, apperr.ErrNotFound
	}
	m.Enrolled = m.CertFingerprint != ""
	if len(m.CertFingerprint) >= 16 {
		m.FingerprintPrefix = m.CertFingerprint[:16]
	}
	m.Connected = connID != nil
	if metrics != nil {
		m.LastMetrics = json.RawMessage(metrics)
	}
	return m, err
}
```

加 `"encoding/json"` 到 import。`machineCols` 里的列名没有表前缀，现有 SQL 都是单表查询，不受影响。

- [ ] **Step 5: 新建 `internal/fleet/agent.go`**

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
)

// Agent 上报的容器阶段。
const (
	PhaseRunning     = "running"
	PhaseStopped     = "stopped"
	PhaseMissingData = "missing_data" // 容器在、数据盘未挂载
	PhaseUnknown     = "unknown"      // Agent 无法确认（docker inspect 出错）
)

type InstanceState struct {
	InstanceID string `json:"instance_id"`
	Phase      string `json:"phase"`
}

// StateReport 是 Agent 上报的本机实例集合，原文存入 machines.last_state_report。
type StateReport struct {
	Instances []InstanceState `json:"instances"`
}

// RunningIDs 返回报告中阶段为 running 的实例 ID。
func (r StateReport) RunningIDs() []string {
	var ids []string
	for _, s := range r.Instances {
		if s.Phase == PhaseRunning {
			ids = append(ids, s.InstanceID)
		}
	}
	return ids
}

// ConnOwner 是机器当前的连接归属：持有流的网关连接 id（无连接为空串）与证书指纹。
type ConnOwner struct {
	ConnID      string
	Fingerprint string
}

func (s *Service) MachineBySerial(ctx context.Context, serial string) (Machine, error) {
	return scanMachine(s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT `+machineCols+` FROM machines WHERE serial = $1`, serial))
}

// AttachAgentConn 把机器的流归属到 connID（新连接顶掉旧归属）。
func (s *Service) AttachAgentConn(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, connID string) error {
	tag, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_conn_id = $2, agent_connected_at = $3, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1`,
		machineID, connID, s.clock.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
		return apperr.ErrNotFound
	}
	return nil
}

// DetachAgentConn 清除归属，但只清自己的：别的副本已接管时不动。
func (s *Service) DetachAgentConn(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, connID string) error {
	_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_conn_id = NULL, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1 AND agent_conn_id = $2`,
		machineID, connID, s.clock.Now())
	return err
}

func (s *Service) ConnOwners(ctx context.Context, ids []int64) (map[int64]ConnOwner, error) {
	out := map[int64]ConnOwner{}
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		return out, nil
	}
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT id, COALESCE(agent_conn_id, ''), cert_fingerprint FROM machines WHERE id = ANY($1)`, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	for rows.Next() {
		var id int64
		var o ConnOwner
		if err := rows.Scan(&id, &o.ConnID, &o.Fingerprint); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[id] = o
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}

// RecordMetrics 保存 Agent 版本与最近一份指标；metrics 为 nil 时只更新版本，version 为空时只更新指标。
func (s *Service) RecordMetrics(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, version string, metrics json.RawMessage) error {
	var m []byte
	if metrics != nil {
		m = metrics
	}
	_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines
		SET agent_version = CASE WHEN $2 = '' THEN agent_version ELSE $2 END,
		    last_metrics = COALESCE($3::jsonb, last_metrics), updated_at = $4
		WHERE id = $1`, machineID, version, m, s.clock.Now())
	return err
}

func (s *Service) RecordStateReport(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, r StateReport) error {
	if r.Instances == nil {
		r.Instances = []InstanceState{}
	}
	b, err := json.Marshal(r)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	_, err = s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines SET last_state_report = $2, state_reported_at = $3, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1`,
		machineID, b, s.clock.Now())
	return err
}

// ReportsFor 返回 ids 中在线、且本次连接之后上报过的机器的最近一份 StateReport（清扫器兜底恢复计时用）。
func (s *Service) ReportsFor(ctx context.Context, ids []int64) (map[int64]StateReport, error) {
	out := map[int64]StateReport{}
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		return out, nil
	}
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT id, last_state_report FROM machines
		WHERE id = ANY($1) AND agent_online AND last_state_report IS NOT NULL
		  AND agent_connected_at IS NOT NULL AND state_reported_at >= agent_connected_at`, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	for rows.Next() {
		var id int64
		var raw []byte
		if err := rows.Scan(&id, &raw); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		var r StateReport
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &r); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out[id] = r
	}
	return out, rows.Err()
}
```

（本文件不需要导入 `pgx`，上面 import 块里去掉 `"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"`。）

- [ ] **Step 6: 新建 `internal/fleet/enroll.go`**

```go
package fleet

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/randx"
)

const (
	EnrollTokenPrefix = "spk1_"
	EnrollTokenTTL    = 24 * time.Hour
)

// ErrEnrollToken 覆盖令牌不存在、已用、已过期、被新令牌取代、格式不对的全部情况（统一文案，不泄露原因）。
var ErrEnrollToken = apperr.New("invalid_token", "令牌无效", 400)

type EnrollToken struct {
	ID        int64
	Plain     string // 只在签发时返回一次
	ExpiresAt time.Time
}

type EnrollTokenRow struct {
	ID        int64
	MachineID int64
	IssuedBy  int64
	Serial    string
}

func hashEnrollToken(plain string) []byte {
	h := sha256.Sum256([]byte(plain))
	return h[:]
}

// IssueEnrollToken 为机器签发一次性接入令牌；同机旧的未用令牌立即失效。审计只记录令牌记录 id。
func (s *Service) IssueEnrollToken(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor, machineID int64) (EnrollToken, error) {
	now := s.clock.Now()
	plain := EnrollTokenPrefix + randx.Token()
	var out EnrollToken
	err := db.WithTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := s.Get(ctx, tx, machineID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machine_enroll_tokens SET expires_at = $2
			WHERE machine_id = $1 AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > $2`, machineID, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		exp := now.Add(EnrollTokenTTL)
		var id int64
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `INSERT INTO machine_enroll_tokens (machine_id, token_hash, expires_at, issued_by, created_at)
			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5) RETURNING id`, machineID, hashEnrollToken(plain), exp, actor.AdminID, now).Scan(&id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		out = EnrollToken{ID: id, Plain: plain, ExpiresAt: exp}
		return audit.Record(ctx, tx, now, actor.Entry("machine.enroll_token", "machine", strconv.FormatInt(machineID, 10),
			map[string]any{"token_id": id, "expires_at": exp}))
	})
	return out, err
}

// ConsumeEnrollToken 在调用方事务内校验并标记令牌已用。首尾空白被忽略。
func (s *Service) ConsumeEnrollToken(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, plain string) (EnrollTokenRow, error) {
	plain = strings.TrimSpace(plain)
	if !strings.HasPrefix(plain, EnrollTokenPrefix) {
		return EnrollTokenRow{}, ErrEnrollToken
	}
	now := s.clock.Now()
	var r EnrollTokenRow
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT t.id, t.machine_id, t.issued_by, m.serial
		FROM machine_enroll_tokens t JOIN machines m ON m.id = t.machine_id
		WHERE t.token_hash = $1 AND t.used_at IS NULL AND t.expires_at > $2 FOR UPDATE OF t`,
		hashEnrollToken(plain), now).Scan(&r.ID, &r.MachineID, &r.IssuedBy, &r.Serial)
	if db.IsNoRows(err) {
		return EnrollTokenRow{}, ErrEnrollToken
	}
	if err != nil {
		return EnrollTokenRow{}, err
	}
	_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machine_enroll_tokens SET used_at = $2 WHERE id = $1`, r.ID, now)
	return r, err
}

// SetCertFingerprint 写入新证书指纹并清空连接归属（旧 Agent 的流在下一个 tick 被断开）。
func (s *Service) SetCertFingerprint(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, machineID int64, fp string) error {
	_, err := q.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines SET cert_fingerprint = $2, agent_conn_id = NULL, updated_at = $3 WHERE id = $1`,
		machineID, fp, s.clock.Now())
	return err
}

// RevokeEnrollment 撤销接入：清空指纹与连接归属，写审计。未接入的机器返回 invalid_state。
func (s *Service) RevokeEnrollment(ctx context.Context, actor audit.Actor, machineID int64) error {
	now := s.clock.Now()
	return db.WithTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var fp string
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT cert_fingerprint FROM machines WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE`, machineID).Scan(&fp)
		if db.IsNoRows(err) {
			return apperr.ErrNotFound
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if fp == "" {
			return apperr.ErrInvalidState
		}
		if err := s.SetCertFingerprint(ctx, tx, machineID, ""); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return audit.Record(ctx, tx, now, actor.Entry("machine.revoke_enrollment", "machine", strconv.FormatInt(machineID, 10),
			map[string]any{"fingerprint": fp}))
	})
}
```

```go
func TestClaimPendingOnlyForOwnedConns(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, clk := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	a := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	b := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-B")
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, a, "conn-a"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, b, "conn-b"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	first, _ := svc.Enqueue(ctx, pool, a, "ins-aaaaaaaa", fleet.CmdCreateInstance, fleet.CreateInstancePayload{ImageRef: "img:1"})
	clk.Advance(time.Second)
	second, _ := svc.Enqueue(ctx, pool, a, "ins-aaaaaaaa", fleet.CmdStopInstance, nil)
	_, _ = svc.Enqueue(ctx, pool, b, "ins-bbbbbbbb", fleet.CmdCreateInstance, fleet.CreateInstancePayload{ImageRef: "img:1"})

	if got, err := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, nil); err != nil || got != nil {
		t.Fatalf("claim nil = %v, %v", got, err)
	}
	got, err := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, []string{"conn-a", "conn-zzz"})
	if err != nil || len(got) != 2 || got[0].ID != first || got[1].ID != second || got[0].ConnID != "conn-a" {
		t.Fatalf("claim = %+v, %v", got, err)
	}
	if got[0].Status != "sent" || got[0].Attempts != 1 || got[0].SentAt == nil || !got[0].SentAt.Equal(clk.Now()) {
		t.Fatalf("claimed row = %+v", got[0])
	}
	if again, _ := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, []string{"conn-a"}); len(again) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("second claim = %+v", again)
	}
	// 机器 b 的指令仍 pending；用 b 的旧连接 id 认领不到
	if pend, _ := svc.PendingCommands(ctx, b); len(pend) != 1 || pend[0].Status != "pending" {
		t.Fatalf("b pending = %+v", pend)
	}
	_ = svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, b, "conn-b2")
	if got, _ := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, []string{"conn-b"}); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("stale conn claimed %+v", got)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, []string{"conn-b2"}); len(got) != 1 || got[0].ConnID != "conn-b2" {
		t.Fatalf("new conn claim = %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestSentCommandsTouchAndSentBefore(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, clk := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	a := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	_ = svc.AttachAgentConn(ctx, a, "c")
	id, _ := svc.Enqueue(ctx, pool, a, "ins-aaaaaaaa", fleet.CmdWipeInstance, nil)
	if _, err := svc.ClaimPending(ctx, []string{"c"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t0 := clk.Now()
	sent, err := svc.SentCommands(ctx, a)
	if err != nil || len(sent) != 1 || sent[0].ID != id {
		t.Fatalf("sent = %+v, %v", sent, err)
	}
	clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	if err := svc.TouchSent(ctx, []string{id, "nope"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	sent, _ = svc.SentCommands(ctx, a)
	if !sent[0].SentAt.Equal(t0.Add(time.Minute)) || sent[0].Attempts != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("after touch = %+v", sent[0])
	}
	if got, _ := svc.SentBefore(ctx, t0); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("sent before touch = %+v", got)
	}
	got, _ := svc.SentBefore(ctx, clk.Now())
	if len(got) != 1 || got[0].ID != id || got[0].Type != fleet.CmdWipeInstance {
		t.Fatalf("sent before now = %+v", got)
	}
	// 离线机器的 sent 指令不计时
	if _, err := svc.MarkOfflineStale(ctx, 0); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.SentBefore(ctx, clk.Now()); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("offline sent = %+v", got)
	}
	// 已完成的不再是 sent
	_, _ = svc.Heartbeat(ctx, a)
	if _, _, err := svc.CompleteCommand(ctx, pool, id, true, "ok"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got, _ := svc.SentCommands(ctx, a); len(got) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("done still sent: %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestHasAnyOpenCommandAndPullBroadcast(t *testing.T) {
	svc, pool, _ := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	a := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-A")
	b := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-B")
	c := dbtest.CreateMachine(t, pool, "SN-C")
	_, _ = pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_online = false WHERE id = $1`, c)
	if open, _ := svc.HasAnyOpenCommand(ctx, pool, "ins-aaaaaaaa"); open {
		t.Fatal("no commands yet")
	}
	id, _ := svc.Enqueue(ctx, pool, a, "ins-aaaaaaaa", fleet.CmdStartInstance, nil)
	if open, _ := svc.HasAnyOpenCommand(ctx, pool, "ins-aaaaaaaa"); !open {
		t.Fatal("pending start must count")
	}
	_, _, _ = svc.CompleteCommand(ctx, pool, id, false, "x")
	if open, _ := svc.HasAnyOpenCommand(ctx, pool, "ins-aaaaaaaa"); open {
		t.Fatal("failed command is not open")
	}
	n, err := svc.BroadcastPullImage(ctx, "nvcr.io/x/y:1")
	if err != nil || n != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("broadcast = %d, %v", n, err)
	}
	for _, mid := range []int64{a, b} {
		cmds, _ := svc.PendingCommands(ctx, mid)
		var p fleet.PullImagePayload
		if len(cmds) != 1 || cmds[0].Type != fleet.CmdPullImage || cmds[0].InstanceID != "" ||
			json.Unmarshal(cmds[0].Payload, &p) != nil || p.ImageRef != "nvcr.io/x/y:1" {
			t.Fatalf("machine %d pull = %+v", mid, cmds)
		}
	}
	if cmds, _ := svc.PendingCommands(ctx, c); len(cmds) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("offline machine got pull: %+v", cmds)
	}
	if ids, _ := svc.OnlineMachineIDs(ctx); len(ids) != 2 || ids[0] != a || ids[1] != b {
		t.Fatalf("online ids = %v", ids)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 修改 `internal/fleet/commands.go`**

常量与结构：

```go
const (
	CmdCreateInstance CommandType = "create_instance"
	CmdStartInstance  CommandType = "start_instance"
	CmdStopInstance   CommandType = "stop_instance"
	CmdWipeInstance   CommandType = "wipe_instance"
	CmdPullImage      CommandType = "pull_image" // 镜像预拉取，instance_id 为空
)

type PullImagePayload struct {
	ImageRef string `json:"image_ref"`
}

type Command struct {
	ID         string
	MachineID  int64
	InstanceID string
	Type       CommandType
	Payload    json.RawMessage
	Status     string
	Result     string
	Attempts   int
	CreatedAt  time.Time
	SentAt     *time.Time
}

const commandCols = `id, machine_id, instance_id, type, payload, status, result, attempts, created_at, sent_at`
```

`scanCommand` 追加 `&c.SentAt`。新方法：

```go
// ClaimedCommand 是刚被本副本认领的指令，ConnID 为认领时机器的归属连接。
type ClaimedCommand struct {
	Command
	ConnID string
}

// ClaimPending 原子认领本副本持有的流（connIDs）所属机器的 pending 指令：状态改为 sent、attempts+1、写 sent_at。
// 返回行里的 ConnID 若已不是调用方持有的流（SQL 期间被顶掉），调用方不推送，指令保持 sent 由补推处理。
func (s *Service) ClaimPending(ctx context.Context, connIDs []string) ([]ClaimedCommand, error) {
	if len(connIDs) == 0 {
		return nil, nil
	}
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `UPDATE agent_commands c
		SET status = 'sent', attempts = c.attempts + 1, sent_at = $2, updated_at = $2
		FROM machines m
		WHERE m.id = c.machine_id AND m.agent_conn_id = ANY($1) AND c.status = 'pending'
		RETURNING c.id, c.machine_id, c.instance_id, c.type, c.payload, c.status, c.result, c.attempts, c.created_at, c.sent_at, m.agent_conn_id`,
		connIDs, s.clock.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(r pgx.CollectableRow) (ClaimedCommand, error) {
		var c ClaimedCommand
		var payload []byte
		err := r.Scan(&c.ID, &c.MachineID, &c.InstanceID, &c.Type, &payload, &c.Status, &c.Result, &c.Attempts, &c.CreatedAt, &c.SentAt, &c.ConnID)
		c.Payload = payload
		return c, err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	sort.SliceStable(out, func(i, j int) bool {
		if !out[i].CreatedAt.Equal(out[j].CreatedAt) {
			return out[i].CreatedAt.Before(out[j].CreatedAt)
		}
		return out[i].ID < out[j].ID
	})
	return out, nil
}

// SentCommands 返回机器上已下发、尚未回报的指令（补推用）。
func (s *Service) SentCommands(ctx context.Context, machineID int64) ([]Command, error) {
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT `+commandCols+` FROM agent_commands
		WHERE machine_id = $1 AND status = 'sent' ORDER BY created_at, id`, machineID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(r pgx.CollectableRow) (Command, error) { return scanCommand(r) })
}

// TouchSent 补推后刷新 sent_at，超时从头计时。
func (s *Service) TouchSent(ctx context.Context, ids []string) error {
	if len(ids) == 0 {
		return nil
	}
	_, err := s.pool.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE agent_commands SET sent_at = $2, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = ANY($1) AND status = 'sent'`, ids, s.clock.Now())
	return err
}

// SentBefore 返回在线机器上 sent_at 不晚于 before 的已下发指令（超时判定的候选，按类型的超时由调用方再筛）。
func (s *Service) SentBefore(ctx context.Context, before time.Time) ([]Command, error) {
	rows, err := s.pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT c.id, c.machine_id, c.instance_id, c.type, c.payload, c.status, c.result, c.attempts, c.created_at, c.sent_at
		FROM agent_commands c JOIN machines m ON m.id = c.machine_id
		WHERE c.status = 'sent' AND m.agent_online AND c.sent_at IS NOT NULL AND c.sent_at <= $1
		ORDER BY c.sent_at, c.id`, before)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(r pgx.CollectableRow) (Command, error) { return scanCommand(r) })
}

// HasAnyOpenCommand 报告某实例是否还有任何未完成（pending/sent）的指令，不限类型。
func (s *Service) HasAnyOpenCommand(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, instanceID string) (bool, error) {
	var ok bool
	err := q.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agent_commands WHERE instance_id = $1 AND status IN ('pending', 'sent'))`, instanceID).Scan(&ok)
	return ok, err
}

// OnlineMachineIDs 返回当前 Agent 在线的全部机器 id。
func (s *Service) OnlineMachineIDs(ctx context.Context) ([]int64, error) {
	return s.queryIDs(ctx, `SELECT id FROM machines WHERE agent_online ORDER BY id`)
}

// BroadcastPullImage 向所有在线机器下发镜像预拉取；返回下发台数。
func (s *Service) BroadcastPullImage(ctx context.Context, ref string) (int, error) {
	ids, err := s.OnlineMachineIDs(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	err = db.WithTx(ctx, s.pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		for _, id := range ids {
			if _, err := s.Enqueue(ctx, tx, id, "", CmdPullImage, PullImagePayload{ImageRef: ref}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	return len(ids), err
}
```

import 追加 `"sort"`。

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 的 `platform_pki` 表；`secretbox.Box`（`Seal` / `Open`）；`clock.Clock`；`dbtest`。
- Produces:
  - `type CA struct{ ... }`；`func EnsureCA(ctx, pool *pgxpool.Pool, box *secretbox.Box, clk clock.Clock) (*CA, error)`
  - `func (c *CA) CertDER() []byte`、`CertPEM() []byte`、`Fingerprint() string`、`Pool() *x509.CertPool`

  - `func ParseCSR(der []byte) (*ecdsa.PublicKey, error)`（校验签名；只接受 P-256）

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`internal/pki/pki_test.go`：

```go
package pki_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/rsa"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"encoding/hex"
	"net"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/secretbox"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { dbtest.Main(m) }

func newCA(t *testing.T) (*pki.CA, *clock.Fake) {
	t.Helper()
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	box, _ := secretbox.New(dbtest.Key)
	clk := clock.NewFake(dbtest.T0)
	ca, err := pki.EnsureCA(context.Background(), pool, box, clk)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return ca, clk
}

func csrFor(t *testing.T, key any, cn string) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{
		Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: cn, Organization: []string{"evil"}}, DNSNames: []string{"evil.example"},
	}, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return der
}

func TestEnsureCAIsSingletonAndPersistent(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	box, _ := secretbox.New(dbtest.Key)
	clk := clock.NewFake(dbtest.T0)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	fps := make([]string, 8)
	for i := range fps {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(i int) {
			defer wg.Done()
			ca, err := pki.EnsureCA(ctx, pool, box, clk)
			if err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
				return
			}
			fps[i] = ca.Fingerprint()
		}(i)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	for _, fp := range fps[1:] {
		if fp != fps[0] {
			t.Fatalf("fingerprints differ: %v", fps)
		}
	}
	var n int
	_ = pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM platform_pki`).Scan(&n)
	if n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pki rows = %d", n)
	}
	ca, _ := pki.EnsureCA(ctx, pool, box, clk)
	cert, _ := x509.ParseCertificate(ca.CertDER())
	if !cert.IsCA || cert.PublicKeyAlgorithm != x509.ECDSA || cert.NotAfter.Sub(cert.NotBefore) < 19*365*24*time.Hour {
		t.Fatalf("ca cert = %+v", cert)
	}
	if len(ca.Fingerprint()) != 64 || ca.Fingerprint() != pki.Fingerprint(ca.CertDER()) {
		t.Fatalf("fingerprint = %s", ca.Fingerprint())
	}
	// 换一把 SPARK_SECRET_KEY 后 CA 私钥不可解密
	other, _ := secretbox.New([]byte("another-32-byte-key-for-testing!"))
	if _, err := pki.EnsureCA(ctx, pool, other, clk); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("wrong secret key must fail")
	}
}

func TestIssueMachineUsesSerialAndIgnoresCSR(t *testing.T) {
	ca, clk := newCA(t)
	key, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	pub, err := pki.ParseCSR(csrFor(t, key, "evil"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	der, err := ca.IssueMachine(pub, "SN-001", clk.Now())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, _ := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if cert.Subject.CommonName != "SN-001" || len(cert.DNSNames) != 0 || len(cert.Subject.Organization) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("subject not overridden: %+v", cert.Subject)
	}
	if cert.NotAfter.Sub(clk.Now()) < 5*365*24*time.Hour-time.Hour {
		t.Fatalf("validity = %v", cert.NotAfter)
	}
	if _, err := cert.Verify(x509.VerifyOptions{Roots: ca.Pool(), KeyUsages: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth}, CurrentTime: clk.Now()}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("verify: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := hex.DecodeString(pki.Fingerprint(der)); err != nil || len(pki.Fingerprint(der)) != 64 {
		t.Fatalf("fingerprint = %s", pki.Fingerprint(der))
	}
}

func TestParseCSRRejectsBadSignatureAndNonP256(t *testing.T) {
	key, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	der := csrFor(t, key, "x")
	der[len(der)-1] ^= 0xff
	if _, err := pki.ParseCSR(der); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("tampered csr accepted")
	}
	rsaKey, _ := rsa.GenerateKey(rand.Reader, 2048)
	if _, err := pki.ParseCSR(csrFor(t, rsaKey, "x")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("rsa csr accepted")
	}
	p384, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P384(), rand.Reader)
	if _, err := pki.ParseCSR(csrFor(t, p384, "x")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("p384 csr accepted")
	}
}

func TestServerCertSANsAndRotation(t *testing.T) {
	ca, clk := newCA(t)
	sc, err := pki.NewServerCerts(ca, []string{"agent.example", "192.168.1.5"}, clk)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cert, err := sc.GetCertificate(&tls.ClientHelloInfo{})
	if err != nil || cert.Leaf == nil || len(cert.Certificate) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("cert = %+v, %v", cert, err)
	}
	if cert.Leaf.DNSNames[0] != "agent.example" || !cert.Leaf.IPAddresses[0].Equal(net.ParseIP("192.168.1.5")) {
		t.Fatalf("sans = %v %v", cert.Leaf.DNSNames, cert.Leaf.IPAddresses)
	}
	if err := cert.Leaf.VerifyHostname("192.168.1.5"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if rotated, _ := sc.Rotate(); rotated {
		t.Fatal("fresh cert must not rotate")
	}
	clk.Advance(340 * 24 * time.Hour)
	rotated, err := sc.Rotate()
	if err != nil || !rotated {
		t.Fatalf("rotate = %v, %v", rotated, err)
	}
	fresh, _ := sc.GetCertificate(nil)
	if !fresh.Leaf.NotAfter.After(cert.Leaf.NotAfter) || fresh.Leaf.NotAfter.Sub(clk.Now()) < 364*24*time.Hour {
		t.Fatalf("not reissued: %v vs %v", fresh.Leaf.NotAfter, cert.Leaf.NotAfter)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `internal/pki/ca.go`**

```go
// Package pki 提供平台内置 CA：根证书存库（私钥 secretbox 加密）、签发网关服务端证书与机器客户端证书。
package pki

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math/big"
	"net"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/secretbox"
)

const (
	caValidity      = 20 * 365 * 24 * time.Hour
	serverValidity  = 365 * 24 * time.Hour
	machineValidity = 5 * 365 * 24 * time.Hour
)

type CA struct {
	cert    *x509.Certificate
	certDER []byte
	key     *ecdsa.PrivateKey
	pool    *x509.CertPool
}

// EnsureCA 读取或首次生成根 CA；多副本并发首次启动时靠 ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING 保证只有一套。
func EnsureCA(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, box *secretbox.Box, clk clock.Clock) (*CA, error) {
	ca, err := loadCA(ctx, pool, box)
	if err == nil {
		return ca, nil
	}
	if !db.IsNoRows(err) {
		return nil, err
	}
	now := clk.Now()
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	serial, err := randomSerial()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	tpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber:          serial,
		Subject:               pkix.Name{CommonName: "Spark Cloud Agent CA"},
		NotBefore:             now.Add(-5 * time.Minute),
		NotAfter:              now.Add(caValidity),
		IsCA:                  true,
		BasicConstraintsValid: true,
		KeyUsage:              x509.KeyUsageCertSign | x509.KeyUsageCRLSign | x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tpl, tpl, &key.PublicKey, key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	keyDER, err := x509.MarshalPKCS8PrivateKey(key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if _, err := pool.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO platform_pki (id, ca_cert, ca_key_enc, created_at) VALUES (1, $1, $2, $3) ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`,
		der, box.Seal(keyDER), now); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return loadCA(ctx, pool, box)
}

func loadCA(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, box *secretbox.Box) (*CA, error) {
	var certDER, keyEnc []byte
	if err := pool.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT ca_cert, ca_key_enc FROM platform_pki WHERE id = 1`).Scan(&certDER, &keyEnc); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	keyDER, err := box.Open(keyEnc)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("解密 CA 私钥失败（SPARK_SECRET_KEY 是否被更换？）: %w", err)
	}
	k, err := x509.ParsePKCS8PrivateKey(keyDER)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	key, ok := k.(*ecdsa.PrivateKey)
	if !ok {
		return nil, errors.New("pki: CA key is not ECDSA")
	}
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(certDER)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	p := x509.NewCertPool()
	p.AddCert(cert)
	return &CA{cert: cert, certDER: certDER, key: key, pool: p}, nil
}

func randomSerial() (*big.Int, error) {
	return rand.Int(rand.Reader, new(big.Int).Lsh(big.NewInt(1), 128))
}

func (c *CA) CertDER() []byte { return c.certDER }
func (c *CA) CertPEM() []byte { return pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: c.certDER}) }
func (c *CA) Fingerprint() string { return Fingerprint(c.certDER) }
func (c *CA) Pool() *x509.CertPool { return c.pool }

// Fingerprint 返回证书 DER 的 SHA-256 小写十六进制。
func Fingerprint(der []byte) string {
	h := sha256.Sum256(der)
	return hex.EncodeToString(h[:])
}

// IssueServer 为网关签发服务端证书，SAN 按 hosts 自动区分 IP 与域名，有效期 1 年。
func (c *CA) IssueServer(hosts []string, now time.Time) (tls.Certificate, error) {
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return tls.Certificate{}, err
	}
	serial, err := randomSerial()
	if err != nil {
		return tls.Certificate{}, err
	}
	tpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: serial,
		Subject:      pkix.Name{CommonName: "Spark Cloud Agent Gateway"},
		NotBefore:    now.Add(-5 * time.Minute),
		NotAfter:     now.Add(serverValidity),
		KeyUsage:     x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
		ExtKeyUsage:  []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth},
	}
	for _, h := range hosts {
		if ip := net.ParseIP(h); ip != nil {
			tpl.IPAddresses = append(tpl.IPAddresses, ip)
		} else {
			tpl.DNSNames = append(tpl.DNSNames, h)
		}
	}
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tpl, c.cert, &key.PublicKey, c.key)
	if err != nil {
		return tls.Certificate{}, err
	}
	leaf, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
	if err != nil {
		return tls.Certificate{}, err
	}
	return tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{der, c.certDER}, PrivateKey: key, Leaf: leaf}, nil
}

// IssueMachine 用 CSR 里的公钥签发机器证书：Subject 只有 CN = 序列号，忽略 CSR 的其他内容，有效期 5 年。
func (c *CA) IssueMachine(pub *ecdsa.PublicKey, serial string, now time.Time) ([]byte, error) {
	sn, err := randomSerial()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	tpl := &x509.Certificate{
		SerialNumber: sn,
		Subject:      pkix.Name{CommonName: serial},
		NotBefore:    now.Add(-5 * time.Minute),
		NotAfter:     now.Add(machineValidity),
		KeyUsage:     x509.KeyUsageDigitalSignature,
		ExtKeyUsage:  []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageClientAuth},
	}
	return x509.CreateCertificate(rand.Reader, tpl, c.cert, pub, c.key)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `internal/pki/csr.go` 与 `internal/pki/server.go`**

`csr.go`：

```go
package pki

import (
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/x509"
	"errors"
)

// ParseCSR 校验 CSR 签名并只取出公钥；只接受 ECDSA P-256。
func ParseCSR(der []byte) (*ecdsa.PublicKey, error) {
	req, err := x509.ParseCertificateRequest(der)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := req.CheckSignature(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	pub, ok := req.PublicKey.(*ecdsa.PublicKey)
	if !ok || pub.Curve != elliptic.P256() {
		return nil, errors.New("pki: CSR public key must be ECDSA P-256")
	}
	return pub, nil
}
```

`server.go`：

```go
package pki

import (
	"crypto/tls"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
)

// RenewBefore 是服务端证书自动重签的提前量。
const RenewBefore = 30 * 24 * time.Hour

// ServerCerts 持有网关当前的服务端证书并支持热替换；不落盘。
type ServerCerts struct {
	ca    *CA
	hosts []string
	clk   clock.Clock
	mu    sync.RWMutex
	cert  *tls.Certificate
}

func NewServerCerts(ca *CA, hosts []string, clk clock.Clock) (*ServerCerts, error) {
	s := &ServerCerts{ca: ca, hosts: hosts, clk: clk}
	cert, err := ca.IssueServer(hosts, clk.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	s.cert = &cert
	return s, nil
}

// GetCertificate 供 tls.Config.GetCertificate 使用。
func (s *ServerCerts) GetCertificate(*tls.ClientHelloInfo) (*tls.Certificate, error) {
	return s.Current(), nil
}

func (s *ServerCerts) Current() *tls.Certificate {
	s.mu.RLock()
	defer s.mu.RUnlock()
	return s.cert
}

// Rotate 在剩余有效期不足 RenewBefore 时重签；返回是否重签。
func (s *ServerCerts) Rotate() (bool, error) {
	now := s.clk.Now()
	if s.Current().Leaf.NotAfter.Sub(now) >= RenewBefore {
		return false, nil
	}
	cert, err := s.ca.IssueServer(s.hosts, now)
	if err != nil {
		return false, err
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.cert = &cert
	s.mu.Unlock()
	return true, nil
}
```

`TestSweepResumesOnlineSuspended` 改为（清扫器兜底需要机器有本次连接之后的 StateReport）：

```go
func TestSweepResumesOnlineSuspended(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour)
	frozen := 23 * time.Hour
	n, err := e.svc.SuspendMachines(e.ctx, e.machines)
	mustCount(t, n, err, 1, "suspended")
	e.clock.Advance(2 * time.Hour)
	e.heartbeatAll()
	// 没有 StateReport：清扫器不恢复（不知道容器是否正常）
	if err := e.svc.Sweep(e.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := e.get(inst.ID); !got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("resumed without state report: %+v", got)
	}
	e.reportRunning(e.machines[0], inst.ID)
	if err := e.svc.Sweep(e.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	got := e.get(inst.ID)
	if got.Suspended() || got.ExpiresAt == nil || !got.ExpiresAt.Equal(e.clock.Now().Add(frozen)) {
		t.Fatalf("after sweep = %+v", got)
	}
}

// 容器不在（报告为空）→ 保持暂停并告警一次；第二次报告变 running 才恢复。
func TestResumeMachineKeepsPausedWhenContainerDown(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour)
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.SuspendMachines(e.ctx, e.machines)), nil, 1, "suspended")
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		n, err := e.svc.ResumeMachine(e.ctx, e.machines[0], nil)
		mustCount(t, n, err, 0, "resumed with container down")
	}
	if got := e.get(inst.ID); !got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("must stay suspended: %+v", got)
	}
	var alerts int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'container_down' AND instance_id = $1 AND message = $2`,
		inst.ID, rental.AlertContainerDown("SN000", inst.ID)).Scan(&alerts)
	if alerts != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("container_down alerts = %d, want exactly 1", alerts)
	}
	n, err := e.svc.ResumeMachine(e.ctx, e.machines[0], []string{"ins-other000", inst.ID})
	mustCount(t, n, err, 1, "resumed when running")
	if got := e.get(inst.ID); got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("not resumed: %+v", got)
	}
}

// 清扫器兜底：用 last_state_report 里 running 的实例列表恢复；报告里没有的保持暂停并告警。
func TestSweepResumeUsesLastStateReport(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour)
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.SuspendMachines(e.ctx, e.machines)), nil, 1, "suspended")
	e.heartbeatAll()
	if err := e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, e.machines[0], "c1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := e.fleet.RecordStateReport(e.ctx, e.machines[0], fleet.StateReport{Instances: []fleet.InstanceState{{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseStopped}}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := e.svc.Sweep(e.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := e.get(inst.ID); !got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("stopped container must not resume: %+v", got)
	}
	var alerts int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'container_down'`).Scan(&alerts)
	if alerts != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("container_down alerts = %d", alerts)
	}
	e.reportRunning(e.machines[0], inst.ID)
	if err := e.svc.Sweep(e.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got := e.get(inst.ID); got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("not resumed: %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestFailTimedOutCommands(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 2)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	if err := e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, e.machines[0], "c1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	claimed, err := e.fleet.ClaimPending(e.ctx, []string{"c1"})
	if err != nil || len(claimed) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("claim = %+v, %v", claimed, err)
	}
	e.clock.Advance(rental.CreateCommandTimeout - time.Second)
	e.heartbeatAll()
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.FailTimedOutCommands(e.ctx)), nil, 0, "before timeout")
	e.clock.Advance(time.Second)
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.FailTimedOutCommands(e.ctx)), nil, 1, "at timeout")
	got := e.get(inst.ID)
	if got.State != rental.StateFailed || e.balance(e.userID) != startFunds || e.machineState(e.machines[0]) != fleet.StateFaulty {
		t.Fatalf("after timeout: state=%s balance=%d machine=%s", got.State, e.balance(e.userID), e.machineState(e.machines[0]))
	}
	cmds, _ := e.fleet.CommandsForInstance(e.ctx, inst.ID, 10)
	if cmds[0].Status != "failed" || cmds[0].Result != "timeout after 15m0s" {
		t.Fatalf("command = %+v", cmds[0])
	}
	var reason string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT reason FROM instance_events WHERE instance_id = $1 AND to_state = 'failed'`, inst.ID).Scan(&reason)
	if reason != "开通失败: timeout after 15m0s" {
		t.Fatalf("event reason = %q", reason)
	}

	// 离线机器的 sent 指令不计时；stop 超时 5 分钟 → stop_failed 告警
	_, inst2 := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	e.clock.Advance(24 * time.Hour)
	e.heartbeatAll()
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.ExpireDue(e.ctx)), nil, 1, "expired")
	_ = e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, e.machines[1], "c2")
	if claimed, _ := e.fleet.ClaimPending(e.ctx, []string{"c2"}); len(claimed) != 1 || claimed[0].Type != fleet.CmdStopInstance {
		t.Fatalf("claimed stop = %+v", claimed)
	}
	e.clock.Advance(rental.StopCommandTimeout + time.Minute)
	if _, err := e.fleet.MarkOfflineStale(e.ctx, rental.OfflineTimeout); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.FailTimedOutCommands(e.ctx)), nil, 0, "offline machine must not time out")
	e.heartbeatAll()
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.FailTimedOutCommands(e.ctx)), nil, 1, "stop timed out once online")
	var alerts int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'stop_failed' AND instance_id = $1`, inst2.ID).Scan(&alerts)
	if alerts != 1 || e.get(inst2.ID).State != rental.StateStopped {
		t.Fatalf("stop timeout: alerts=%d state=%s", alerts, e.get(inst2.ID).State)
	}
	if got := rental.CommandTimeout(fleet.CmdWipeInstance); got != 30*time.Minute {
		t.Fatalf("wipe timeout = %v", got)
	}
	if got := rental.CommandTimeout(fleet.CmdPullImage); got != 60*time.Minute {
		t.Fatalf("pull timeout = %v", got)
	}
}
```

```go
// reportRunning 模拟网关收到一份「该实例 running」的 StateReport（含本次连接归属）。
func (e *env) reportRunning(machineID int64, instanceID string) {
	e.t.Helper()
	if err := e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, machineID, "conn-test"); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := e.fleet.RecordStateReport(e.ctx, machineID, fleet.StateReport{Instances: []fleet.InstanceState{{InstanceID: instanceID, Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning}}}); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// firstOf 把 (n, err) 压成 n，错误直接失败；用于 mustCount 之前已能断言 err 的场合。
func firstOf(n int, err error) int {
	if err != nil {
		panic(err)
	}
	return n
}
```

```go
package rental

import "fmt"

// 告警正文必须稳定（不含数值），RaiseAlertOnce 按 kind + message 去重。
func AlertContainerDown(serial, instanceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上实例 %s 的容器未在运行", serial, instanceID)
}
func AlertContainerMissing(serial, instanceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上实例 %s 的容器已不存在", serial, instanceID)
}
func AlertDataDiskMissing(serial, instanceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上实例 %s 的数据盘未挂载", serial, instanceID)
}
func AlertOrphanContainer(serial, instanceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上存在不属于当前实例的容器 %s", serial, instanceID)
}
func AlertOrphanWipeFailed(serial, instanceID string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上实例 %s 的残留容器清理失败", serial, instanceID)
}
```

`internal/rental/sweeper.go`：

```go
// CommandTimeout 返回指令类型的超时值。
func CommandTimeout(t fleet.CommandType) time.Duration {
	switch t {
	case fleet.CmdCreateInstance:
		return CreateCommandTimeout
	case fleet.CmdStartInstance:
		return StartCommandTimeout
	case fleet.CmdStopInstance:
		return StopCommandTimeout
	case fleet.CmdWipeInstance:
		return WipeCommandTimeout
	}
	return PullCommandTimeout
}

// FailTimedOutCommands 把在线机器上超过类型超时仍未回报的 sent 指令判为失败，交给状态机。
func (s *Service) FailTimedOutCommands(ctx context.Context) (int, error) {
	now := s.d.Clock.Now()
	cmds, err := s.d.Fleet.SentBefore(ctx, now.Add(-StartCommandTimeout)) // 最短的超时值
	if err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	n := 0
	var errs []error
	for _, c := range cmds {
		timeout := CommandTimeout(c.Type)
		if c.SentAt == nil || now.Sub(*c.SentAt) < timeout {
			continue
		}
		if err := s.HandleCommandResult(ctx, c.ID, false, "timeout after "+timeout.String()); err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("command %s: %w", c.ID, err))
			continue
		}
		n++
	}
	return n, errors.Join(errs...)
}

// ResumeMachine 机器恢复在线后，只对容器确实在运行（runningIDs）的暂停实例补回时长并重算提醒标记；
// 其余暂停实例保持暂停并告警 container_down，由管理员换机或退款。
func (s *Service) ResumeMachine(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, runningIDs []string) (int, error) {
	now := s.d.Clock.Now()
	n := 0
	err := db.WithTx(ctx, s.d.Pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		list, err := collectInstances(tx.Query(ctx, `SELECT `+instanceCols+` FROM instances
			WHERE machine_id = $1 AND state = 'running' AND suspended_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id FOR UPDATE`, machineID))
		if err != nil || len(list) == 0 {
			return err
		}
		m, err := s.d.Fleet.Get(ctx, tx, machineID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, inst := range list {
			if !slices.Contains(runningIDs, inst.ID) {
				if err := s.d.Fleet.RaiseAlertOnce(ctx, tx, fleet.Alert{Level: "critical", Kind: "container_down",
					MachineID: machineID, InstanceID: inst.ID, Message: AlertContainerDown(m.Serial, inst.ID)}); err != nil {
					return err
				}
				continue
			}
			var remaining time.Duration
			if inst.PausedRemaining != nil {
				remaining = time.Duration(*inst.PausedRemaining) * time.Second
			}
			r24, r1 := reminderFlags(remaining)
			if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE instances SET expires_at = $2, suspended_at = NULL, paused_remaining = NULL,
					reminded_24h = $3, reminded_1h = $4, updated_at = $5 WHERE id = $1`,
				inst.ID, now.Add(remaining), r24, r1, now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := recordEvent(ctx, tx, inst.ID, StateRunning, StateRunning, "machine online: lease resumed", now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := s.refreshLease(ctx, tx, inst.ID, now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			n++
		}
		return nil
	})
	return n, err
}
```

`resumeOnline` 替换为：

```go
// resumeOnline 清扫器兜底：对已在线、且本次连接之后上报过 StateReport 的机器，按报告里 running 的实例恢复计时。
func (s *Service) resumeOnline(ctx context.Context) error {
	rows, err := s.d.Pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT machine_id FROM instances WHERE state = 'running' AND suspended_at IS NOT NULL ORDER BY machine_id`)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ids, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowTo[int64])
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	reports, err := s.d.Fleet.ReportsFor(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var errs []error
	for _, id := range ids {
		rep, ok := reports[id]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if _, err := s.ResumeMachine(ctx, id, rep.RunningIDs()); err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("machine %d: %w", id, err))
		}
	}
	return errors.Join(errs...)
}
```

### Task 5: rental 对账 `Reconcile`

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/rental/reconcile.go`
- Test: `internal/rental/reconcile_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 `Fleet.HasAnyOpenCommand` / `Enqueue`、Task 1 `fleet.StateReport` / `Phase*`、Task 4 `Alert*` 文案、`lockInstance`、`Fleet.RaiseAlertOnce`、`Fleet.Get`。
- Produces: `func (s *Service) Reconcile(ctx, machineID int64, report fleet.StateReport) error`——按规格 §3.2 表逐行处理，每个动作独立事务；`InstanceIDPattern = ^ins-[a-z0-9]{8}$`，不匹配的上报项记 `warning` 告警 `invalid_report`（文案「机器 {serial} 上报了无法识别的实例标识」）并跳过。恢复计时不在这里（网关另调 `ResumeMachine`）。

`internal/rental/reconcile_test.go`：

```go
package rental_test

import (
	"testing"

	"spark-cloud/internal/catalog"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

func report(states ...fleet.InstanceState) fleet.StateReport { return fleet.StateReport{Instances: states} }

// alertCount 统计告警；kind 或 instanceID 为空串表示不按该项筛选。
func (e *env) alertCount(kind, instanceID string) int {
	e.t.Helper()
	var n int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE ($1 = '' OR kind = $1) AND ($2 = '' OR instance_id = $2)`, kind, instanceID).Scan(&n)
	return n
}

func (e *env) openCommands(instanceID string, t fleet.CommandType) int {
	e.t.Helper()
	var n int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM agent_commands WHERE instance_id = $1 AND type = $2 AND status IN ('pending', 'sent')`, instanceID, string(t)).Scan(&n)
	return n
}

func TestReconcileOrphanContainerEnqueuesWipeOnce(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	_ = e.svc.Release(e.ctx, e.userID, inst.ID)
	e.ack(true) // released，机器 idle
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning})); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdWipeInstance) != 1 || e.alertCount("orphan_container", inst.ID) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("orphan: wipes=%d alerts=%d", e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdWipeInstance), e.alertCount("orphan_container", inst.ID))
	}
	var msg, level string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT message, level FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'orphan_container'`).Scan(&msg, &level)
	if msg != rental.AlertOrphanContainer("SN000", inst.ID) || level != "warning" {
		t.Fatalf("alert = %s %s", level, msg)
	}
	// 平台从未见过的 ID 同样清理
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: "ins-zzzzzzzz", Phase: fleet.PhaseStopped})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.openCommands("ins-zzzzzzzz", fleet.CmdWipeInstance) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("unknown instance not wiped")
	}
}

// 别的机器上的实例出现在这台机器的报告里 → 也是孤儿。
func TestReconcileInstanceOfOtherMachineIsOrphan(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 2)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d) // 分配到 machines[0]
	e.ack(true)
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[1], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var machineID int64
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT machine_id FROM agent_commands WHERE instance_id = $1 AND type = 'wipe_instance'`, inst.ID).Scan(&machineID)
	if machineID != e.machines[1] || e.alertCount("orphan_container", inst.ID) != 1 || e.get(inst.ID).State != rental.StateRunning {
		t.Fatalf("wipe on machine %d, alerts=%d, state=%s", machineID, e.alertCount("orphan_container", inst.ID), e.get(inst.ID).State)
	}
}

func TestReconcileInvalidInstanceIDIsAlertedAndSkipped(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	bad := report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: "../../etc/passwd", Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning}, fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: "INS-ABCDEFGH", Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning})
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], bad); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	var n int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM agent_commands`).Scan(&n)
	if n != 0 || e.alertCount("invalid_report", "") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("commands=%d alerts=%d", n, e.alertCount("invalid_report", ""))
	}
}

func TestReconcileRunningWhileStoppedEnqueuesStop(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	e.clock.Advance(24 * time.Hour)
	e.heartbeatAll()
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.ExpireDue(e.ctx)), nil, 1, "expired")
	// 到期的 stop 指令仍 pending → 有未完成指令，不重复入队
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("stop commands = %d", e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance))
	}
	e.ack(true) // stop 完成
	// 容器又跑起来了（例如管理员手工 docker start）→ 入队 stop
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseRunning})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance) != 1 || e.get(inst.ID).State != rental.StateStopped {
		t.Fatalf("stop enqueued = %d", e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance))
	}
	// 报告 stopped 与记录一致 → 无动作
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseStopped})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.openCommands(inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("stopped/stopped must not enqueue")
	}
}

func TestReconcileContainerDownAlertsWithoutAction(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	for _, rep := range []fleet.StateReport{report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseStopped}), report()} {
		if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], rep); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	var cmds int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM agent_commands WHERE status IN ('pending','sent')`).Scan(&cmds)
	got := e.get(inst.ID)
	if cmds != 0 || got.State != rental.StateRunning || got.Suspended() || e.alertCount("container_down", inst.ID) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("container down: cmds=%d state=%s alerts=%d", cmds, got.State, e.alertCount("container_down", inst.ID))
	}
	// 暂停中的实例由 ResumeMachine 负责告警，Reconcile 不重复
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.SuspendMachines(e.ctx, e.machines)), nil, 1, "suspended")
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.alertCount("container_down", inst.ID) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("suspended instance alerted again by reconcile")
	}
}

func TestReconcileMissingAndMissingData(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)
	_, inst := e.place(catalog.D1d)
	e.ack(true)
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report(fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: inst.ID, Phase: fleet.PhaseMissingData})); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.alertCount("data_disk_missing", inst.ID) != 1 {
		t.Fatal("data_disk_missing not raised")
	}
	e.clock.Advance(24 * time.Hour)
	e.heartbeatAll()
	mustCount(t, firstOf(e.svc.ExpireDue(e.ctx)), nil, 1, "expired")
	e.ack(true)
	if err := e.svc.Reconcile(e.ctx, e.machines[0], report()); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if e.alertCount("container_missing", inst.ID) != 1 || e.get(inst.ID).State != rental.StateStopped {
		t.Fatalf("container_missing = %d", e.alertCount("container_missing", inst.ID))
	}
	var msg string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT message FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'container_missing'`).Scan(&msg)
	if msg != rental.AlertContainerMissing("SN000", inst.ID) {
		t.Fatalf("message = %s", msg)
	}
}

func TestReconcileCreatingWipingAndUnknownUntouched(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t, 1)

	if n != 1 || e.alertCount("", "") != 0 {

	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `internal/rental/reconcile.go`**

```go
package rental

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"regexp"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
)

// InstanceIDPattern 是平台生成的实例 ID 形态（randx.ID("ins-", 8)）；Agent 上报的其他字符串一律不处理。
var InstanceIDPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^ins-[a-z0-9]{8}$`)

// Reconcile 按设计 §3.2 的对账表处理一份 StateReport。每个动作在独立事务内执行；
// 有实例行的先 lockInstance，以锁内读到的状态重新判定；只有实例没有任何未完成指令时才入队 Stop / Wipe。
// 恢复计时不在这里：网关在本函数之后用 report.RunningIDs() 调 ResumeMachine。
func (s *Service) Reconcile(ctx context.Context, machineID int64, report fleet.StateReport) error {
	m, err := s.d.Fleet.Get(ctx, s.d.Pool, machineID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var errs []error
	reported := map[string]bool{}
	invalid := false
	for _, st := range report.Instances {
		if !InstanceIDPattern.MatchString(st.InstanceID) {
			invalid = true
			continue
		}
		reported[st.InstanceID] = true
		if err := s.reconcileReported(ctx, m, st); err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("instance %s: %w", st.InstanceID, err))
		}
	}
	if invalid {
		if err := s.d.Fleet.RaiseAlertOnce(ctx, s.d.Pool, fleet.Alert{Level: "warning", Kind: "invalid_report", MachineID: machineID,
			Message: fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 上报了无法识别的实例标识", m.Serial)}); err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, err)
		}
	}
	expected, err := collectInstances(s.d.Pool.Query(ctx, `SELECT `+instanceCols+` FROM instances
		WHERE machine_id = $1 AND state IN ('creating', 'running', 'stopped', 'wiping') ORDER BY id`, machineID))
	if err != nil {
		return errors.Join(append(errs, err)...)
	}
	for _, inst := range expected {
		if reported[inst.ID] {
			continue
		}
		if err := s.reconcileMissing(ctx, m, inst.ID); err != nil {
			errs = append(errs, fmt.Errorf("instance %s: %w", inst.ID, err))
		}
	}
	return errors.Join(errs...)
}

func (s *Service) reconcileReported(ctx context.Context, m fleet.Machine, st fleet.InstanceState) error {
	return db.WithTx(ctx, s.d.Pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		inst, err := lockInstance(ctx, tx, st.InstanceID)
		if errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) {
			return s.wipeOrphan(ctx, tx, m, st.InstanceID)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if inst.MachineID != m.ID || inst.State == StateReleased || inst.State == StateFailed {
			return s.wipeOrphan(ctx, tx, m, inst.ID)
		}
		if st.Phase == fleet.PhaseMissingData {
			return s.alertOnce(ctx, tx, "data_disk_missing", m.ID, inst.ID, AlertDataDiskMissing(m.Serial, inst.ID))
		}
		switch inst.State {
		case StateStopped:
			switch st.Phase {
			case fleet.PhaseRunning:
				return s.enqueueIfIdle(ctx, tx, m.ID, inst.ID, fleet.CmdStopInstance)
			case fleet.PhaseStopped, fleet.PhaseUnknown:
				return nil
			}
		case StateRunning:
			if st.Phase == fleet.PhaseRunning || st.Phase == fleet.PhaseUnknown || inst.Suspended() {
				return nil // running 的恢复计时与暂停中的告警都由 ResumeMachine 处理
			}
			return s.alertOnce(ctx, tx, "container_down", m.ID, inst.ID, AlertContainerDown(m.Serial, inst.ID))
		}
		return nil // creating / wiping：等指令结果
	})
}

// reconcileMissing 处理平台记录里有、Agent 没有上报的实例。
func (s *Service) reconcileMissing(ctx context.Context, m fleet.Machine, instanceID string) error {
	return db.WithTx(ctx, s.d.Pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		inst, err := lockInstance(ctx, tx, instanceID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		switch inst.State {
		case StateRunning:
			if inst.Suspended() {
				return nil
			}
			return s.alertOnce(ctx, tx, "container_down", m.ID, inst.ID, AlertContainerDown(m.Serial, inst.ID))
		case StateStopped:
			return s.alertOnce(ctx, tx, "container_missing", m.ID, inst.ID, AlertContainerMissing(m.Serial, inst.ID))
		}
		return nil
	})
}

// wipeOrphan 对不属于本机当前实例的容器入队清理（instance_id 照填），并告警。
func (s *Service) wipeOrphan(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, m fleet.Machine, instanceID string) error {
	if err := s.enqueueIfIdle(ctx, tx, m.ID, instanceID, fleet.CmdWipeInstance); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return s.alertOnce(ctx, tx, "orphan_container", m.ID, instanceID, AlertOrphanContainer(m.Serial, instanceID))
}

// enqueueIfIdle 只在实例没有任何未完成指令时入队，避免与刚入队的 start、仍在执行的 stop、上一轮的孤儿 wipe 打架。
func (s *Service) enqueueIfIdle(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, machineID int64, instanceID string, t fleet.CommandType) error {
	open, err := s.d.Fleet.HasAnyOpenCommand(ctx, tx, instanceID)
	if err != nil || open {
		return err
	}
	_, err = s.d.Fleet.Enqueue(ctx, tx, machineID, instanceID, t, nil)
	return err
}

func (s *Service) alertOnce(ctx context.Context, q db.Querier, kind string, machineID int64, instanceID, message string) error {
	level := "critical"
	if kind == "orphan_container" {
		level = "warning"
	}
	return s.d.Fleet.RaiseAlertOnce(ctx, q, fleet.Alert{Level: level, Kind: kind, MachineID: machineID, InstanceID: instanceID, Message: message})
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 写 proto**

`api/proto/agent/v1/agent.proto`：

```proto
syntax = "proto3";

package spark.agent.v1;

option go_package = "spark-cloud/internal/agentpb;agentpb";

// AgentGateway 是平台侧网关：Enroll 不需要客户端证书（一次性令牌 + CSR），Connect 必须 mTLS。
service AgentGateway {
  rpc Enroll(EnrollRequest) returns (EnrollResponse);
  rpc Connect(stream AgentMessage) returns (stream PlatformMessage);
}

message EnrollRequest {
  string token = 1;    // spk1_<令牌>
  bytes csr_der = 2;   // PKCS#10 DER，平台只取其公钥
}

message EnrollResponse {
  bytes cert_der = 1;  // 机器证书 DER（CN = 序列号）
  bytes ca_der = 2;    // 根 CA 证书 DER
}

message AgentMessage {
  oneof msg {
    Hello hello = 1;          // 建流后第一条
    StateReport state = 2;    // 建流后第二条，此后每 60 秒一次，以及每条指令执行完后
    Heartbeat heartbeat = 3;  // 每 10 秒
    CommandResult result = 4;
  }
}

message PlatformMessage {
  oneof msg {
    Command command = 1;
    AgentConfig config = 2;   // 建流后平台发的第一条
  }
}

message Hello {
  string agent_version = 1;
  string hostname = 2;
  repeated string ips = 3;    // 本机全部 IPv4 地址
}

message AgentConfig {
  string lan_ip = 1;                 // machines.lan_ip，容器 SSH 端口发布到该地址
  uint32 heartbeat_interval_s = 2;
  repeated string allow_cidrs = 3;   // 内网白名单
  repeated string dns_servers = 4;
  uint32 bandwidth_mbps = 5;         // 默认限速
  uint32 data_size_gib = 6;          // 数据盘大小
}

message Command {
  string id = 1;           // agent_commands.id
  string instance_id = 2;  // pull_image 为空
  oneof body {
    CreateInstance create = 10;
    StartInstance start = 11;
    StopInstance stop = 12;
    WipeInstance wipe = 13;
    PullImage pull = 14;
  }
}

message CreateInstance {
  string image_ref = 1;
  string upstream_pubkey = 2;   // authorized_keys 一行
  uint32 bandwidth_mbps = 3;
  uint32 ssh_port = 4;          // 本计划固定 2222
  uint32 data_size_gib = 5;
}
message StartInstance {}
message StopInstance {}
message WipeInstance {}
message PullImage { string image_ref = 1; }

message CommandResult {
  string command_id = 1;
  bool ok = 2;
  string message = 3;   // 失败原因：命令 + stderr 末尾 500 字节
}

message StateReport { repeated InstanceState instances = 1; }

message InstanceState {
  string instance_id = 1;
  string phase = 2;   // running / stopped / missing_data / unknown
}

message Heartbeat { Metrics metrics = 1; }

message Metrics {
  double gpu_util = 1;
  double gpu_temp_c = 2;
  double gpu_power_w = 3;
  uint64 mem_total = 4;
  uint64 mem_used = 5;
  uint64 root_disk_total = 6;
  uint64 root_disk_used = 7;
  uint64 data_disk_used = 8;
  double load1 = 9;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 写一个冒烟测试**

`internal/agentpb/agentpb_test.go`：

```go
package agentpb_test

import (
	"testing"

	"google.golang.org/protobuf/proto"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
)

func TestCommandRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	in := &agentpb.PlatformMessage{Msg: &agentpb.PlatformMessage_Command{Command: &agentpb.Command{
		Id: "cmd-1", InstanceId: "ins-abcdefgh",
		Body: &agentpb.Command_Create{Create: &agentpb.CreateInstance{ImageRef: "nvcr.io/x:1", UpstreamPubkey: "ssh-ed25519 AAAA", BandwidthMbps: 200, SshPort: 2222, DataSizeGib: 2048}},
	}}}
	b, err := proto.Marshal(in)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var out agentpb.PlatformMessage
	if err := proto.Unmarshal(b, &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	c := out.GetCommand()
	if c.GetId() != "cmd-1" || c.GetCreate().GetSshPort() != 2222 || c.GetCreate().GetImageRef() != "nvcr.io/x:1" {
		t.Fatalf("round trip = %v", &out)
	}
	if got := agentpb.AgentGateway_ServiceDesc.ServiceName; got != "spark.agent.v1.AgentGateway" {
		t.Fatalf("service = %s", got)
	}
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `config.Config` 新字段：`AgentAddr string`（`SPARK_AGENT_ADDR`，默认 `:9443`）、`AgentHosts []string`（`SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS`，逗号分隔、去空白、去空项；dev 默认 `localhost,127.0.0.1`；prod 必填）、`AgentDownloadURL string`、`AgentBinarySHA256 string`、`UpstreamSSHKeyFile string`（prod 必填）、`AgentDataSizeGiB int`（默认 2048，须 ≥ 1）、`AgentAllowCIDRs []string`（每项必须是合法 CIDR）、`AgentDNSServers []string`（默认 `223.5.5.5,119.29.29.29`，每项必须是 IP，且私网地址必须落在 `AgentAllowCIDRs` 内——规格 §4.2）、`AgentBandwidthMbps int`（默认 200，须 ≥ 1）。
  - `func (c Config) AgentAdvertiseAddr() string`：`AgentHosts[0]` + `AgentAddr` 的端口（例如 `agent.example:9443`）。
  - `pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(path string) error`：生成 ed25519，OpenSSH 私钥 PEM 写 `path`（0600，已存在则报错「文件已存在」），公钥写 `path + ".pub"`。
  - `pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(path string) (string, error)`：读 OpenSSH 私钥，返回 `ssh-ed25519 AAAA...`（无换行、无注释）；文件 group/other 可读时报错「权限过宽」。
  - CLI：`platform admin gen-upstream-key --path <文件>`。
