```go
func TestLoadAgentDefaults(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := config.Load(env(map[string]string{"SPARK_DATABASE_URL": "x", "SPARK_SECRET_KEY": validKey}))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if c.AgentAddr != ":9443" || strings.Join(c.AgentHosts, ",") != "localhost,127.0.0.1" || c.AgentDataSizeGiB != 2048 ||
		c.AgentBandwidthMbps != 200 || strings.Join(c.AgentDNSServers, ",") != "223.5.5.5,119.29.29.29" || len(c.AgentAllowCIDRs) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("agent defaults = %+v", c)
	}
	if c.AgentAdvertiseAddr() != "localhost:9443" {
		t.Fatalf("advertise = %s", c.AgentAdvertiseAddr())
	}
}

func TestLoadAgentValues(t *testing.T) {
	c, err := config.Load(env(map[string]string{"SPARK_DATABASE_URL": "x", "SPARK_SECRET_KEY": validKey,
		"SPARK_AGENT_ADDR": "10.0.0.5:19443", "SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS": " agent.example , 10.0.0.5,, ", "SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS": "10.0.9.0/24, 192.168.1.10/32",
		"SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS": "1.1.1.1", "SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB": "512", "SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS": "50",
		"SPARK_AGENT_DOWNLOAD_URL": "http://files.local/agent", "SPARK_AGENT_BINARY_SHA256": "abc", "SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE": "/etc/spark/up.key"}))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if strings.Join(c.AgentHosts, ",") != "agent.example,10.0.0.5" || c.AgentAdvertiseAddr() != "agent.example:19443" ||
		strings.Join(c.AgentAllowCIDRs, ",") != "10.0.9.0/24,192.168.1.10/32" || c.AgentDNSServers[0] != "1.1.1.1" ||
		c.AgentDataSizeGiB != 512 || c.AgentBandwidthMbps != 50 || c.AgentDownloadURL != "http://files.local/agent" || c.UpstreamSSHKeyFile != "/etc/spark/up.key" {
		t.Fatalf("agent config = %+v", c)
	}
}

func TestLoadAgentErrors(t *testing.T) {
	with := func(k, v string) map[string]string {
		m := prodEnv()
		m[k] = v
		return m
	}
	if _, err := config.Load(env(prodEnv())); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("valid prod: %v", err)
	}
	cases := []struct{ name, key, value, want string }{
		{"prod hosts", "SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS", "", "SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS"},
		{"prod upstream", "SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE", "", "SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE"},
		{"bad cidr", "SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS", "10.0.0.0", "SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS"},
		{"bad dns", "SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS", "dns.example", "SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS"},
		{"private dns outside allow list", "SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS", "10.0.0.53", "SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS"},
		{"bad size", "SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB", "0", "SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB"},
		{"bad bw", "SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS", "x", "SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS"},
		{"bad addr", "SPARK_AGENT_ADDR", "9443", "SPARK_AGENT_ADDR"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		_, err := config.Load(env(with(c.key, c.value)))
		if err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), c.want) {
			t.Fatalf("%s: err = %v, want mention of %s", c.name, err, c.want)
		}
	}
	// 私网 DNS 落在白名单内则允许（规格 §4.2：DNS 须在白名单内或为公网地址）
	m := prodEnv()
	m["SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS"], m["SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS"] = "10.0.0.53", "10.0.0.0/24"
	if _, err := config.Load(env(m)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("private dns inside allow list: %v", err)
	}
}
```

`internal/pki/upstream_test.go`：

```go
package pki_test

import (
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
)

func TestUpstreamKeyGenerateAndLoad(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "upstream.key")
	if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(path); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(path); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "已存在") {
		t.Fatalf("overwrite = %v", err)
	}
	st, _ := os.Stat(path)
	if st.Mode().Perm() != 0o600 {
		t.Fatalf("perm = %o", st.Mode().Perm())
	}
	pub, err := pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(path)
	if err != nil || !strings.HasPrefix(pub, "ssh-ed25519 AAAA") || strings.ContainsAny(pub, "\n ") && strings.Count(pub, " ") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pub = %q, %v", pub, err)
	}
	pubFile, _ := os.ReadFile(path + ".pub")
	if strings.TrimSpace(string(pubFile)) != pub {
		t.Fatalf(".pub = %q", pubFile)
	}
	if err := os.Chmod(path, 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(path); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "权限") {
		t.Fatalf("loose perm = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "missing")); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing file accepted")
	}
}
```

```go
func TestAdminGenUpstreamKey(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "up.key")
	var out bytes.Buffer
	if err := runAdmin(context.Background(), nil, []string{"gen-upstream-key", "--path", path}, &out, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := os.Stat(path + ".pub"); err != nil || !strings.Contains(out.String(), "ssh-ed25519 ") {
		t.Fatalf("output = %s, err = %v", out.String(), err)
	}
	if err := runAdmin(context.Background(), nil, []string{"gen-upstream-key"}, &out, nil); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing --path accepted")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现配置**

`internal/platform/config/config.go` `Config` 追加：


`Load` 里在 `SweepInterval` 解析之后加：

```go
	c.AgentAddr = or("SPARK_AGENT_ADDR", ":9443")
	if _, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(c.AgentAddr); err != nil {
		return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("SPARK_AGENT_ADDR 必须是 host:port 形式，当前为 %q", c.AgentAddr)
	}
	c.AgentHosts = splitList(or("SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS", "localhost,127.0.0.1"))
	c.AgentDownloadURL = strings.TrimRight(getenv("SPARK_AGENT_DOWNLOAD_URL"), "/")
	c.AgentBinarySHA256 = strings.TrimSpace(getenv("SPARK_AGENT_BINARY_SHA256"))
	c.UpstreamSSHKeyFile = getenv("SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE")
	if c.AgentDataSizeGiB, err = positiveInt("SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB", or("SPARK_AGENT_DATA_SIZE_GIB", "2048")); err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	if c.AgentBandwidthMbps, err = positiveInt("SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS", or("SPARK_AGENT_BANDWIDTH_MBPS", "200")); err != nil {
		return Config{}, err
	}
	c.AgentAllowCIDRs = splitList(getenv("SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS"))
	for _, cidr := range c.AgentAllowCIDRs {
		if _, _, err := net.ParseCIDR(cidr); err != nil {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS 含无效网段 %q", cidr)
		}
	}
	c.AgentDNSServers = splitList(or("SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS", "223.5.5.5,119.29.29.29"))
	for _, ip := range c.AgentDNSServers {
		parsed := net.ParseIP(ip)
		if parsed == nil {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS 含无效地址 %q", ip)
		}
		// 容器的 DNS 查询由 dockerd 在宿主机网络命名空间代发、不经过 SPARK-FWD：私网 DNS 必须显式在白名单里
		if parsed.IsPrivate() && !inAnyCIDR(parsed, c.AgentAllowCIDRs) {
			return Config{}, fmt.Errorf("SPARK_AGENT_DNS_SERVERS 的 %s 是私网地址，必须包含在 SPARK_AGENT_ALLOW_CIDRS 内", ip)
		}
	}
	if c.Env == "prod" {
		if len(c.AgentHosts) == 0 {
			return Config{}, errors.New("生产环境必须设置 SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS（网关证书的域名或 IP）")
		}
		if c.UpstreamSSHKeyFile == "" {
			return Config{}, errors.New("生产环境必须设置 SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE（用 platform admin gen-upstream-key 生成）")
		}
	}
```

注意 `or("SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS", ...)` 在 prod 下也会给默认值，所以读取方式改为：

```go
	hosts := getenv("SPARK_AGENT_HOSTS")
	if hosts == "" && c.Env != "prod" {
		hosts = "localhost,127.0.0.1"
	}
	c.AgentHosts = splitList(hosts)
```

辅助函数：

```go
// splitList 按逗号切分、去空白、去空项。
func splitList(v string) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, p := range strings.Split(v, ",") {
		if p = strings.TrimSpace(p); p != "" {
			out = append(out, p)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func inAnyCIDR(ip net.IP, cidrs []string) bool {
	for _, c := range cidrs {
		if _, n, err := net.ParseCIDR(c); err == nil && n.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func positiveInt(name, v string) (int, error) {
	n, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(v))
	if err != nil || n <= 0 {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("%s 必须是正整数，当前为 %q", name, v)
	}
	return n, nil
}

// AgentAdvertiseAddr 是写进安装命令的平台地址：第一个主机名 + 网关端口。
func (c Config) AgentAdvertiseAddr() string {
	_, port, _ := net.SplitHostPort(c.AgentAddr)
	host := "localhost"
	if len(c.AgentHosts) > 0 {
		host = c.AgentHosts[0]
	}
	return net.JoinHostPort(host, port)
}
```

import 追加 `"net"`、`"strconv"`。

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `internal/pki/upstream.go`**

```go
package pki

import (
	"crypto/ed25519"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"strings"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"
)

// GenerateUpstreamKey 生成平台登录容器用的 ed25519 私钥（OpenSSH 格式，0600）与 .pub。
func GenerateUpstreamKey(path string) error {
	if _, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("文件已存在：%s", path)
	}
	pub, priv, err := ed25519.GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	block, err := ssh.MarshalPrivateKey(priv, "spark-cloud upstream")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(path, pem.EncodeToMemory(block), 0o600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	sshPub, err := ssh.NewPublicKey(pub)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.WriteFile(path+".pub", ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(sshPub), 0o644)
}

// LoadUpstreamPublicKey 读取私钥并派生 authorized_keys 格式的公钥（不含注释与换行）。
func LoadUpstreamPublicKey(path string) (string, error) {
	st, err := os.Stat(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	if st.Mode().Perm()&0o077 != 0 {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("上游 SSH 私钥 %s 权限过宽（%o），请 chmod 600", path, st.Mode().Perm())
	}
	raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	key, err := ssh.ParseRawPrivateKey(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("解析上游 SSH 私钥: %w", err)
	}
	priv, ok := key.(*ed25519.PrivateKey)
	if !ok {
		return "", errors.New("上游 SSH 私钥必须是 ed25519")
	}
	pub, err := ssh.NewPublicKey(priv.Public())
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(pub))), nil
}
```

（`ssh.ParseRawPrivateKey` 对 ed25519 返回 `*ed25519.PrivateKey`；若返回值类型为 `ed25519.PrivateKey`，两种都用 type switch 处理。）


`cmd/platform/main.go` 的 `adminMain`：`gen-upstream-key` 不需要数据库，在连接数据库之前短路：


实现：

```go
func adminGenUpstreamKey(args []string, out io.Writer) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("admin gen-upstream-key", flag.ContinueOnError)
	fs.SetOutput(out)
	path := fs.String("path", "", "私钥保存路径（将以 0600 写入，另生成 .pub）")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if *path == "" {
		return errors.New("请用 --path 指定私钥保存路径\n" + adminUsage)
	}
	if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(*path); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	pub, err := pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(*path)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	fmt.Fprintf(out, "已生成上游 SSH 密钥：%s（请设置 SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE=%s）\n公钥：%s\n", *path, *path, pub)
	return nil
}
```

### Task 8: `internal/agentgw` 骨架：TLS 配置、`Enroll`、限流、安装命令、启动 / 停止

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/agentgw/server.go`、`internal/agentgw/enroll.go`、`internal/agentgw/iplimit.go`
- Test: `internal/agentgw/helpers_test.go`、`internal/agentgw/enroll_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `Fleet.ConsumeEnrollToken` / `SetCertFingerprint` / `IssueEnrollToken`、Task 3 `pki.CA` / `pki.ParseCSR` / `pki.Fingerprint` / `pki.NewServerCerts`、Task 6 `agentpb`、Task 4/5 的 `rental.Service`、`audit`。
- Produces:
  - `type Config struct { AdvertiseAddr string; Hosts []string; AllowCIDRs, DNSServers []string; BandwidthMbps, DataSizeGiB int; UpstreamPubKey string; DownloadURL, BinarySHA256 string; Tick time.Duration; EnrollLimit int; HeartbeatInterval time.Duration }`（零值：`Tick` 1s、`EnrollLimit` 10、`HeartbeatInterval` 10s）
  - `type Deps struct { Pool *pgxpool.Pool; Fleet *fleet.Service; Rental *rental.Service; CA *pki.CA; Clock clock.Clock; CertClock clock.Clock; Log *slog.Logger }`——`Clock` 用于业务时间戳（心跳、`sent_at`、令牌、审计），**`CertClock` 用于签发与轮换证书**，nil 时为 `clock.Real{}`：gRPC 客户端按墙上时钟校验证书，用假时钟 T0 签的 1 年期证书会在 2027-01-01 之后让所有走真实 TLS 的测试失效。
  - `func New(cfg Config, d Deps) (*Server, error)`
  - `func (s *Server) TLSConfig() *tls.Config`
  - `func (s *Server) Serve(ctx context.Context, lis net.Listener) error`（启动 gRPC 服务、投递循环与每日证书轮换；`ctx` 结束时优雅停止并返回 nil）
  - `func (s *Server) ListenAndServe(ctx context.Context, addr string) error`
  - `func (s *Server) InstallCommand(token string) string`、`func (s *Server) CAFingerprint() string`、`func (s *Server) CAPEM() []byte`
  - `func InstallCommand(addr, token, caSHA256, downloadURL, binarySHA256 string) string`（纯函数）：有 `downloadURL` 时为 `curl -fsSL <url>/install.sh | sudo sh -s -- --platform <addr> --token <token> --ca-sha256 <fp> --binary-sha256 <sha> --download-url <url>`（`sudo` 会重置环境变量，脚本只能从参数得知二进制地址），否则 `sudo spark-agent enroll --platform <addr> --token <token> --ca-sha256 <fp>`。
  - gRPC 错误码：令牌问题 → `PermissionDenied`「令牌无效」；CSR 不合法 → `InvalidArgument`；限流 → `ResourceExhausted`。
  - 测试辅助（本任务建立，Task 9/10 复用）：`newEnv(t) *env`（`env{pool, clk, fleet, rental, ca, srv, addr, cancel}`，`srv` 用 `Tick: 20 * time.Millisecond`、`EnrollLimit` 默认），`e.machine(serial) int64`（`dbtest.CreateMachine`）、`e.enroll(t, machineID) *agentCreds`（签发令牌、生成密钥、CSR、调用 `Enroll`，返回 `{key, certDER, caDER}`）、`e.dial(creds *agentCreds) agentpb.AgentGatewayClient`（`creds == nil` 时不带客户端证书）。

`internal/agentgw/helpers_test.go`：

```go
package agentgw_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"crypto/x509/pkix"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentgw"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/catalog"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/identity"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/secretbox"
	"spark-cloud/internal/plugins/kyc"
	"spark-cloud/internal/plugins/sms"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
	"spark-cloud/internal/wallet"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { dbtest.Main(m) }

type noopNotifier struct{}

func (noopNotifier) NotifyExpiry(context.Context, int64, string, time.Duration) error { return nil }

type env struct {
	t      *testing.T
	ctx    context.Context
	pool   *pgxpool.Pool
	clk    *clock.Fake
	fleet  *fleet.Service
	rental *rental.Service
	wallet *wallet.Service
	ca     *pki.CA
	srv    *agentgw.Server
	addr   string
}

const tick = 20 * time.Millisecond

func newEnvWith(t *testing.T, tweak func(*agentgw.Config)) *env {
	t.Helper()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	clk := clock.NewFake(dbtest.T0)
	box, _ := secretbox.New(dbtest.Key)
	log := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	ids := identity.NewService(identity.Deps{Pool: pool, Clock: clk, SMS: &sms.Mock{}, KYC: kyc.Mock{}, Box: box})
	w := wallet.NewService(pool, clk)
	f := fleet.NewService(pool, clk)
	r := rental.NewService(rental.Deps{Pool: pool, Clock: clk, Box: box, Log: log, Users: ids, Wallet: w, Catalog: catalog.NewService(pool), Fleet: f, Notifier: noopNotifier{}})
	ca, err := pki.EnsureCA(ctx, pool, box, clk)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg := agentgw.Config{AdvertiseAddr: "agent.test:9443", Hosts: []string{"127.0.0.1"}, DNSServers: []string{"223.5.5.5"}, AllowCIDRs: []string{"10.0.9.0/24"},
		BandwidthMbps: 200, DataSizeGiB: 2048, UpstreamPubKey: "ssh-ed25519 AAAAtest", Tick: tick}
	if tweak != nil {
		tweak(&cfg)
	}
	srv, err := agentgw.New(cfg, agentgw.Deps{Pool: pool, Fleet: f, Rental: r, CA: ca, Clock: clk, Log: log})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(done); _ = srv.Serve(ctx, lis) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { cancel(); <-done })
	return &env{t: t, ctx: ctx, pool: pool, clk: clk, fleet: f, rental: r, wallet: w, ca: ca, srv: srv, addr: lis.Addr().String()}
}

func newEnv(t *testing.T) *env { return newEnvWith(t, nil) }

type agentCreds struct {
	key     *ecdsa.PrivateKey
	certDER []byte
	caDER   []byte
}

func (c *agentCreds) tlsCert() tls.Certificate {
	return tls.Certificate{Certificate: [][]byte{c.certDER}, PrivateKey: c.key}
}

func (e *env) machine(serial string) int64 { return dbtest.CreateMachine(e.t, e.pool, serial) }

// dial 建立到网关的 gRPC 连接；creds 为 nil 时不带客户端证书（只能调 Enroll）。
func (e *env) dial(creds *agentCreds) agentpb.AgentGatewayClient {
	e.t.Helper()
	tc := &tls.Config{RootCAs: e.ca.Pool(), ServerName: "127.0.0.1", MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS13}
	if creds != nil {
		tc.Certificates = []tls.Certificate{creds.tlsCert()}
	}
	cc, err := grpc.NewClient(e.addr, grpc.WithTransportCredentials(credentials.NewTLS(tc)))
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e.t.Cleanup(func() { _ = cc.Close() })
	return agentpb.NewAgentGatewayClient(cc)
}

func newKey(t *testing.T) *ecdsa.PrivateKey {
	t.Helper()
	k, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return k
}

func csrFor(t *testing.T, key *ecdsa.PrivateKey) []byte {
	t.Helper()
	der, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{Subject: pkix.Name{CommonName: "evil"}, DNSNames: []string{"evil"}}, key)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return der
}

// enroll 走完整接入：签发令牌 → Enroll → 返回可用于 Connect 的凭据。
func (e *env) enroll(machineID int64) *agentCreds {
	e.t.Helper()
	tok, err := e.fleet.IssueEnrollToken(e.ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, machineID)
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	key := newKey(e.t)
	resp, err := e.dial(nil).Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: tok.Plain, CsrDer: csrFor(e.t, key)})
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatalf("enroll: %v", err)
	}
	return &agentCreds{key: key, certDER: resp.CertDer, caDER: resp.CaDer}
}

// waitFor 轮询直到 cond 为真（最多 wait），否则失败。
func (e *env) waitFor(what string, cond func() bool) {
	e.t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if cond() {
			return
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	e.t.Fatalf("timeout waiting for %s", what)
}
```

`internal/agentgw/enroll_test.go`：

```go
package agentgw_test

import (
	"crypto/x509"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentgw"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
)

func TestEnrollIssuesCertBoundToSerial(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	if err := e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, mid, "stale"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	cert, err := x509.ParseCertificate(creds.certDER)
	if err != nil || cert.Subject.CommonName != "SN-001" || len(cert.DNSNames) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("cert = %+v, %v", cert.Subject, err)
	}
	if pki.Fingerprint(creds.caDER) != e.ca.Fingerprint() {
		t.Fatal("ca der mismatch")
	}
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if !m.Enrolled || m.CertFingerprint != pki.Fingerprint(creds.certDER) || m.Connected {
		t.Fatalf("machine = %+v", m)
	}
	var actorType, diff string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT actor_type, diff::text FROM audit_logs WHERE action = 'machine.enrolled'`).Scan(&actorType, &diff)
	if actorType != "system" || !strings.Contains(diff, `"issued_by": 1`) && !strings.Contains(diff, `"issued_by":1`) || !strings.Contains(diff, m.CertFingerprint) {
		t.Fatalf("audit = %s %s", actorType, diff)
	}
	// 重新接入：指纹被覆盖
	creds2 := e.enroll(mid)
	m, _ = e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if m.CertFingerprint != pki.Fingerprint(creds2.certDER) {
		t.Fatal("re-enroll must replace fingerprint")
	}
}

func TestEnrollRejectsBadTokensAndCSR(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	tok, _ := e.fleet.IssueEnrollToken(e.ctx, audit.Actor{}, mid)
	c := e.dial(nil)
	key := newKey(t)
	for name, token := range map[string]string{"garbage": "spk1_nope", "no prefix": strings.TrimPrefix(tok.Plain, "spk1_")} {
		_, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: token, CsrDer: csrFor(t, key)})
		if st, _ := status.FromError(err); st.Code() != codes.PermissionDenied || st.Message() != "令牌无效" {
			t.Fatalf("%s: %v", name, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: tok.Plain, CsrDer: []byte("not a csr")}); status.Code(err) != codes.InvalidArgument {
		t.Fatalf("bad csr = %v", err)
	}
	// 坏 CSR 没有消耗令牌；带空白的令牌可用；用过的不能再用
	if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: " " + tok.Plain + "\n", CsrDer: csrFor(t, key)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("trimmed token: %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: tok.Plain, CsrDer: csrFor(t, key)}); status.Code(err) != codes.PermissionDenied {
		t.Fatalf("reuse = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestEnrollRateLimited(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnvWith(t, func(c *agentgw.Config) { c.EnrollLimit = 2 })
	c := e.dial(nil)
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ {
		if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: "spk1_x", CsrDer: nil}); status.Code(err) != codes.PermissionDenied && status.Code(err) != codes.InvalidArgument {
			t.Fatalf("attempt %d = %v", i, err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: "spk1_x"}); status.Code(err) != codes.ResourceExhausted {
		t.Fatalf("third = %v", err)
	}
	e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	if _, err := c.Enroll(e.ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: "spk1_x"}); status.Code(err) == codes.ResourceExhausted {
		t.Fatalf("after window = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestInstallCommand(t *testing.T) {
	got := agentgw.InstallCommand("agent.example:9443", "spk1_abc", "ff00", "http://files.local/agent", "beef")
	want := "curl -fsSL http://files.local/agent/install.sh | sudo sh -s -- --platform agent.example:9443 --token spk1_abc --ca-sha256 ff00 --binary-sha256 beef --download-url http://files.local/agent"
	if got != want {
		t.Fatalf("got %q", got)
	}
	if got := agentgw.InstallCommand("agent.example:9443", "spk1_abc", "ff00", "", ""); got != "sudo spark-agent enroll --platform agent.example:9443 --token spk1_abc --ca-sha256 ff00" {
		t.Fatalf("bare = %q", got)
	}
	e := newEnv(t)
	if !strings.Contains(e.srv.InstallCommand("spk1_t"), "--platform agent.test:9443 --token spk1_t --ca-sha256 "+e.ca.Fingerprint()) {
		t.Fatalf("server command = %s", e.srv.InstallCommand("spk1_t"))
	}
}
```

`enroll_test.go` import 加 `"time"`。注意限流测试里空 CSR 的请求会先过限流再校验 CSR，所以前两次返回 `InvalidArgument` / `PermissionDenied` 都算「未被限流」——实现里限流检查必须在最前面。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `iplimit.go`**

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"sync"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
)

// ipLimiter 按来源 IP 做固定窗口计数（Enroll 每分钟 10 次）；窗口按注入时钟计算。
type ipLimiter struct {
	limit  int
	window time.Duration
	clk    clock.Clock
	mu     sync.Mutex
	seen   map[string]*ipWindow
}

type ipWindow struct {
	start time.Time
	n     int
}

func newIPLimiter(limit int, window time.Duration, clk clock.Clock) *ipLimiter {
	return &ipLimiter{limit: limit, window: window, clk: clk, seen: map[string]*ipWindow{}}
}

func (l *ipLimiter) allow(ip string) bool {
	now := l.clk.Now()
	l.mu.Lock()
	defer l.mu.Unlock()
	w := l.seen[ip]
	if w == nil || now.Sub(w.start) >= l.window {
		w = &ipWindow{start: now}
		l.seen[ip] = w
		if len(l.seen) > 10000 { // 防止内存无限增长
			for k, v := range l.seen {
				if now.Sub(v.start) >= l.window {
					delete(l.seen, k)
				}
			}
		}
	}
	w.n++
	return w.n <= l.limit
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `server.go`**

```go
// Package agentgw 是平台侧 Agent 网关：mTLS gRPC 服务，负责接入（Enroll）、双向流（Connect）、指令投递、心跳与对账。
package agentgw

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/tls"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/keepalive"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/peer"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

type Config struct {
	AdvertiseAddr     string   // 安装命令里的平台地址 host:port
	Hosts             []string // 服务端证书 SAN
	AllowCIDRs        []string
	DNSServers        []string
	BandwidthMbps     int
	DataSizeGiB       int
	UpstreamPubKey    string
	DownloadURL       string
	BinarySHA256      string
	Tick              time.Duration // 投递 tick，零值 1 秒
	EnrollLimit       int           // Enroll 每 IP 每分钟次数，零值 10
	HeartbeatInterval time.Duration // 下发给 Agent 的心跳间隔，零值 10 秒
}

type Deps struct {
	Pool      *pgxpool.Pool
	Fleet     *fleet.Service
	Rental    *rental.Service
	CA        *pki.CA
	Clock     clock.Clock // 业务时间戳（可注入假时钟）
	CertClock clock.Clock // 证书签发与轮换：必须是墙上时钟，nil → clock.Real{}（对端按真实时间校验证书）
	Log       *slog.Logger
}

type Server struct {
	agentpb.UnimplementedAgentGatewayServer
	cfg     Config
	d       Deps
	certs   *pki.ServerCerts
	limiter *ipLimiter
	conns   *registry
	ticks   atomic.Int64
	// deliveries 记录指令最近一次被本副本推送时的 tick 号（集成测试断言「≤2 个 tick 内投递」）。
	deliveries sync.Map
}

func New(cfg Config, d Deps) (*Server, error) {
	if cfg.Tick <= 0 {
		cfg.Tick = time.Second
	}
	if cfg.EnrollLimit <= 0 {
		cfg.EnrollLimit = 10
	}
	if cfg.HeartbeatInterval <= 0 {
		cfg.HeartbeatInterval = 10 * time.Second
	}
	if d.Log == nil {
		d.Log = slog.Default()
	}
	if d.CA == nil || d.Fleet == nil || d.Rental == nil || d.Pool == nil || d.Clock == nil {
		return nil, errors.New("agentgw: missing deps")
	}
	if len(cfg.Hosts) == 0 {
		cfg.Hosts = []string{"localhost", "127.0.0.1"}
	}
	if d.CertClock == nil {
		d.CertClock = clock.Real{}
	}
	certs, err := pki.NewServerCerts(d.CA, cfg.Hosts, d.CertClock)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &Server{cfg: cfg, d: d, certs: certs, limiter: newIPLimiter(cfg.EnrollLimit, time.Minute, d.Clock), conns: newRegistry()}, nil
}

// TLSConfig：客户端证书可选（Enroll 不带、Connect 必须带且由 CA 签发），最低 TLS 1.3，服务端证书热替换。
func (s *Server) TLSConfig() *tls.Config {
	return &tls.Config{
		MinVersion:     tls.VersionTLS13,
		ClientAuth:     tls.VerifyClientCertIfGiven,
		ClientCAs:      s.d.CA.Pool(),
		GetCertificate: s.certs.GetCertificate,
	}
}

func (s *Server) CAFingerprint() string { return s.d.CA.Fingerprint() }
func (s *Server) CAPEM() []byte        { return s.d.CA.CertPEM() }

// InstallCommand 生成后台展示的一次性安装命令。
func (s *Server) InstallCommand(token string) string {
	return InstallCommand(s.cfg.AdvertiseAddr, token, s.d.CA.Fingerprint(), s.cfg.DownloadURL, s.cfg.BinarySHA256)
}

func InstallCommand(addr, token, caSHA256, downloadURL, binarySHA256 string) string {
	if downloadURL == "" {
		return fmt.Sprintf("sudo spark-agent enroll --platform %s --token %s --ca-sha256 %s", addr, token, caSHA256)
	}
	// 通过管道 + sudo 执行时脚本拿不到任何环境变量，也不知道自己来自哪个 URL，所以二进制地址必须作为参数带上
	base := strings.TrimRight(downloadURL, "/")
	return fmt.Sprintf("curl -fsSL %s/install.sh | sudo sh -s -- --platform %s --token %s --ca-sha256 %s --binary-sha256 %s --download-url %s",
		base, addr, token, caSHA256, binarySHA256, base)
}

func (s *Server) ListenAndServe(ctx context.Context, addr string) error {
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", addr)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return s.Serve(ctx, lis)
}

// Serve 运行 gRPC 服务、投递循环与每日证书检查，直到 ctx 结束。
func (s *Server) Serve(ctx context.Context, lis net.Listener) error {
	gs := grpc.NewServer(
		grpc.Creds(credentials.NewTLS(s.TLSConfig())),
		grpc.KeepaliveParams(keepalive.ServerParameters{Time: 30 * time.Second, Timeout: 10 * time.Second}),
		grpc.KeepaliveEnforcementPolicy(keepalive.EnforcementPolicy{MinTime: 10 * time.Second, PermitWithoutStream: true}),
	)
	agentpb.RegisterAgentGatewayServer(gs, s)
	errc := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { errc <- gs.Serve(lis) }()
	go s.runTicks(ctx)
	go s.runCertRotation(ctx)
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
		s.conns.abortAll(errors.New("gateway shutting down"))
		gs.Stop()
		<-errc
		return nil
	case err := <-errc:
		return err
	}
}

func (s *Server) runCertRotation(ctx context.Context) {
	t := time.NewTicker(24 * time.Hour)
	defer t.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-t.C:
			if rotated, err := s.certs.Rotate(); err != nil {
				s.d.Log.Error("agent gateway cert rotation failed", "err", err)
			} else if rotated {
				s.d.Log.Info("agent gateway server certificate renewed")
			}
		}
	}
}

// RotateServerCert 供测试与运维手动触发证书检查。
func (s *Server) RotateServerCert() (bool, error) { return s.certs.Rotate() }

func peerIP(ctx context.Context) string {
	p, ok := peer.FromContext(ctx)
	if !ok || p.Addr == nil {
		return ""
	}
	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(p.Addr.String())
	if err != nil {
		return p.Addr.String()
	}
	return host
}
```

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"context"
	"time"
)

func (s *Server) runTicks(ctx context.Context) {
	t := time.NewTicker(s.cfg.Tick)
	defer t.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-t.C:
			s.ticks.Add(1) // Task 10 在此处执行投递
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 实现 `enroll.go`**

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/db"
)

// Enroll：校验令牌 → 校验 CSR 签名并只取公钥 → 签发 CN=序列号 的证书 → 写指纹并清空连接归属 → 标记令牌已用 → 审计。
// 全部在一个事务里；令牌类失败统一返回「令牌无效」。
func (s *Server) Enroll(ctx context.Context, req *agentpb.EnrollRequest) (*agentpb.EnrollResponse, error) {
	ip := peerIP(ctx)
	if !s.limiter.allow(ip) {
		return nil, status.Error(codes.ResourceExhausted, "操作过于频繁，请稍后再试")
	}
	pub, err := pki.ParseCSR(req.GetCsrDer())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, status.Error(codes.InvalidArgument, "CSR 无效")
	}
	now := s.d.Clock.Now()
	var certDER []byte
	err = db.WithTx(ctx, s.d.Pool, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		row, err := s.d.Fleet.ConsumeEnrollToken(ctx, tx, req.GetToken())
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		der, err := s.d.CA.IssueMachine(pub, row.Serial, s.d.CertClock.Now()) // 证书有效期按墙上时钟；审计仍用业务时钟 now
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		fp := pki.Fingerprint(der)
		if err := s.d.Fleet.SetCertFingerprint(ctx, tx, row.MachineID, fp); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		certDER = der
		return audit.Record(ctx, tx, now, audit.Actor{IP: ip}.Entry("machine.enrolled", "machine", strconv.FormatInt(row.MachineID, 10),
			map[string]any{"token_id": row.ID, "issued_by": row.IssuedBy, "fingerprint": fp}))
	})
	if errors.Is(err, fleet.ErrEnrollToken) {
		return nil, status.Error(codes.PermissionDenied, "令牌无效")
	}
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("enroll failed", "ip", ip, "err", err)
		return nil, status.Error(codes.Internal, "内部错误")
	}
	return &agentpb.EnrollResponse{CertDer: certDER, CaDer: s.d.CA.CertDER()}, nil
}
```

`helpers_test.go` 追加：

```go
type agentStream struct {
	t      *testing.T
	stream agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectClient
	msgs   chan *agentpb.PlatformMessage
	errs   chan error
}

// connect 打开双向流并在后台收消息；不发 Hello。
func (e *env) connect(creds *agentCreds) *agentStream {
	e.t.Helper()
	stream, err := e.dial(creds).Connect(e.ctx)
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a := &agentStream{t: e.t, stream: stream, msgs: make(chan *agentpb.PlatformMessage, 64), errs: make(chan error, 1)}
	go func() {
		for {
			m, err := stream.Recv()
			if err != nil {
				a.errs <- err
				return
			}
			a.msgs <- m
		}
	}()
	return a
}

func (a *agentStream) send(m *agentpb.AgentMessage) {
	a.t.Helper()
	if err := a.stream.Send(m); err != nil {
		a.t.Fatalf("send: %v", err)
	}
}
func (a *agentStream) hello(ips ...string) {
	a.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Hello{Hello: &agentpb.Hello{AgentVersion: "test-0.1", Hostname: "spark", Ips: ips}}})
}
func (a *agentStream) report(states ...*agentpb.InstanceState) {
	a.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_State{State: &agentpb.StateReport{Instances: states}}})
}
func (a *agentStream) heartbeat(m *agentpb.Metrics) {
	a.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Heartbeat{Heartbeat: &agentpb.Heartbeat{Metrics: m}}})
}
func (a *agentStream) result(id string, ok bool, msg string) {
	a.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Result{Result: &agentpb.CommandResult{CommandId: id, Ok: ok, Message: msg}}})
}
func (a *agentStream) next(timeout time.Duration) *agentpb.PlatformMessage {
	select {
	case m := <-a.msgs:
		return m
	case <-time.After(timeout):
		return nil
	}
}
func (a *agentStream) waitErr(timeout time.Duration) error {
	select {
	case err := <-a.errs:
		return err
	case <-time.After(timeout):
		return nil
	}
}
func running(id string) *agentpb.InstanceState { return &agentpb.InstanceState{InstanceId: id, Phase: "running"} }
func stopped(id string) *agentpb.InstanceState { return &agentpb.InstanceState{InstanceId: id, Phase: "stopped"} }

// up 建流 + Hello + 空 StateReport，并等到平台处理完首个报告（可投递）。
func (e *env) up(creds *agentCreds, machineID int64, states ...*agentpb.InstanceState) *agentStream {
	e.t.Helper()
	a := e.connect(creds)
	a.hello("10.0.0.10")
	if cfg := a.next(2 * time.Second); cfg == nil || cfg.GetConfig() == nil {
		e.t.Fatalf("expected AgentConfig first, got %v", cfg)
	}
	before := e.srv.ReportCount(machineID)
	a.report(states...)
	e.waitFor("first report", func() bool { return e.srv.ReportCount(machineID) > before })
	return a
}

func (e *env) alertCount(kind string) int {
	e.t.Helper()
	var n int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE kind = $1`, kind).Scan(&n)
	return n
}
```

`internal/agentgw/connect_test.go`：

```go
package agentgw_test

import (
	"math"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/catalog"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

func TestConnectRequiresValidCert(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	// 没有客户端证书
	a := e.connect(nil)
	a.hello()
	if err := a.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("no cert = %v", err)
	}
	old := e.enroll(mid)
	fresh := e.enroll(mid) // 重新接入：旧证书失效
	a = e.connect(old)
	a.hello()
	if err := a.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("old cert = %v", err)
	}
	a = e.connect(fresh)
	a.hello()
	if m := a.next(2 * time.Second); m == nil || m.GetConfig() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("fresh cert must get config, got %v", m)
	}
	// 撤销接入后同一证书被拒
	if err := e.fleet.RevokeEnrollment(e.ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, mid); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a2 := e.connect(fresh)
	a2.hello()
	if err := a2.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Unauthenticated {
		t.Fatalf("revoked = %v", err)
	}
	// 第一条不是 Hello → InvalidArgument
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	a3 := e.connect(creds)
	a3.report()
	if err := a3.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.InvalidArgument {
		t.Fatalf("no hello = %v", err)
	}
}

func TestConnectConfigHeartbeatMetricsAndAlerts(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	_, _ = e.pool.Exec(e.ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_online = false, last_seen_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, mid)
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	a := e.connect(creds)
	a.hello("10.0.0.10", "192.168.1.5")
	cfg := a.next(2 * time.Second).GetConfig()
	if cfg.GetLanIp() != "10.0.0.10" || cfg.GetHeartbeatIntervalS() != 10 || cfg.GetDnsServers()[0] != "223.5.5.5" ||
		cfg.GetAllowCidrs()[0] != "10.0.9.0/24" || cfg.GetBandwidthMbps() != 200 || cfg.GetDataSizeGib() != 2048 {
		t.Fatalf("config = %v", cfg)
	}
	e.waitFor("online", func() bool { m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid); return m.AgentOnline && m.Connected })
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if m.AgentConnectedAt == nil || m.LastSeenAt == nil || m.AgentVersion != "test-0.1" || e.alertCount("lan_ip_mismatch") != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("after hello = %+v alerts=%d", m, e.alertCount("lan_ip_mismatch"))
	}
	before := e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid)
	a.heartbeat(&agentpb.Metrics{GpuUtil: 12, GpuTempC: 95, GpuPowerW: 30, MemTotal: 128 << 30, MemUsed: 8 << 30, RootDiskTotal: 1000, RootDiskUsed: 950, Load1: 0.5})
	e.waitFor("heartbeat", func() bool { return e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid) > before })
	m, _ = e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if string(m.LastMetrics) == "" || !containsJSON(string(m.LastMetrics), `"gpu_temp_c": 95`) {
		t.Fatalf("metrics = %s", m.LastMetrics)
	}
	for i := 0; i < 2; i++ { // 同样的越限心跳只告警一次
		before = e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid)
		a.heartbeat(&agentpb.Metrics{GpuTempC: 96, RootDiskTotal: 1000, RootDiskUsed: 960})
		e.waitFor("heartbeat", func() bool { return e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid) > before })
	}
	if e.alertCount("gpu_temp_high") != 1 || e.alertCount("root_disk_high") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("threshold alerts gpu=%d disk=%d", e.alertCount("gpu_temp_high"), e.alertCount("root_disk_high"))
	}
	var msg string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT message FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'root_disk_high'`).Scan(&msg)
	if msg != "机器 SN-001 根分区使用率超过 90%" {
		t.Fatalf("message = %q", msg)
	}
}

func TestHeartbeatSanitizesNonFiniteMetrics(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	a := e.up(e.enroll(mid), mid)
	before := e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid)
	a.heartbeat(&agentpb.Metrics{GpuUtil: math.NaN(), GpuTempC: math.Inf(1), Load1: 1.5})
	e.waitFor("heartbeat", func() bool { return e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid) > before })
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if !containsJSON(string(m.LastMetrics), `"load1": 1.5`) || !containsJSON(string(m.LastMetrics), `"gpu_util": 0`) {
		t.Fatalf("metrics = %s", m.LastMetrics)
	}
	if err := a.waitErr(200 * time.Millisecond); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("stream dropped: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestLanIPMismatchAlert(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	for i, ips := range [][]string{{"192.168.9.9"}, {}} {
		mid := e.machine("SN-00" + string(rune('1'+i)))
		a := e.connect(e.enroll(mid))
		a.hello(ips...)
		if a.next(2*time.Second) == nil {
			t.Fatal("no config")
		}
		e.waitFor("alert", func() bool { return e.alertCount("lan_ip_mismatch") == i+1 })
	}
	var msg string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT message FROM alerts WHERE kind = 'lan_ip_mismatch' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1`).Scan(&msg)
	if msg != "机器 SN-001 登记的内网 IP 与 Agent 上报的地址不一致" {
		t.Fatalf("message = %q", msg)
	}
}

func TestStateReportReconcilesAndResumes(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	uid := dbtest.CreateUser(t, e.pool, "13600000001", true)
	dbtest.Fund(t, e.pool, uid, 1_000_000)
	_, inst, err := e.rental.Place(e.ctx, rental.PlaceRequest{UserID: uid, ProductID: dbtest.ProductID(t, e.pool), ImageID: dbtest.ImageID(t, e.pool), Duration: catalog.D1d})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, mid)
	if err := e.rental.HandleCommandResult(e.ctx, cmds[0].ID, true, "ok"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e.clk.Advance(time.Hour)
	if _, err := e.rental.SuspendMachines(e.ctx, []int64{mid}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	// 报告里没有该实例 → 保持暂停并告警；报告 running → 恢复
	a := e.up(creds, mid)
	got, _ := e.rental.Get(e.ctx, inst.ID)
	if !got.Suspended() || e.alertCount("container_down") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("after empty report: suspended=%v alerts=%d", got.Suspended(), e.alertCount("container_down"))
	}
	before := e.srv.ReportCount(mid)
	a.report(running(inst.ID))
	e.waitFor("second report", func() bool { return e.srv.ReportCount(mid) > before })
	if got, _ = e.rental.Get(e.ctx, inst.ID); got.Suspended() {
		t.Fatal("not resumed after running report")
	}
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if m.StateReportedAt == nil || !containsJSON(string(mustReport(e, mid)), inst.ID) {
		t.Fatalf("state report not stored: %+v", m)
	}
}

func TestNewConnectionReplacesOldAndDisconnectClearsOwnConn(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	a := e.up(creds, mid)
	b := e.up(creds, mid)
	if err := a.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Aborted {
		t.Fatalf("old stream = %v", err)
	}
	if !e.srv.Connected(mid) {
		t.Fatal("new stream must be registered")
	}
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if !m.Connected {
		t.Fatal("old stream detach must not clear new conn id")
	}
	_ = b.stream.CloseSend()
	e.waitFor("conn cleared", func() bool { m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid); return !m.Connected && !e.srv.Connected(mid) })
	if m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid); !m.AgentOnline {
		t.Fatal("disconnect must not mark offline (sweeper does after 5 minutes)")
	}
}

func TestUnknownCommandResultIsIgnored(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	a := e.up(e.enroll(mid), mid)
	a.result("no-such-command", true, "ok")
	before := e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid)
	a.heartbeat(&agentpb.Metrics{Load1: 1})
	e.waitFor("heartbeat after bogus result", func() bool { return e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid) > before })
	if err := a.waitErr(100 * time.Millisecond); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("stream dropped: %v", err)
	}
}
```

```go
// containsJSON 忽略 jsonb 重新格式化造成的空格差异。
func containsJSON(doc, fragment string) bool {
	norm := func(s string) string { return strings.ReplaceAll(s, " ", "") }
	return strings.Contains(norm(doc), norm(fragment))
}

func mustReport(e *env, machineID int64) []byte {
	e.t.Helper()
	var raw []byte
	if err := e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT last_state_report FROM machines WHERE id = $1`, machineID).Scan(&raw); err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return raw
}
```

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"math"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
)

func toStateReport(r *agentpb.StateReport) fleet.StateReport {
	out := fleet.StateReport{Instances: []fleet.InstanceState{}}
	for _, s := range r.GetInstances() {
		out.Instances = append(out.Instances, fleet.InstanceState{InstanceID: s.GetInstanceId(), Phase: s.GetPhase()})
	}
	return out
}

// metricsDoc 是写入 machines.last_metrics 的形态；字段名与前端 Metrics 类型一致。
type metricsDoc struct {
	GPUUtil       float64 `json:"gpu_util"`
	GPUTempC      float64 `json:"gpu_temp_c"`
	GPUPowerW     float64 `json:"gpu_power_w"`
	MemTotal      uint64  `json:"mem_total"`
	MemUsed       uint64  `json:"mem_used"`
	RootDiskTotal uint64  `json:"root_disk_total"`
	RootDiskUsed  uint64  `json:"root_disk_used"`
	DataDiskUsed  uint64  `json:"data_disk_used"`
	Load1         float64 `json:"load1"`
}

func finite(v float64) float64 {
	if math.IsNaN(v) || math.IsInf(v, 0) {
		return 0
	}
	return v
}

// metricsJSON 把指标转成 JSON；NaN / Inf 置 0，否则 json.Marshal 会失败导致断流。
func metricsJSON(m *agentpb.Metrics) (json.RawMessage, error) {
	d := metricsDoc{GPUUtil: finite(m.GetGpuUtil()), GPUTempC: finite(m.GetGpuTempC()), GPUPowerW: finite(m.GetGpuPowerW()),
		MemTotal: m.GetMemTotal(), MemUsed: m.GetMemUsed(), RootDiskTotal: m.GetRootDiskTotal(), RootDiskUsed: m.GetRootDiskUsed(),
		DataDiskUsed: m.GetDataDiskUsed(), Load1: finite(m.GetLoad1())}
	return json.Marshal(d)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `connect.go`（整体替换 Task 8 的桩）**

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"github.com/google/uuid"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/peer"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
)

const helloTimeout = 10 * time.Second

// conn 是本副本持有的一条 Agent 流。
type conn struct {
	id          string
	machineID   int64
	serial      string
	fingerprint string
	stream      agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectServer
	sendMu      sync.Mutex

	mu     sync.Mutex
	pushed map[string]bool // 已向该流推送、尚未收到结果的指令 id（补推集合）

	ready      atomic.Bool // 首个 StateReport 已处理，可投递
	readyTick  atomic.Int64
	reports    atomic.Int64
	heartbeats atomic.Int64

	done     chan struct{}
	doneOnce sync.Once
	abortErr error
}

func (c *conn) abort(err error) {
	c.doneOnce.Do(func() {
		c.abortErr = err
		close(c.done)
	})
}

func (c *conn) send(m *agentpb.PlatformMessage) error {
	c.sendMu.Lock()
	defer c.sendMu.Unlock()
	return c.stream.Send(m)
}

func (c *conn) markPushed(id string) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.pushed[id] = true
	c.mu.Unlock()
}

func (c *conn) wasPushed(id string) bool {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.pushed[id]
}

func (c *conn) unpush(id string) {
	c.mu.Lock()
	delete(c.pushed, id)
	c.mu.Unlock()
}

// retainPushed 只保留仍为 sent 的指令：被 CancelCommands 取消或已超时的指令不会再有结果，否则集合在连接存活期内只增不减。
func (c *conn) retainPushed(ids []string) {
	keep := make(map[string]bool, len(ids))
	for _, id := range ids {
		keep[id] = true
	}
	c.mu.Lock()
	for id := range c.pushed {
		if !keep[id] {
			delete(c.pushed, id)
		}
	}
	c.mu.Unlock()
}

type registry struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	byMachine map[int64]*conn
	byConn    map[string]*conn
}

func newRegistry() *registry { return &registry{byMachine: map[int64]*conn{}, byConn: map[string]*conn{}} }

// replace 登记新流并返回被顶掉的旧流（同一台机器）。
func (r *registry) replace(c *conn) *conn {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	old := r.byMachine[c.machineID]
	r.byMachine[c.machineID] = c
	r.byConn[c.id] = c
	if old != nil {
		delete(r.byConn, old.id)
	}
	return old
}

// remove 只在该流仍是机器当前流时移除。
func (r *registry) remove(c *conn) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	if r.byMachine[c.machineID] == c {
		delete(r.byMachine, c.machineID)
	}
	delete(r.byConn, c.id)
}

func (r *registry) byID(id string) *conn {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return r.byConn[id]
}

func (r *registry) get(machineID int64) *conn {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return r.byMachine[machineID]
}

func (r *registry) snapshot() []*conn {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]*conn, 0, len(r.byMachine))
	for _, c := range r.byMachine {
		out = append(out, c)
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b *conn) int { return int(a.machineID - b.machineID) })
	return out
}

func (r *registry) abortAll(err error) {
	for _, c := range r.snapshot() {
		c.abort(err)
	}
}

// Connected 报告本副本是否持有该机器的流。
func (s *Server) Connected(machineID int64) bool { return s.conns.get(machineID) != nil }

func (s *Server) ReportCount(machineID int64) int64 {
	if c := s.conns.get(machineID); c != nil {
		return c.reports.Load()
	}
	return 0
}

func (s *Server) HeartbeatCount(machineID int64) int64 {
	if c := s.conns.get(machineID); c != nil {
		return c.heartbeats.Load()
	}
	return 0
}

// ReadyTick 返回该机器当前流处理完首个 StateReport 时的 tick 号。
func (s *Server) ReadyTick(machineID int64) (int64, bool) {
	c := s.conns.get(machineID)
	if c == nil || !c.ready.Load() {
		return 0, false
	}
	return c.readyTick.Load(), true
}

func (s *Server) Ticks() int64 { return s.ticks.Load() }

// authenticate 校验客户端证书：由 CA 签发（TLS 层已验）→ CN 对应机器 → 指纹非空且匹配。
func (s *Server) authenticate(ctx context.Context) (fleet.Machine, string, error) {
	p, ok := peer.FromContext(ctx)
	if !ok {
		return fleet.Machine{}, "", status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "no peer")
	}
	ti, ok := p.AuthInfo.(credentials.TLSInfo)
	if !ok || len(ti.State.VerifiedChains) == 0 || len(ti.State.PeerCertificates) == 0 {
		return fleet.Machine{}, "", status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "client certificate required")
	}
	leaf := ti.State.PeerCertificates[0]
	fp := pki.Fingerprint(leaf.Raw)
	if now := s.d.CertClock.Now(); now.Before(leaf.NotBefore) || now.After(leaf.NotAfter) {
		return fleet.Machine{}, "", status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "certificate expired")
	}
	m, err := s.d.Fleet.MachineBySerial(ctx, leaf.Subject.CommonName)
	if errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) || (err == nil && (m.CertFingerprint == "" || m.CertFingerprint != fp)) {
		return fleet.Machine{}, "", status.Error(codes.Unauthenticated, "certificate not recognised")
	}
	if err != nil {
		return fleet.Machine{}, "", status.Error(codes.Internal, "lookup failed")
	}
	return m, fp, nil
}

// Connect 处理一条 Agent 流：建流校验 → Hello → 登记归属与心跳 → 下发 AgentConfig → 循环处理消息。
func (s *Server) Connect(stream agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectServer) error {
	ctx := stream.Context()
	m, fp, err := s.authenticate(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	first, err := recvTimeout(ctx, stream, helloTimeout)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	hello := first.GetHello()
	if hello == nil {
		return status.Error(codes.InvalidArgument, "first message must be Hello")
	}
	c := &conn{id: uuid.NewString(), machineID: m.ID, serial: m.Serial, fingerprint: fp, stream: stream, pushed: map[string]bool{}, done: make(chan struct{})}
	if err := s.d.Fleet.AttachAgentConn(ctx, m.ID, c.id); err != nil {
		return status.Error(codes.Internal, "attach failed")
	}
	// 归属已写入数据库：从这里起任何返回路径都要清掉自己的归属（只清自己的）
	defer func() {
		s.conns.remove(c)
		if err := s.d.Fleet.DetachAgentConn(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), m.ID, c.id); err != nil {
			s.d.Log.Error("detach agent conn", "machine", m.ID, "err", err)
		}
	}()
	if _, err := s.d.Fleet.Heartbeat(ctx, m.ID); err != nil {
		return status.Error(codes.Internal, "heartbeat failed")
	}
	if old := s.conns.replace(c); old != nil {
		old.abort(status.Error(codes.Aborted, "replaced by a newer connection"))
	}
	if err := s.d.Fleet.RecordMetrics(ctx, m.ID, hello.GetAgentVersion(), nil); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("record agent version", "machine", m.ID, "err", err)
	}
	if err := c.send(&agentpb.PlatformMessage{Msg: &agentpb.PlatformMessage_Config{Config: s.agentConfig(m)}}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.checkLanIP(ctx, m, hello.GetIps())

	msgs := make(chan *agentpb.AgentMessage)
	recvErr := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		for {
			msg, err := stream.Recv()
			if err != nil {
				recvErr <- err
				return
			}
			select {
			case msgs <- msg:
			case <-c.done:
				return
			}
		}
	}()
	for {
		select {
		case <-c.done:
			return c.abortErr
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case err := <-recvErr:
			if errors.Is(err, io.EOF) {
				return nil
			}
			return err
		case msg := <-msgs:
			s.handleMessage(ctx, c, msg)
		}
	}
}

func recvTimeout(ctx context.Context, stream agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectServer, d time.Duration) (*agentpb.AgentMessage, error) {
	type res struct {
		m   *agentpb.AgentMessage
		err error
	}
	ch := make(chan res, 1)
	go func() { m, err := stream.Recv(); ch <- res{m, err} }()
	select {
	case r := <-ch:
		return r.m, r.err
	case <-time.After(d):
		return nil, status.Error(codes.DeadlineExceeded, "hello timeout")
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return nil, ctx.Err()
	}
}

func (s *Server) agentConfig(m fleet.Machine) *agentpb.AgentConfig {
	return &agentpb.AgentConfig{LanIp: m.LanIP, HeartbeatIntervalS: uint32(s.cfg.HeartbeatInterval / time.Second),
		AllowCidrs: s.cfg.AllowCIDRs, DnsServers: s.cfg.DNSServers, BandwidthMbps: uint32(s.cfg.BandwidthMbps), DataSizeGib: uint32(s.cfg.DataSizeGiB)}
}

func (s *Server) checkLanIP(ctx context.Context, m fleet.Machine, ips []string) {
	if slices.Contains(ips, m.LanIP) {
		return
	}
	s.alertOnce(ctx, "warning", "lan_ip_mismatch", m.ID, fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 登记的内网 IP 与 Agent 上报的地址不一致", m.Serial))
}

func (s *Server) alertOnce(ctx context.Context, level, kind string, machineID int64, message string) {
	if err := s.d.Fleet.RaiseAlertOnce(ctx, s.d.Pool, fleet.Alert{Level: level, Kind: kind, MachineID: machineID, Message: message}); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("raise alert", "kind", kind, "machine", machineID, "err", err)
	}
}

func (s *Server) handleMessage(ctx context.Context, c *conn, msg *agentpb.AgentMessage) {
	switch m := msg.Msg.(type) {
	case *agentpb.AgentMessage_State:
		s.handleReport(ctx, c, m.State)
	case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Heartbeat:
		s.handleHeartbeat(ctx, c, m.Heartbeat)
	case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Result:
		s.handleResult(ctx, c, m.Result)
	case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Hello:
		s.d.Log.Warn("duplicate hello ignored", "machine", c.machineID)
	}
}

// handleReport：先存原文，再对账，再按 running 列表恢复计时；处理完首个报告后才允许投递。
func (s *Server) handleReport(ctx context.Context, c *conn, r *agentpb.StateReport) {
	rep := toStateReport(r)
	if err := s.d.Fleet.RecordStateReport(ctx, c.machineID, rep); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("record state report", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
	}
	if err := s.d.Rental.Reconcile(ctx, c.machineID, rep); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("reconcile", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
	}
	if _, err := s.d.Rental.ResumeMachine(ctx, c.machineID, rep.RunningIDs()); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("resume machine", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
	}
	if c.ready.CompareAndSwap(false, true) {
		c.readyTick.Store(s.ticks.Load())
	}
	c.reports.Add(1)
}

func (s *Server) handleHeartbeat(ctx context.Context, c *conn, h *agentpb.Heartbeat) {
	if _, err := s.d.Fleet.Heartbeat(ctx, c.machineID); err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("heartbeat", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
	}
	m := h.GetMetrics()
	if m != nil {
		if doc, err := metricsJSON(m); err == nil {
			if err := s.d.Fleet.RecordMetrics(ctx, c.machineID, "", doc); err != nil {
				s.d.Log.Error("record metrics", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
			}
		}
		if m.GetRootDiskTotal() > 0 && float64(m.GetRootDiskUsed())/float64(m.GetRootDiskTotal()) > 0.9 {
			s.alertOnce(ctx, "warning", "root_disk_high", c.machineID, fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s 根分区使用率超过 90%%", c.serial))
		}
		if t := m.GetGpuTempC(); t > 90 && t < 1000 {
			s.alertOnce(ctx, "warning", "gpu_temp_high", c.machineID, fmt.Sprintf("机器 %s GPU 温度超过 90°C", c.serial))
		}
	}
	c.heartbeats.Add(1)
}
```

（`handleHeartbeat` 以空版本调用 `RecordMetrics`：Task 1 的实现对空版本保留旧值。）

```go
func (s *Server) handleResult(ctx context.Context, c *conn, r *agentpb.CommandResult) {
	// 先完成指令再从已推送集合移除：反过来会让同一时刻的 tick 把仍为 sent 的指令再推一次并刷新 sent_at
	defer c.unpush(r.GetCommandId())
	err := s.d.Rental.HandleCommandResult(ctx, r.GetCommandId(), r.GetOk(), r.GetMessage())
	if errors.Is(err, apperr.ErrNotFound) {
		s.d.Log.Warn("result for unknown command ignored", "machine", c.machineID, "command", r.GetCommandId())
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("handle command result", "machine", c.machineID, "command", r.GetCommandId(), "err", err)
	}
}
```

```go
package agentgw_test

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/catalog"
	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

// place 下单并返回实例 id 与 create 指令 id。
func (e *env) place(mid int64) (string, string) {
	e.t.Helper()
	uid := dbtest.CreateUser(e.t, e.pool, "1360000"+itoa(mid), true)
	dbtest.Fund(e.t, e.pool, uid, 1_000_000)
	_, inst, err := e.rental.Place(e.ctx, rental.PlaceRequest{UserID: uid, ProductID: dbtest.ProductID(e.t, e.pool), ImageID: dbtest.ImageID(e.t, e.pool), Duration: catalog.D1d})
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, mid)
	return inst.ID, cmds[0].ID
}

func itoa(n int64) string { return fmt.Sprintf("%04d", n) }

func (e *env) command(id string) fleet.Command {
	e.t.Helper()
	var instanceID string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT instance_id FROM agent_commands WHERE id = $1`, id).Scan(&instanceID)
	cmds, err := e.fleet.CommandsForInstance(e.ctx, instanceID, 50)
	if err != nil {
		e.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, c := range cmds {
		if c.ID == id {
			return c
		}
	}
	e.t.Fatalf("command %s not found", id)
	return fleet.Command{}
}

func TestDeliveryWaitsForFirstStateReport(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	instID, cmdID := e.place(mid)
	a := e.connect(e.enroll(mid))
	a.hello("10.0.0.10")
	if cfg := a.next(time.Second); cfg.GetConfig() == nil {
		t.Fatal("no config")
	}
	if m := a.next(6 * tick); m != nil { // 没有 StateReport 之前不投递
		t.Fatalf("delivered before report: %v", m)
	}
	if c := e.command(cmdID); c.Status != "pending" {
		t.Fatalf("status before report = %s", c.Status)
	}
	a.report()
	m := a.next(2 * time.Second)
	cmd := m.GetCommand()
	if cmd == nil || cmd.GetId() != cmdID || cmd.GetInstanceId() != instID {
		t.Fatalf("delivered = %v", m)
	}
	cr := cmd.GetCreate()
	if cr.GetImageRef() == "" || cr.GetUpstreamPubkey() != "ssh-ed25519 AAAAtest" || cr.GetBandwidthMbps() != 200 || cr.GetSshPort() != 2222 || cr.GetDataSizeGib() != 2048 {
		t.Fatalf("create body = %v", cr)
	}
	c := e.command(cmdID)
	if c.Status != "sent" || c.Attempts != 1 || c.SentAt == nil {
		t.Fatalf("claimed = %+v", c)
	}
	if _, ok := e.srv.DeliveryTick(cmdID); !ok {
		t.Fatal("delivery tick not recorded")
	}
	// 结果 → 实例 running
	a.result(cmdID, true, "ok")
	e.waitFor("running", func() bool { inst, _ := e.rental.Get(e.ctx, instID); return inst.State == rental.StateRunning })
	if m := a.next(4 * tick); m != nil {
		t.Fatalf("nothing else should be delivered: %v", m)
	}
}

func TestSentCommandsRepushedOnReconnect(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	_, cmdID := e.place(mid)
	creds := e.enroll(mid)
	a := e.up(creds, mid)
	if m := a.next(2 * time.Second); m.GetCommand().GetId() != cmdID {
		t.Fatalf("first delivery = %v", m)
	}
	first := e.command(cmdID)
	if m := a.next(4 * tick); m != nil {
		t.Fatalf("must not re-push to the same stream: %v", m)
	}
	_ = a.stream.CloseSend()
	e.waitFor("disconnected", func() bool { return !e.srv.Connected(mid) })
	e.clk.Advance(time.Minute)
	b := e.up(creds, mid)
	if m := b.next(2 * time.Second); m.GetCommand().GetId() != cmdID {
		t.Fatalf("re-push after reconnect = %v", m)
	}
	again := e.command(cmdID)
	if again.Attempts != 1 || !again.SentAt.After(*first.SentAt) || again.Status != "sent" {
		t.Fatalf("after re-push = %+v (first %+v)", again, first)
	}
}

func TestOwnershipMismatchAbortsStreamAndStopsDelivery(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	a := e.up(e.enroll(mid), mid)
	// 模拟另一副本接管：数据库归属换成别的连接 id
	if err := e.fleet.AttachAgentConn(e.ctx, mid, "other-replica"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, cmdID := e.place(mid)
	if err := a.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Aborted {
		t.Fatalf("stream = %v", err)
	}
	if c := e.command(cmdID); c.Status != "pending" {
		t.Fatalf("command must stay pending for the other replica, got %s", c.Status)
	}
	m, _ := e.fleet.Get(e.ctx, e.pool, mid)
	if !m.Connected { // 归属是别人的，本副本断流时不能清
		t.Fatal("other replica's conn id was cleared")
	}
}

func TestRevokeAbortsStream(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	a := e.up(e.enroll(mid), mid)
	if err := e.fleet.RevokeEnrollment(e.ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, mid); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := a.waitErr(2 * time.Second); status.Code(err) != codes.Aborted {
		t.Fatalf("stream = %v", err)
	}
	e.waitFor("conn cleared", func() bool { return !e.srv.Connected(mid) })
}

func TestLateResultIgnoredAndPullDelivered(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	mid := e.machine("SN-001")
	instID, cmdID := e.place(mid)
	a := e.up(e.enroll(mid), mid)
	if m := a.next(2 * time.Second); m.GetCommand().GetId() != cmdID {
		t.Fatal("not delivered")
	}
	// 平台先把 create 判超时
	if err := e.rental.HandleCommandResult(e.ctx, cmdID, false, "timeout after 15m0s"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a.result(cmdID, true, "late")
	before := e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid)
	a.heartbeat(&agentpb.Metrics{})
	e.waitFor("hb", func() bool { return e.srv.HeartbeatCount(mid) > before })
	if inst, _ := e.rental.Get(e.ctx, instID); inst.State != rental.StateFailed {
		t.Fatalf("late ack revived instance: %s", inst.State)
	}
	// 机器已 faulty 但仍在线：pull_image 广播照常投递并完成
	if _, err := e.fleet.BroadcastPullImage(e.ctx, "nvcr.io/x/y:2"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m := a.next(2 * time.Second)
	if m.GetCommand().GetPull().GetImageRef() != "nvcr.io/x/y:2" || m.GetCommand().GetInstanceId() != "" {
		t.Fatalf("pull = %v", m)
	}
	a.result(m.GetCommand().GetId(), true, "pulled")
	e.waitFor("pull done", func() bool {
		var st string
		_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT status FROM agent_commands WHERE id = $1`, m.GetCommand().GetId()).Scan(&st)
		return st == "done"
	})
}

func TestTwoMachinesGetOnlyTheirOwnCommands(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	m1, m2 := e.machine("SN-001"), e.machine("SN-002")
	a1 := e.up(e.enroll(m1), m1)
	a2 := e.up(e.enroll(m2), m2)
	_, c1 := e.place(m1)
	_, c2 := e.place(m2)
	got1, got2 := a1.next(2*time.Second).GetCommand().GetId(), a2.next(2*time.Second).GetCommand().GetId()
	if got1 != c1 || got2 != c2 {
		t.Fatalf("routing: %s/%s want %s/%s", got1, got2, c1, c2)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `convert.go` 的 `toProtoCommand`**

```go
func toProtoCommand(cmd fleet.Command, cfg Config) (*agentpb.Command, error) {
	out := &agentpb.Command{Id: cmd.ID, InstanceId: cmd.InstanceID}
	switch cmd.Type {
	case fleet.CmdCreateInstance:
		var p fleet.CreateInstancePayload
		if err := json.Unmarshal(cmd.Payload, &p); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out.Body = &agentpb.Command_Create{Create: &agentpb.CreateInstance{ImageRef: p.ImageRef, UpstreamPubkey: cfg.UpstreamPubKey,
			BandwidthMbps: uint32(cfg.BandwidthMbps), SshPort: 2222, DataSizeGib: uint32(cfg.DataSizeGiB)}}
	case fleet.CmdStartInstance:
		out.Body = &agentpb.Command_Start{Start: &agentpb.StartInstance{}}
	case fleet.CmdStopInstance:
		out.Body = &agentpb.Command_Stop{Stop: &agentpb.StopInstance{}}
	case fleet.CmdWipeInstance:
		out.Body = &agentpb.Command_Wipe{Wipe: &agentpb.WipeInstance{}}
	case fleet.CmdPullImage:
		var p fleet.PullImagePayload
		if err := json.Unmarshal(cmd.Payload, &p); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		out.Body = &agentpb.Command_Pull{Pull: &agentpb.PullImage{ImageRef: p.ImageRef}}
	default:
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown command type %q", cmd.Type)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `deliver.go`（整体替换）**

```go
package agentgw

import (
	"context"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc/codes"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/status"

	"spark-cloud/internal/fleet"
)

func (s *Server) runTicks(ctx context.Context) {
	t := time.NewTicker(s.cfg.Tick)
	defer t.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-t.C:
			s.TickNow(ctx)
		}
	}
}

// TickNow 执行一次投递 tick（设计 §3.1）：
//  1. 核对本副本每条流的归属（agent_conn_id）与指纹，不符即断流（跨副本顶替、重新接入、撤销接入）；
//  2. 对已处理完首个 StateReport 的流原子认领 pending 指令并推送；
//  3. 对这些流补推 sent 且不在已推送集合里的指令，并刷新 sent_at。
func (s *Server) TickNow(ctx context.Context) {
	n := s.ticks.Add(1)
	conns := s.conns.snapshot()
	if len(conns) == 0 {
		return
	}
	ids := make([]int64, 0, len(conns))
	for _, c := range conns {
		ids = append(ids, c.machineID)
	}
	owners, err := s.d.Fleet.ConnOwners(ctx, ids)
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("tick: conn owners", "err", err)
		return
	}
	var ready []*conn
	var readyIDs []string
	for _, c := range conns {
		o := owners[c.machineID]
		if o.ConnID != c.id || o.Fingerprint != c.fingerprint {
			c.abort(status.Error(codes.Aborted, "connection superseded or enrollment revoked"))
			continue
		}
		if c.ready.Load() {
			ready = append(ready, c)
			readyIDs = append(readyIDs, c.id)
		}
	}
	claimed, err := s.d.Fleet.ClaimPending(ctx, readyIDs)
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("tick: claim", "err", err)
		return
	}
	for _, cmd := range claimed {
		c := s.conns.byID(cmd.ConnID)
		if c == nil || c.machineID != cmd.MachineID {
			continue // 认领期间被顶掉：保持 sent，由接管的副本补推
		}
		s.push(ctx, c, cmd.Command, n)
	}
	for _, c := range ready {
		sent, err := s.d.Fleet.SentCommands(ctx, c.machineID)
		if err != nil {
			s.d.Log.Error("tick: sent commands", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
			continue
		}
		var touched, sentIDs []string
		for _, cmd := range sent {
			sentIDs = append(sentIDs, cmd.ID)
			if c.wasPushed(cmd.ID) {
				continue
			}
			if s.push(ctx, c, cmd, n) {
				touched = append(touched, cmd.ID)
			}
		}
		if err := s.d.Fleet.TouchSent(ctx, touched); err != nil {
			s.d.Log.Error("tick: touch sent", "machine", c.machineID, "err", err)
		}
		c.retainPushed(sentIDs) // 被取消 / 超时的指令从已推送集合里清掉
	}
}

// push 把指令推给流；发送失败则断流（指令保持 sent，重连后补推）。
func (s *Server) push(ctx context.Context, c *conn, cmd fleet.Command, tick int64) bool {
	msg, err := toProtoCommand(cmd, s.cfg)
	if err != nil {
		s.d.Log.Error("tick: convert command", "command", cmd.ID, "err", err)
		return false
	}
	if err := c.send(&agentpb.PlatformMessage{Msg: &agentpb.PlatformMessage_Command{Command: msg}}); err != nil {
		c.abort(err)
		return false
	}
	c.markPushed(cmd.ID)
	s.deliveries.Store(cmd.ID, tick)
	return true
}

// DeliveryTick 返回指令最近一次被本副本推送时的 tick 号。
func (s *Server) DeliveryTick(cmdID string) (int64, bool) {
	v, ok := s.deliveries.Load(cmdID)
	if !ok {
		return 0, false
	}
	return v.(int64), true
}
```

### Task 11: Agent 基础：`cmdrun`（命令执行 / 录制）、`state`（状态文件）、`metrics`（指标采集）

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/agent/cmdrun/{runner.go,exec.go,recorder.go}`、`internal/agent/cmdrun/cmdrun_test.go`
- Create: `internal/agent/state/store.go`、`internal/agent/state/export_test.go`（`func SetMaxResultsForTest(n int) (restore func())`，改包级变量 `maxResults`）、`internal/agent/state/store_test.go`
- Create: `internal/agent/metrics/metrics.go`、`internal/agent/metrics/metrics_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces（Task 12～17 全部依赖）：
  - `cmdrun.Runner`：`Run(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) (stdout []byte, err error)`。
  - `cmdrun.Exec{}`：用 `os/exec`；非零退出返回 `*cmdrun.CmdError{Cmd string; ExitCode int; Stderr string}`，`Error()` = `"<name arg...>: exit <code>: <stderr 末尾 ≤500 字节>"`；找不到命令也包成 `CmdError`（ExitCode -1）。
  - `cmdrun.Recorder`：`Calls []string`（每次调用的 `name + " " + strings.Join(args, " ")`）、`Stub(cmdPrefix string, out string, err error)`（按命令行前缀匹配脚本化输出，后注册的优先）、`Reset()`、`Lines() string`（`Calls` 用 `\n` 连接，供快照断言）。未命中 stub 的调用返回 `("", nil)`。
  - `cmdrun.Line(name string, args ...string) string`（组命令行，Recorder 与错误信息共用）。
  - `state.Result{OK bool; Message string; Sent bool}`、`state.Instance{ID, ImageRef, LanIP string; Desired string("running"|"stopped"); SSHPort, BandwidthMbps, DataSizeGiB int}`。
  - `state.Open(path string) (*Store, error)`（不存在 → 空）；`(*Store).Result(id) (Result, bool)`、`PutResult(id string, r Result) error`（保留最近 1000 条——包级变量 `maxResults`，测试通过 `export_test.go` 的 `SetMaxResultsForTest(n)` 调小；先写临时文件 fsync 再 rename）、`MarkSent(id) error`、`UnsentResults() []ResultEntry{ID string; Result}`、`Instance(id) (Instance, bool)`、`PutInstance(Instance) error`、`DeleteInstance(id) error`、`Instances() []Instance`（按 ID 排序）。
  - `metrics.Collector{Run cmdrun.Runner; ReadFile func(string) ([]byte, error)}`；`(*Collector).Collect(ctx, dataMount string) Metrics`（任何一项失败都置 0，不返回错误）；`metrics.Metrics{GPUUtil, GPUTempC, GPUPowerW float64; MemTotal, MemUsed, RootDiskTotal, RootDiskUsed, DataDiskUsed uint64; Load1 float64}`；纯函数 `ParseNvidiaSMI(out string) (util, temp, power float64)`、`ParseMeminfo(b []byte) (total, used uint64)`、`ParseDF(out string) (total, used uint64)`、`ParseLoadavg(b []byte) float64`。
  - 采集命令：`nvidia-smi --query-gpu=utilization.gpu,temperature.gpu,power.draw --format=csv,noheader,nounits`、`df -B1 --output=size,used /`、`df -B1 --output=size,used <dataMount>`（`dataMount` 为空时跳过）、`/proc/meminfo`、`/proc/loadavg`。

`internal/agent/cmdrun/cmdrun_test.go`：

```go
package cmdrun_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/cmdrun"
)

func TestExecCapturesStdoutAndStderrTail(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	out, err := cmdrun.Exec{}.Run(ctx, "sh", "-c", "echo hello; echo oops >&2; exit 3")
	var ce *cmdrun.CmdError
	if !errors.As(err, &ce) || ce.ExitCode != 3 || strings.TrimSpace(ce.Stderr) != "oops" || string(out) != "hello\n" {
		t.Fatalf("out=%q err=%v", out, err)
	}
	if ce.Error() != "sh -c echo hello; echo oops >&2; exit 3: exit 3: oops\n" {
		t.Fatalf("message = %q", ce.Error())
	}
	long := strings.Repeat("x", 600)
	_, err = cmdrun.Exec{}.Run(ctx, "sh", "-c", "echo "+long+" >&2; exit 1")
	if !errors.As(err, &ce) || len(ce.Stderr) != 500 || !strings.HasSuffix(ce.Error(), "x\n") { // 末尾 500 字节（含换行）
		t.Fatalf("tail = %d", len(ce.Stderr))
	}
	if _, err := cmdrun.Exec{}.Run(ctx, "definitely-not-a-command-xyz"); !errors.As(err, &ce) || ce.ExitCode != -1 {
		t.Fatalf("missing command = %v", err)
	}
	if out, err := cmdrun.Exec{}.Run(ctx, "sh", "-c", "exit 0"); err != nil || len(out) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("ok = %q %v", out, err)
	}
}

func TestRecorderStubsAndSnapshots(t *testing.T) {
	r := &cmdrun.Recorder{}
	r.Stub("docker inspect", `{"Id":"x"}`, nil)
	r.Stub("docker inspect --type container missing", "", &cmdrun.CmdError{Cmd: "docker inspect", ExitCode: 1, Stderr: "No such object"})
	out, err := r.Run(context.Background(), "docker", "inspect", "--type", "container", "ins-1")
	if err != nil || string(out) != `{"Id":"x"}` {
		t.Fatalf("stub = %q %v", out, err)
	}
	if _, err := r.Run(context.Background(), "docker", "inspect", "--type", "container", "missing"); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("more specific stub must win")
	}
	if out, err := r.Run(context.Background(), "tc", "qdisc", "show"); err != nil || len(out) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("unstubbed = %q %v", out, err)
	}
	if r.Lines() != "docker inspect --type container ins-1\ndocker inspect --type container missing\ntc qdisc show" {
		t.Fatalf("lines = %q", r.Lines())
	}
	r.Reset()
	if len(r.Calls) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("reset")
	}
}
```

```go
package state_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"testing"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"
)

func TestStoreResultsInstancesAndReopen(t *testing.T) {
	path := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "state.json")
	s, err := state.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, ok := s.Result("c1"); ok {
		t.Fatal("empty store has results")
	}
	if err := s.PutResult("c1", state.Result{OK: false, Message: "boom"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.PutInstance(state.Instance{ID: "ins-aaaaaaaa", Desired: "running", ImageRef: "img:1", SSHPort: 2222, BandwidthMbps: 200, DataSizeGiB: 2048, LanIP: "10.0.0.10"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if un := s.UnsentResults(); len(un) != 1 || un[0].ID != "c1" || un[0].Message != "boom" {
		t.Fatalf("unsent = %+v", un)
	}
	if err := s.MarkSent("c1"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// 重新打开读到同样内容
	s2, err := state.Open(path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if r, ok := s2.Result("c1"); !ok || r.Message != "boom" || !r.Sent {
		t.Fatalf("reopened result = %+v %v", r, ok)
	}
	if inst, ok := s2.Instance("ins-aaaaaaaa"); !ok || inst.Desired != "running" || inst.DataSizeGiB != 2048 {
		t.Fatalf("reopened instance = %+v", inst)
	}
	if len(s2.UnsentResults()) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("sent flag lost")
	}
	if err := s2.DeleteInstance("ins-aaaaaaaa"); err != nil || len(s2.Instances()) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("delete = %v %v", err, s2.Instances())
	}
	// 只保留最近 N 条结果（生产 N=1000；测试把 N 调成 5，macOS 上每次 fsync 很慢），最早的被淘汰
	restore := state.SetMaxResultsForTest(5)
	defer restore()
	for i := 0; i < 8; i++ {
		if err := s2.PutResult(fmt.Sprintf("r%d", i), state.Result{OK: true}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if _, ok := s2.Result("r2"); ok {
		t.Fatal("oldest result not evicted")
	}
	if _, ok := s2.Result("r3"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("5th newest result must remain")
	}
	if _, ok := s2.Result("r7"); !ok {
		t.Fatal("newest result missing")
	}
	// 没有残留临时文件
	entries, _ := os.ReadDir(filepath.Dir(path))
	if len(entries) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("dir = %v", entries)
	}
}
```

```go
package metrics_test

import (

func TestParseNvidiaSMIHandlesNA(t *testing.T) {
	u, tc, p := metrics.ParseNvidiaSMI("12, 45, 30.50\n")
	if u != 12 || tc != 45 || p != 30.5 {
		t.Fatalf("parsed = %v %v %v", u, tc, p)
	}
	u, tc, p = metrics.ParseNvidiaSMI("[N/A], 51, [N/A]\n")
	if u != 0 || tc != 51 || p != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("N/A = %v %v %v", u, tc, p)
	}
	if u, tc, p = metrics.ParseNvidiaSMI(""); u != 0 || tc != 0 || p != 0 {
		t.Fatal("empty")
	}
}

func TestParseMeminfoDFLoadavg(t *testing.T) {
	total, used := metrics.ParseMeminfo([]byte("MemTotal:       131072000 kB\nMemFree:  1000 kB\nMemAvailable:   100000000 kB\n"))
	if total != 131072000*1024 || used != (131072000-100000000)*1024 {
		t.Fatalf("meminfo = %d %d", total, used)
	}
	dt, du := metrics.ParseDF("        1B-blocks       Used\n4000787030016 1234567\n")
	if dt != 4000787030016 || du != 1234567 {
		t.Fatalf("df = %d %d", dt, du)
	}
	if l := metrics.ParseLoadavg([]byte("0.52 0.40 0.30 1/900 1234\n")); l != 0.52 {
		t.Fatalf("load = %v", l)
	}
}

func TestCollectNeverFails(t *testing.T) {
	r := &cmdrun.Recorder{}
	r.Stub("nvidia-smi", "", errors.New("no gpu"))
	r.Stub("df -B1 --output=size,used /", "1B-blocks Used\n1000 900\n", nil)
	r.Stub("df -B1 --output=size,used /var/lib/spark-agent/mnt/ins-a", "1B-blocks Used\n2000 5\n", nil)
	files := map[string]string{"/proc/meminfo": "MemTotal: 100 kB\nMemAvailable: 40 kB\n", "/proc/loadavg": "1.25 0 0 1/1 1\n"}
	c := &metrics.Collector{Run: r, ReadFile: func(p string) ([]byte, error) {
		if s, ok := files[p]; ok {
			return []byte(s), nil
		}
		return nil, errors.New("missing")
	}}
	m := c.Collect(context.Background(), "/var/lib/spark-agent/mnt/ins-a")
	if m.GPUUtil != 0 || m.RootDiskTotal != 1000 || m.RootDiskUsed != 900 || m.DataDiskUsed != 5 || m.MemTotal != 100*1024 || m.MemUsed != 60*1024 || m.Load1 != 1.25 {
		t.Fatalf("metrics = %+v", m)
	}
	if m := c.Collect(context.Background(), ""); m.DataDiskUsed != 0 {
		t.Fatal("no data mount must give 0")
	}
	if r.Lines() != "nvidia-smi --query-gpu=utilization.gpu,temperature.gpu,power.draw --format=csv,noheader,nounits\ndf -B1 --output=size,used /\ndf -B1 --output=size,used /var/lib/spark-agent/mnt/ins-a\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=utilization.gpu,temperature.gpu,power.draw --format=csv,noheader,nounits\ndf -B1 --output=size,used /" {
		t.Fatalf("calls:\n%s", r.Lines())
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `cmdrun`**

`runner.go`：

```go
// Package cmdrun 封装 Agent 对系统命令的调用：生产用 Exec，测试用 Recorder 做命令快照。
package cmdrun

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

type Runner interface {
	// Run 执行命令并返回 stdout；非零退出返回 *CmdError。
	Run(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) ([]byte, error)
}

// Line 组出可读的命令行（不做 shell 转义，只用于日志、错误与快照）。
func Line(name string, args ...string) string {
	if len(args) == 0 {
		return name
	}
	return name + " " + strings.Join(args, " ")
}

const stderrTail = 500

type CmdError struct {
	Cmd      string
	ExitCode int
	Stderr   string // 末尾最多 500 字节
}

func (e *CmdError) Error() string { return fmt.Sprintf("%s: exit %d: %s", e.Cmd, e.ExitCode, e.Stderr) }

func tail(b []byte) string {
	if len(b) > stderrTail {
		b = b[len(b)-stderrTail:]
	}
	return string(b)
}
```

`exec.go`：

```go
package cmdrun

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"os/exec"
)

type Exec struct{}

func (Exec) Run(ctx context.Context, name string, args ...string) ([]byte, error) {
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, name, args...)
	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
	cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = &stdout, &stderr
	err := cmd.Run()
	if err == nil {
		return stdout.Bytes(), nil
	}
	code := -1
	var ee *exec.ExitError
	if errors.As(err, &ee) {
		code = ee.ExitCode()
	}
	msg := stderr.Bytes()
	if len(msg) == 0 {
		msg = []byte(err.Error())
	}
	return stdout.Bytes(), &CmdError{Cmd: Line(name, args...), ExitCode: code, Stderr: tail(msg)}
}
```

`recorder.go`：

```go
package cmdrun

import (
	"context"
	"strings"
	"sync"
)

type stub struct {
	prefix string
	out    string
	err    error
}

// Recorder 记录每次调用的命令行，并按前缀返回脚本化的输出；后注册的 stub 优先。
type Recorder struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	Calls []string
	stubs []stub
}

func (r *Recorder) Stub(cmdPrefix, out string, err error) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.stubs = append(r.stubs, stub{prefix: cmdPrefix, out: out, err: err})
}

func (r *Recorder) Run(_ context.Context, name string, args ...string) ([]byte, error) {
	line := Line(name, args...)
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.Calls = append(r.Calls, line)
	for i := len(r.stubs) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		if strings.HasPrefix(line, r.stubs[i].prefix) {
			return []byte(r.stubs[i].out), r.stubs[i].err
		}
	}
	return nil, nil
}

func (r *Recorder) Reset() {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.Calls = nil
}

func (r *Recorder) Lines() string {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return strings.Join(r.Calls, "\n")
}
```

`TestRecorderStubsAndSnapshots` 里「更具体的 stub 优先」靠注册顺序（后注册优先），测试已按此顺序注册。

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `state/store.go`**

```go
// Package state 是 Agent 的本地状态文件：已执行指令的结果（去重回放、补发）与实例期望状态（重启恢复）。
package state

// maxResults 由 export_test.go 的 SetMaxResultsForTest 在测试里调小（macOS 上每次 fsync 要 10～20ms，1000 条会跑十几秒）。

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sort"
	"sync"
)

var maxResults = 1000

type Result struct {
	OK      bool   `json:"ok"`
	Message string `json:"message"`
	Sent    bool   `json:"sent"`
}

type ResultEntry struct {
	ID string `json:"id"`
	Result
}

type Instance struct {
	ID            string `json:"id"`
	Desired       string `json:"desired"` // running / stopped
	ImageRef      string `json:"image_ref"`
	LanIP         string `json:"lan_ip"`
	SSHPort       int    `json:"ssh_port"`
	BandwidthMbps int    `json:"bandwidth_mbps"`
	DataSizeGiB   int    `json:"data_size_gib"`
}

type file struct {
	Results   []ResultEntry       `json:"results"` // 旧 → 新
	Instances map[string]Instance `json:"instances"`
}

type Store struct {
	path string
	mu   sync.Mutex
	f    file
}

func Open(path string) (*Store, error) {
	s := &Store{path: path, f: file{Instances: map[string]Instance{}}}
	raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
		return s, nil
	}
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &s.f); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if s.f.Instances == nil {
		s.f.Instances = map[string]Instance{}
	}
	return s, nil
}

// save 先写临时文件并 fsync，再 rename，保证状态文件要么旧要么新。
func (s *Store) save() error {
	b, err := json.MarshalIndent(s.f, "", " ")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(s.path), 0o700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tmp, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(s.path), ".state-*")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer os.Remove(tmp.Name()) //nolint:errcheck // rename 成功后不存在
	if _, err := tmp.Write(b); err != nil {
		tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := tmp.Sync(); err != nil {
		tmp.Close()
		return err
	}
	if err := tmp.Close(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.Chmod(tmp.Name(), 0o600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return os.Rename(tmp.Name(), s.path)
}

func (s *Store) Result(id string) (Result, bool) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	for i := len(s.f.Results) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		if s.f.Results[i].ID == id {
			return s.f.Results[i].Result, true
		}
	}
	return Result{}, false
}

func (s *Store) PutResult(id string, r Result) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	for i := range s.f.Results {
		if s.f.Results[i].ID == id {
			s.f.Results[i].Result = r
			return s.save()
		}
	}
	s.f.Results = append(s.f.Results, ResultEntry{ID: id, Result: r})
	if len(s.f.Results) > maxResults {
		s.f.Results = s.f.Results[len(s.f.Results)-maxResults:]
	}
	return s.save()
}

func (s *Store) MarkSent(id string) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	for i := range s.f.Results {
		if s.f.Results[i].ID == id {
			s.f.Results[i].Sent = true
			return s.save()
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (s *Store) UnsentResults() []ResultEntry {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var out []ResultEntry
	for _, r := range s.f.Results {
		if !r.Sent {
			out = append(out, r)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func (s *Store) Instance(id string) (Instance, bool) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	i, ok := s.f.Instances[id]
	return i, ok
}

func (s *Store) PutInstance(i Instance) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.f.Instances[i.ID] = i
	return s.save()
}

func (s *Store) DeleteInstance(id string) error {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	delete(s.f.Instances, id)
	return s.save()
}

func (s *Store) Instances() []Instance {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	out := make([]Instance, 0, len(s.f.Instances))
	for _, i := range s.f.Instances {
		out = append(out, i)
	}
	sort.Slice(out, func(a, b int) bool { return out[a].ID < out[b].ID })
	return out
}
```

```go
package state

// SetMaxResultsForTest 调小结果保留上限，返回恢复函数。
func SetMaxResultsForTest(n int) (restore func()) {
	old := maxResults
	maxResults = n
	return func() { maxResults = old }
}
```
