### Task 16: Agent 客户端：连接 / 重连 / Hello / 心跳 / 上报 / 结果补发、`enroll` 客户端（CA 指纹固定）、退避

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/agent/client/client.go`、`internal/agent/client/enroll.go`、`internal/agent/client/backoff.go`
- Test: `internal/agent/client/client_test.go`（进程内假网关只实现协议，不碰数据库）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 `agentpb`、Task 11 `metrics` / `state`、Task 15 `executor`（`Submit` / `RunPending` / `Results` / `Report` / `SetConfig` / `StateStore`）、Task 3 `pki`（测试里签假网关证书）。
- Produces（Task 17 的 fakeagent 与 cmd/spark-agent 直接使用）：
  - `client.Config{Addr string; TLS *tls.Config; Version, Hostname string; LocalIPs func() []string; Stepped bool; HeartbeatInterval, ReportInterval time.Duration; Backoff Backoff; NoReconnect bool}`（`HeartbeatInterval` 零值 10s 且此时采用平台下发的 `heartbeat_interval_s`，显式指定则不被覆盖；`ReportInterval` 零值 60s；`NoReconnect` 为真时会话结束 `Run` 直接返回）
  - `client.Backoff{Min, Max time.Duration}`（零值 1s / 60s）；`func (b Backoff) Next(attempt int) time.Duration`（指数、上限、±20% 抖动）
  - `client.New(cfg Config, ex *executor.Executor, collect func(ctx) metrics.Metrics, log *slog.Logger) *Client`
  - `(*Client).Run(ctx) error`（重连循环直到 ctx 结束，`NoReconnect` 时一次会话后返回；每次建流：Hello → StateReport → 等 AgentConfig（应用到 executor）→ 补发未发送结果 → 循环；自动模式定时心跳（每次心跳后再补发 `UnsentResults()`）/ 定时上报；执行器结果到达时发送结果 + StateReport）
  - `(*Client).Step(ctx, override *state.Result) (int, error)`（步进：`RunPending` → 发送结果 → 发 StateReport）、`SendHeartbeat(ctx) error`、`SendStateReport(ctx) error`、`Connected() bool`、`ConfigReceived() bool`、`WaitConnected(ctx) error`（直到收到 AgentConfig）、`Received(cmdID string) bool`（该指令已到达本客户端）。
  - `client.Enroll(ctx, addr, token, caSHA256 string, key *ecdsa.PrivateKey) (certDER, caDER []byte, err error)`：TLS 只信任指纹等于 `caSHA256` 的 CA（`InsecureSkipVerify + VerifyPeerCertificate` 自行校验链）。
  - `client.TLSFor(key *ecdsa.PrivateKey, certDER, caDER []byte, serverName string) (*tls.Config, error)`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败的测试**

`internal/agent/client/client_test.go`（进程内假网关只实现协议，不碰数据库）：

```go
package client_test

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/client"
	diskfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/disk/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/executor"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"
	netfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/netfilter/fake"
	rtfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/runtime/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/secretbox"
)

func TestMain(m *testing.M) { dbtest.Main(m) }

// fakeGateway 记录收到的消息，并允许测试向流推送指令。
type fakeGateway struct {
	agentpb.UnimplementedAgentGatewayServer
	mu       sync.Mutex
	received []*agentpb.AgentMessage
	streams  []agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectServer
	enrolls  int
	ca       *pki.CA
	drop     chan struct{} // 关闭当前流
}

func (g *fakeGateway) Enroll(_ context.Context, req *agentpb.EnrollRequest) (*agentpb.EnrollResponse, error) {
	g.mu.Lock()
	g.enrolls++
	g.mu.Unlock()
	pub, err := pki.ParseCSR(req.CsrDer)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	der, err := g.ca.IssueMachine(pub, "SN-TEST", time.Now())
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return &agentpb.EnrollResponse{CertDer: der, CaDer: g.ca.CertDER()}, nil
}

func (g *fakeGateway) Connect(stream agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectServer) error {
	g.mu.Lock()
	g.streams = append(g.streams, stream)
	g.drop = make(chan struct{})
	drop := g.drop
	g.mu.Unlock()
	_ = stream.Send(&agentpb.PlatformMessage{Msg: &agentpb.PlatformMessage_Config{Config: &agentpb.AgentConfig{LanIp: "10.0.0.10", HeartbeatIntervalS: 1, BandwidthMbps: 200, DataSizeGib: 1}}})
	errc := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		for {
			m, err := stream.Recv()
			if err != nil {
				errc <- err
				return
			}
			g.mu.Lock()
			g.received = append(g.received, m)
			g.mu.Unlock()
		}
	}()
	select {
	case <-drop:
		return nil
	case err := <-errc:
		if err == io.EOF {
			return nil
		}
		return err
	}
}

func (g *fakeGateway) msgs() []*agentpb.AgentMessage {
	g.mu.Lock()
	defer g.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]*agentpb.AgentMessage(nil), g.received...)
}

func (g *fakeGateway) push(cmd *agentpb.Command) {
	g.mu.Lock()
	s := g.streams[len(g.streams)-1]
	g.mu.Unlock()
	_ = s.Send(&agentpb.PlatformMessage{Msg: &agentpb.PlatformMessage_Command{Command: cmd}})
}

type harness struct {
	t    *testing.T
	gw   *fakeGateway
	addr string
	ca   *pki.CA
	key  *ecdsa.PrivateKey
}

func newHarness(t *testing.T) *harness {
	t.Helper()
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	box, _ := secretbox.New(dbtest.Key)
	ca, err := pki.EnsureCA(context.Background(), pool, box, clock.Real{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	certs, _ := pki.NewServerCerts(ca, []string{"127.0.0.1"}, clock.Real{})
	gw := &fakeGateway{ca: ca}
	gs := grpc.NewServer(grpc.Creds(credentials.NewTLS(&tls.Config{MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS13, ClientAuth: tls.VerifyClientCertIfGiven, ClientCAs: ca.Pool(), GetCertificate: certs.GetCertificate})))
	agentpb.RegisterAgentGatewayServer(gs, gw)
	lis, _ := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	go gs.Serve(lis) //nolint:errcheck
	t.Cleanup(gs.Stop)
	key, _ := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	return &harness{t: t, gw: gw, addr: lis.Addr().String(), ca: ca, key: key}
}

func (h *harness) agent(stepped bool) (*client.Client, *executor.Executor, *rtfake.Runtime) {
	h.t.Helper()
	certDER, caDER, err := client.Enroll(context.Background(), h.addr, "spk1_x", h.ca.Fingerprint(), h.key)
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tc, err := client.TLSFor(h.key, certDER, caDER, "127.0.0.1")
	if err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	dir := h.t.TempDir()
	st, _ := state.Open(filepath.Join(dir, "state.json"))
	rt := rtfake.New()
	ex := executor.New(executor.Deps{Runtime: rt, Disk: diskfake.New(4000 << 30), Net: netfake.New(), State: st, StateDir: dir,
		LocalIPs: func() []string { return []string{"10.0.0.10"} }, MemTotal: func() uint64 { return 128 << 30 }}, stepped)
	c := client.New(client.Config{Addr: h.addr, TLS: tc, Version: "test", Hostname: "h", LocalIPs: func() []string { return []string{"10.0.0.10"} },
		Stepped: stepped, HeartbeatInterval: 50 * time.Millisecond, ReportInterval: 80 * time.Millisecond, Backoff: client.Backoff{Min: 10 * time.Millisecond, Max: 50 * time.Millisecond}},
		ex, func(context.Context) metrics.Metrics { return metrics.Metrics{Load1: 1} }, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)))
	return c, ex, rt
}

func waitFor(t *testing.T, what string, cond func() bool) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if cond() {
			return
		}
		time.Sleep(5 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	t.Fatalf("timeout: %s", what)
}

func kinds(ms []*agentpb.AgentMessage) []string {
	var out []string
	for _, m := range ms {
		switch m.Msg.(type) {
		case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Hello:
			out = append(out, "hello")
		case *agentpb.AgentMessage_State:
			out = append(out, "state")
		case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Heartbeat:
			out = append(out, "heartbeat")
		case *agentpb.AgentMessage_Result:
			out = append(out, "result")
		}
	}
	return out
}

func TestEnrollPinsCAFingerprint(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	if _, _, err := client.Enroll(context.Background(), h.addr, "spk1_x", "deadbeef", h.key); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("wrong CA fingerprint accepted")
	}
	certDER, caDER, err := client.Enroll(context.Background(), h.addr, "spk1_x", h.ca.Fingerprint(), h.key)
	if err != nil || pki.Fingerprint(caDER) != h.ca.Fingerprint() {
		t.Fatalf("enroll = %v", err)
	}
	cert, _ := x509.ParseCertificate(certDER)
	if cert.Subject.CommonName != "SN-TEST" {
		t.Fatalf("cn = %s", cert.Subject.CommonName)
	}
}

func TestAutoModeHelloReportHeartbeatsAndResults(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	c, ex, rt := h.agent(false)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go c.Run(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
	go ex.Run(ctx) // 自动模式的消费 goroutine 在 Executor.Run 里启动，否则指令进队列后无人执行
	if err := c.WaitConnected(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	waitFor(t, "hello+state+heartbeat", func() bool { k := kinds(h.gw.msgs()); return len(k) >= 3 && k[0] == "hello" && k[1] == "state" })
	waitFor(t, "periodic heartbeat", func() bool {
		n := 0
		for _, k := range kinds(h.gw.msgs()) {
			if k == "heartbeat" {
				n++
			}
		}
		return n >= 2
	})
	h.gw.push(&agentpb.Command{Id: "cmd-1", InstanceId: "ins-aaaaaaaa", Body: &agentpb.Command_Create{Create: &agentpb.CreateInstance{ImageRef: "img:1", UpstreamPubkey: "k", BandwidthMbps: 200, SshPort: 2222, DataSizeGib: 1}}})
	waitFor(t, "result", func() bool {
		for _, m := range h.gw.msgs() {
			if r := m.GetResult(); r != nil && r.CommandId == "cmd-1" && r.Ok {
				return true
			}
		}
		return false
	})
	if !c.Received("cmd-1") || !rt.Containers()["ins-aaaaaaaa"].Running {
		t.Fatal("command not executed")
	}
	// 结果之后紧跟一份 StateReport，且含 running
	ms := h.gw.msgs()
	for i, m := range ms {
		if r := m.GetResult(); r != nil && r.CommandId == "cmd-1" {
			if i+1 >= len(ms) || ms[i+1].GetState() == nil || ms[i+1].GetState().Instances[0].Phase != "running" {
				t.Fatalf("no state report after result: %v", kinds(ms))
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestReconnectResendsUnsentResultsAfterHello(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	c, ex, _ := h.agent(true)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go c.Run(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
	if err := c.WaitConnected(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// 断开网关侧的流，再在断线期间执行指令：结果发送失败，重连后补发
	h.gw.push(&agentpb.Command{Id: "cmd-1", InstanceId: "ins-aaaaaaaa", Body: &agentpb.Command_Create{Create: &agentpb.CreateInstance{ImageRef: "img:1", DataSizeGib: 1}}})
	waitFor(t, "received", func() bool { return c.Received("cmd-1") })
	h.gw.mu.Lock()
	close(h.gw.drop)
	h.gw.mu.Unlock()
	waitFor(t, "disconnected", func() bool { return !c.Connected() })
	if res := ex.RunPending(ctx, nil); len(res) != 1 || !res[0].OK { // 断线期间执行，结果留在 state.json（未发送）
		t.Fatalf("executed %+v", res)
	}
	waitFor(t, "reconnected", func() bool { return c.Connected() })
	waitFor(t, "resend", func() bool {
		k := kinds(h.gw.msgs())
		// 第二条流：hello, state, result(补发), state
		idx := 0
		for i, x := range k {
			if x == "hello" {
				idx = i
			}
		}
		rest := k[idx:]
		return len(rest) >= 3 && rest[0] == "hello" && rest[1] == "state" && rest[2] == "result"
	})
}

func TestSteppedModeSendsNothingAutomatically(t *testing.T) {
	h := newHarness(t)
	c, _, _ := h.agent(true)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go c.Run(ctx) //nolint:errcheck
	if err := c.WaitConnected(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	time.Sleep(300 * time.Millisecond) // 远长于心跳与上报间隔
	if k := kinds(h.gw.msgs()); len(k) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("stepped agent sent %v", k)
	}
	if err := c.SendHeartbeat(ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h.gw.push(&agentpb.Command{Id: "cmd-1", InstanceId: "ins-aaaaaaaa", Body: &agentpb.Command_Create{Create: &agentpb.CreateInstance{ImageRef: "img:1", DataSizeGib: 1}}})
	waitFor(t, "received", func() bool { return c.Received("cmd-1") })
	n, err := c.Step(ctx, &state.Result{OK: false, Message: "simulated"})
	if err != nil || n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("step = %d %v", n, err)
	}
	waitFor(t, "result+state", func() bool { k := kinds(h.gw.msgs()); return len(k) == 5 && k[2] == "heartbeat" && k[3] == "result" && k[4] == "state" })
	if r := h.gw.msgs()[3].GetResult(); r.Ok || r.Message != "simulated" {
		t.Fatalf("result = %v", r)
	}
}

func TestBackoff(t *testing.T) {
	b := client.Backoff{}
	prev := time.Duration(0)
	for i := 0; i < 10; i++ {
		d := b.Next(i)
		if d < 800*time.Millisecond || d > 72*time.Second {
			t.Fatalf("attempt %d = %v", i, d)
		}
		if i >= 7 && d < 48*time.Second { // 达到上限 60s ± 20%
			t.Fatalf("attempt %d = %v, want near max", i, d)
		}
		prev = d
	}
	_ = prev
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `client/backoff.go` 与 `client/enroll.go`**

```go
package client

import (
	"math/rand/v2"
	"time"
)

// Backoff 指数退避：Min·2^attempt，封顶 Max，±20% 抖动。
type Backoff struct {
	Min, Max time.Duration
}

func (b Backoff) Next(attempt int) time.Duration {
	if b.Min <= 0 {
		b.Min = time.Second
	}
	if b.Max <= 0 {
		b.Max = 60 * time.Second
	}
	d := b.Min
	for i := 0; i < attempt && d < b.Max; i++ {
		d *= 2
	}
	if d > b.Max {
		d = b.Max
	}
	jitter := 1 + (rand.Float64()*0.4 - 0.2)
	return time.Duration(float64(d) * jitter)
}
```

```go
package client

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/tls"
	"crypto/x509"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"net"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
)

// pinnedCA 返回只信任指纹为 caSHA256 的 CA 的 TLS 配置：服务端证书链里必须带该 CA，且叶子由它签发并匹配主机名。
func pinnedCA(caSHA256, serverName string) *tls.Config {
	return &tls.Config{
		MinVersion:         tls.VersionTLS13,
		InsecureSkipVerify: true, //nolint:gosec // 由 VerifyPeerCertificate 按指纹校验
		VerifyPeerCertificate: func(raw [][]byte, _ [][]*x509.Certificate) error {
			if len(raw) < 2 {
				return errors.New("server did not present its CA certificate")
			}
			var leaf, ca *x509.Certificate
			for i, der := range raw {
				c, err := x509.ParseCertificate(der)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				if i == 0 {
					leaf = c
				}
				if pki.Fingerprint(der) == caSHA256 {
					ca = c
				}
			}
			if ca == nil {
				return errors.New("平台 CA 指纹不匹配 --ca-sha256")
			}
			pool := x509.NewCertPool()
			pool.AddCert(ca)
			_, err := leaf.Verify(x509.VerifyOptions{Roots: pool, DNSName: serverName, KeyUsages: []x509.ExtKeyUsage{x509.ExtKeyUsageServerAuth}})
			return err
		},
	}
}

// Enroll 用一次性令牌换取机器证书；私钥不出本机，只发送 CSR。
func Enroll(ctx context.Context, addr, token, caSHA256 string, key *ecdsa.PrivateKey) (certDER, caDER []byte, err error) {
	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(addr)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("--platform 必须是 host:port: %w", err)
	}
	csr, err := x509.CreateCertificateRequest(rand.Reader, &x509.CertificateRequest{}, key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	cc, err := grpc.NewClient(addr, grpc.WithTransportCredentials(credentials.NewTLS(pinnedCA(caSHA256, host))))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	defer cc.Close()
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	resp, err := agentpb.NewAgentGatewayClient(cc).Enroll(ctx, &agentpb.EnrollRequest{Token: token, CsrDer: csr})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if pki.Fingerprint(resp.GetCaDer()) != caSHA256 {
		return nil, nil, errors.New("平台返回的 CA 与 --ca-sha256 不符")
	}
	return resp.GetCertDer(), resp.GetCaDer(), nil
}

// TLSFor 构造 Connect 用的 mTLS 配置。
func TLSFor(key *ecdsa.PrivateKey, certDER, caDER []byte, serverName string) (*tls.Config, error) {
	ca, err := x509.ParseCertificate(caDER)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	pool := x509.NewCertPool()
	pool.AddCert(ca)
	return &tls.Config{MinVersion: tls.VersionTLS13, RootCAs: pool, ServerName: serverName,
		Certificates: []tls.Certificate{{Certificate: [][]byte{certDER}, PrivateKey: key}}}, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `client/client.go`**

```go
// Package client 是 spark-agent 与网关的连接层：重连、Hello、StateReport、心跳、指令接收与结果回报。
package client

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/tls"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"time"

	"google.golang.org/grpc"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/credentials"
	"google.golang.org/grpc/keepalive"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/executor"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agentpb"
)

type Config struct {
	Addr              string
	TLS               *tls.Config
	Version           string
	Hostname          string
	LocalIPs          func() []string
	Stepped           bool
	HeartbeatInterval time.Duration // 非零时为调用方显式指定，不被平台下发的 heartbeat_interval_s 覆盖
	ReportInterval    time.Duration
	Backoff           Backoff
	NoReconnect       bool // 会话结束后不重连；测试里模拟「被顶掉 / 被撤销就退出」的旧 Agent，避免与新 Agent 互相顶替
}

type Client struct {
	cfg     Config
	ex      *executor.Executor
	collect func(context.Context) metrics.Metrics
	log     *slog.Logger

	mu         sync.Mutex
	stream     agentpb.AgentGateway_ConnectClient
	sendMu     sync.Mutex
	received   map[string]bool
	connected  atomic.Bool
	configured atomic.Bool
	hbInterval time.Duration
	explicitHB bool // Config.HeartbeatInterval 由调用方指定，不被平台下发值覆盖
}

func New(cfg Config, ex *executor.Executor, collect func(context.Context) metrics.Metrics, log *slog.Logger) *Client {
	explicitHB := cfg.HeartbeatInterval > 0
	if cfg.HeartbeatInterval <= 0 {
		cfg.HeartbeatInterval = 10 * time.Second
	}
	if cfg.ReportInterval <= 0 {
		cfg.ReportInterval = 60 * time.Second
	}
	if log == nil {
		log = slog.Default()
	}
	return &Client{cfg: cfg, ex: ex, collect: collect, log: log, received: map[string]bool{}, hbInterval: cfg.HeartbeatInterval, explicitHB: explicitHB}
}

func (c *Client) Connected() bool      { return c.connected.Load() }
func (c *Client) ConfigReceived() bool { return c.configured.Load() }

func (c *Client) Received(id string) bool {
	c.mu.Lock()
	defer c.mu.Unlock()
	return c.received[id]
}

func (c *Client) WaitConnected(ctx context.Context) error {
	for !c.configured.Load() {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(5 * time.Millisecond):
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// Run 重连循环：每次会话结束后按退避重试；平台不可达期间不做任何自主停机。
func (c *Client) Run(ctx context.Context) error {
	attempt := 0
	for {
		start := time.Now()
		err := c.session(ctx)
		if ctx.Err() != nil {
			return nil
		}
		if c.cfg.NoReconnect {
			return err
		}
		if time.Since(start) > time.Minute {
			attempt = 0 // 稳定连接过一段时间，退避从头算
		}
		d := c.cfg.Backoff.Next(attempt)
		attempt++
		c.log.Warn("agent session ended, reconnecting", "err", err, "after", d)
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case <-time.After(d):
		}
	}
}

func (c *Client) session(ctx context.Context) error {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	defer cancel()
	cc, err := grpc.NewClient(c.cfg.Addr, grpc.WithTransportCredentials(credentials.NewTLS(c.cfg.TLS)),
		grpc.WithKeepaliveParams(keepalive.ClientParameters{Time: 30 * time.Second, Timeout: 10 * time.Second, PermitWithoutStream: true}))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer cc.Close()
	stream, err := agentpb.NewAgentGatewayClient(cc).Connect(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.mu.Lock()
	c.stream = stream
	c.mu.Unlock()
	defer func() {
		c.connected.Store(false)
		c.configured.Store(false)
		c.mu.Lock()
		c.stream = nil
		c.mu.Unlock()
	}()
	if err := c.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Hello{Hello: &agentpb.Hello{AgentVersion: c.cfg.Version, Hostname: c.cfg.Hostname, Ips: c.cfg.LocalIPs()}}}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := c.SendStateReport(ctx); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c.connected.Store(true)
	// 第一条平台消息必须是 AgentConfig
	first, err := stream.Recv()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	cfg := first.GetConfig()
	if cfg == nil {
		return errors.New("first platform message is not AgentConfig")
	}
	c.applyConfig(cfg)
	c.configured.Store(true)
	// Hello 之后补发断线期间没发出去的结果
	for _, r := range c.ex.StateStore().UnsentResults() {
		if err := c.sendResult(r.ID, r.Result); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	errc := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		for {
			msg, err := stream.Recv()
			if err != nil {
				errc <- err
				return
			}
			c.handle(msg)
		}
	}()
	hb := time.NewTicker(c.hbInterval)
	rep := time.NewTicker(c.cfg.ReportInterval)
	defer hb.Stop()
	defer rep.Stop()
	// 步进模式下结果由 Step 发送；这里不监听 Results()（nil 通道永远阻塞）
	var results <-chan executor.ResultMsg
	if !c.cfg.Stepped {
		results = c.ex.Results()
	}
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return nil
		case err := <-errc:
			return err
		case r := <-results:
			if err := c.sendResult(r.ID, state.Result{OK: r.OK, Message: r.Message}); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := c.SendStateReport(ctx); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		case <-hb.C:
			if c.cfg.Stepped {
				continue
			}
			if err := c.SendHeartbeat(ctx); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			// Results() 满时结果只落在 state.json（Sent=false）：每次心跳顺手补发，不必等到重连
			for _, r := range c.ex.StateStore().UnsentResults() {
				if err := c.sendResult(r.ID, r.Result); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		case <-rep.C:
			if c.cfg.Stepped {
				continue
			}
			if err := c.SendStateReport(ctx); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
}

func (c *Client) applyConfig(cfg *agentpb.AgentConfig) {
	c.ex.SetConfig(executor.Config{LanIP: cfg.GetLanIp(), DNS: cfg.GetDnsServers(), AllowCIDRs: cfg.GetAllowCidrs(),
		BandwidthMbps: int(cfg.GetBandwidthMbps()), DataSizeGiB: int(cfg.GetDataSizeGib())})
	// 平台下发的心跳间隔只在调用方没有显式指定时生效（测试与 fakeagent 用很短的间隔）
	if s := cfg.GetHeartbeatIntervalS(); s > 0 && !c.explicitHB {
		c.hbInterval = time.Duration(s) * time.Second
	}
}

func (c *Client) handle(msg *agentpb.PlatformMessage) {
	switch m := msg.Msg.(type) {
	case *agentpb.PlatformMessage_Config:
		c.applyConfig(m.Config)
	case *agentpb.PlatformMessage_Command:
		cmd := m.Command
		c.mu.Lock()
		c.received[cmd.GetId()] = true
		c.mu.Unlock()
		c.ex.Submit(toCommand(cmd))
	}
}

func toCommand(cmd *agentpb.Command) executor.Command {
	out := executor.Command{ID: cmd.GetId(), InstanceID: cmd.GetInstanceId()}
	switch b := cmd.Body.(type) {
	case *agentpb.Command_Create:
		out.Type = "create_instance"
		out.ImageRef, out.UpstreamPubKey = b.Create.GetImageRef(), b.Create.GetUpstreamPubkey()
		out.BandwidthMbps, out.SSHPort, out.DataSizeGiB = int(b.Create.GetBandwidthMbps()), int(b.Create.GetSshPort()), int(b.Create.GetDataSizeGib())
	case *agentpb.Command_Start:
		out.Type = "start_instance"
	case *agentpb.Command_Stop:
		out.Type = "stop_instance"
	case *agentpb.Command_Wipe:
		out.Type = "wipe_instance"
	case *agentpb.Command_Pull:
		out.Type = "pull_image"
		out.ImageRef = b.Pull.GetImageRef()
	}
	return out
}

func (c *Client) send(m *agentpb.AgentMessage) error {
	c.mu.Lock()
	s := c.stream
	c.mu.Unlock()
	if s == nil {
		return errors.New("not connected")
	}
	c.sendMu.Lock()
	defer c.sendMu.Unlock()
	return s.Send(m)
}

func (c *Client) sendResult(id string, r state.Result) error {
	if err := c.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Result{Result: &agentpb.CommandResult{CommandId: id, Ok: r.OK, Message: r.Message}}}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return c.ex.StateStore().MarkSent(id)
}

func (c *Client) SendHeartbeat(ctx context.Context) error {
	m := c.collect(ctx)
	return c.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_Heartbeat{Heartbeat: &agentpb.Heartbeat{Metrics: &agentpb.Metrics{
		GpuUtil: m.GPUUtil, GpuTempC: m.GPUTempC, GpuPowerW: m.GPUPowerW, MemTotal: m.MemTotal, MemUsed: m.MemUsed,
		RootDiskTotal: m.RootDiskTotal, RootDiskUsed: m.RootDiskUsed, DataDiskUsed: m.DataDiskUsed, Load1: m.Load1}}}})
}

func (c *Client) SendStateReport(ctx context.Context) error {
	var states []*agentpb.InstanceState
	for _, s := range c.ex.Report(ctx) {
		states = append(states, &agentpb.InstanceState{InstanceId: s.InstanceID, Phase: s.Phase})
	}
	return c.send(&agentpb.AgentMessage{Msg: &agentpb.AgentMessage_State{State: &agentpb.StateReport{Instances: states}}})
}

// Step 步进模式：执行队列（override 非空时直接记录该结果）、逐条发送结果（含回放）、再发 StateReport。
// 返回执行与回放的条数；发送失败时结果仍在 state.json 里未标记 Sent，重连后补发。
func (c *Client) Step(ctx context.Context, override *state.Result) (int, error) {
	results := c.ex.RunPending(ctx, override)
	for i, r := range results {
		if err := c.sendResult(r.ID, state.Result{OK: r.OK, Message: r.Message}); err != nil {
			return i, err
		}
	}
	return len(results), c.SendStateReport(ctx)
}
```

`cmd/spark-agent/main_test.go`：

```go
package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/cmdrun"
)

func TestParseConfigFile(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	_ = os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "config.yaml"), []byte("# 平台地址\nplatform: agent.example:9443\nserver_name: agent.example\n"), 0o600)
	cfg, err := loadAgentConfig(dir)
	if err != nil || cfg.Platform != "agent.example:9443" || cfg.ServerName != "agent.example" {
		t.Fatalf("cfg = %+v %v", cfg, err)
	}
	if _, err := loadAgentConfig(t.TempDir()); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("missing config accepted")
	}
}

func TestSelfcheckReportsEachItem(t *testing.T) {
	r := &cmdrun.Recorder{}
	r.Stub("docker version", "29.6.2\n", nil)
	r.Stub("docker info", `{"nvidia":{"path":"nvidia-container-runtime"}}`, nil)
	r.Stub("iptables -w -S DOCKER-USER", "-N DOCKER-USER\n-A DOCKER-USER -j RETURN\n", nil)
	r.Stub("tc -V", "tc utility\n", nil)
	r.Stub("losetup -V", "losetup from util-linux\n", nil)
	r.Stub("ufw status", "", errors.New("not installed"))
	r.Stub("df", "Avail\n2000000000000\n", nil)
	var out bytes.Buffer
	if err := selfcheck(context.Background(), r, "/var/lib/spark-agent", &out); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("selfcheck: %v\n%s", err, out.String())
	}
	for _, want := range []string{"ok: docker 29.6.2", "ok: nvidia runtime", "ok: iptables DOCKER-USER", "ok: tc", "ok: losetup", "ok: ufw", "ok: disk free"} {
		if !strings.Contains(out.String(), want) {
			t.Fatalf("missing %q in:\n%s", want, out.String())
		}
	}
	r.Stub("docker info", `{}`, nil)
	r.Stub("ufw status", "Status: active\n", nil)
	r.Stub("df", "Avail\n1000\n", nil)
	out.Reset()
	if err := selfcheck(context.Background(), r, "/var/lib/spark-agent", &out); err == nil || !strings.Contains(out.String(), "FAIL: nvidia runtime") || !strings.Contains(out.String(), "FAIL: ufw") || !strings.Contains(out.String(), "FAIL: disk free") {
		t.Fatalf("expected failures, err=%v:\n%s", err, out.String())
	}
}

func TestVersionCommand(t *testing.T) {
	var out bytes.Buffer
	if err := run([]string{"version"}, &out); err != nil || !strings.Contains(out.String(), "spark-agent ") {
		t.Fatalf("version = %q %v", out.String(), err)
	}
	if err := run([]string{"bogus"}, &out); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("unknown subcommand accepted")
	}
}
```

```go
// Package fakeagent 在进程内跑一个完整的 spark-agent（fake 运行时 / 数据盘 / 网络），供集成测试与 e2e-server 使用。
package fakeagent

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/client"
	diskfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/disk/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/executor"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"
	netfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/netfilter/fake"
	rtfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/runtime/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"
	"spark-cloud/internal/pki"
)

// Creds 是一次接入得到的凭据，可在重连 / 顶替测试里复用。
type Creds struct {
	Key     *ecdsa.PrivateKey
	CertDER []byte
	CADER   []byte
}

type Options struct {
	Addr              string
	CAPEM             []byte
	Token             string
	Creds             *Creds // 非空时不 Enroll，直接用这套凭据建流
	Dir               string // 空则临时目录
	Stepped           bool
	NoReconnect       bool // 被顶掉 / 被撤销后不重连（顶替类测试里的旧 Agent）
	Version           string
	IPs               []string // Hello 上报的本机地址；为空时取 "10.0.0.10"
	FreeBytes         uint64   // 0 → 4000 GiB
	Metrics           metrics.Metrics
	HeartbeatInterval time.Duration // 自动模式心跳间隔，零值 100ms（测试里推进假时钟后很快就能同步）
	Log               *slog.Logger
}

type Agent struct {
	Client  *client.Client
	Exec    *executor.Executor
	Runtime *rtfake.Runtime
	Disk    *diskfake.Disk
	Net     *netfake.Netfilter
	Dir     string
	creds   *Creds
	cancel  context.CancelFunc
	done    chan struct{}
}

func (a *Agent) Creds() *Creds { return a.creds }

// Start 接入（或复用凭据）并建流，直到收到 AgentConfig 才返回。
func Start(ctx context.Context, o Options) (*Agent, error) {
	if o.Log == nil {
		o.Log = slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	}
	if o.Version == "" {
		o.Version = "fake-0.1"
	}
	if len(o.IPs) == 0 {
		o.IPs = []string{"10.0.0.10"}
	}
	if o.FreeBytes == 0 {
		o.FreeBytes = 4000 << 30
	}
	if o.HeartbeatInterval <= 0 {
		o.HeartbeatInterval = 100 * time.Millisecond
	}
	dir := o.Dir
	if dir == "" {
		var err error
		if dir, err = os.MkdirTemp("", "fakeagent-"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
	}
	creds := o.Creds
	if creds == nil {
		block, _ := pem.Decode(o.CAPEM)
		if block == nil {
			return nil, errors.New("fakeagent: bad CA PEM")
		}
		key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		certDER, caDER, err := client.Enroll(ctx, o.Addr, o.Token, pki.Fingerprint(block.Bytes), key)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		creds = &Creds{Key: key, CertDER: certDER, CADER: caDER}
	}
	host, _, _ := net.SplitHostPort(o.Addr)
	tc, err := client.TLSFor(creds.Key, creds.CertDER, creds.CADER, host)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	st, err := state.Open(filepath.Join(dir, "state.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	a := &Agent{Runtime: rtfake.New(), Disk: diskfake.New(o.FreeBytes), Net: netfake.New(), Dir: dir, creds: creds, done: make(chan struct{})}
	ips := o.IPs
	a.Exec = executor.New(executor.Deps{Runtime: a.Runtime, Disk: a.Disk, Net: a.Net, State: st, StateDir: dir, Bridge: "spark0",
		LocalIPs: func() []string { return ips }, MemTotal: func() uint64 { return 128 << 30 }, Log: o.Log}, o.Stepped)
	m := o.Metrics
	a.Client = client.New(client.Config{Addr: o.Addr, TLS: tc, Version: o.Version, Hostname: "fake", LocalIPs: func() []string { return ips }, Stepped: o.Stepped,
		NoReconnect: o.NoReconnect, HeartbeatInterval: o.HeartbeatInterval, Backoff: client.Backoff{Min: 50 * time.Millisecond, Max: time.Second}},
		a.Exec, func(context.Context) metrics.Metrics { return m }, o.Log)
	runCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	a.cancel = cancel
	go func() { defer close(a.done); _ = a.Client.Run(runCtx) }()
	if !o.Stepped {
		go a.Exec.Run(runCtx)
	}
	waitCtx, waitCancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 10*time.Second)
	defer waitCancel()
	if err := a.Client.WaitConnected(waitCtx); err != nil {
		a.Stop()
		return nil, errors.New("fakeagent: not connected within 10s")
	}
	return a, nil
}

func (a *Agent) Stop() {
	a.cancel()
	<-a.done
}
```

「重连」在集成测试里通过 `Stop()` + 用 `Creds: ag.Creds(), Dir: ag.Dir` 重新 `Start` 实现（同一证书、同一状态文件），不需要单独的 `Reconnect` 方法。Agent 收到 `AgentConfig` 时 `client.applyConfig` 会把 `heartbeat_interval_s` 写回 `hbInterval`，这会覆盖 `HeartbeatInterval` 的测试值——所以 `applyConfig` 只在 `cfg.HeartbeatInterval` 为零值（未由调用方指定）时采用平台下发的间隔；`client.Config` 的字段注释写明这一点。

- [ ] **Step 4: 实现 `cmd/spark-agent`**


`main.go`：

```go
// spark-agent 是跑在每台 DGX Spark 上的节点代理：enroll 接入、run 常驻、selfcheck 自检。
package main

import (
	"bufio"
	"context"
	"crypto/ecdsa"
	"crypto/elliptic"
	"crypto/rand"
	"crypto/x509"
	"encoding/pem"
	"errors"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"syscall"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/client"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/cmdrun"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/disk"
	diskfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/disk/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/disk/loop"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/executor"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/netfilter"
	netfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/netfilter/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/netfilter/iptables"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/runtime"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/runtime/docker"
	rtfake "spark-cloud/internal/agent/runtime/fake"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"
)

var version = "dev" // -ldflags "-X main.version=..."

const usage = `用法：
  spark-agent enroll --platform <host:port> --token <spk1_...> --ca-sha256 <指纹> [--dir /etc/spark-agent]
  spark-agent run [--dir /etc/spark-agent] [--state-dir /var/lib/spark-agent] [--fake]
  spark-agent selfcheck [--state-dir /var/lib/spark-agent]
  spark-agent version`

func main() {
	if err := run(os.Args[1:], os.Stdout); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "spark-agent:", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}

func run(args []string, out io.Writer) error {
	if len(args) == 0 {
		return errors.New(usage)
	}
	switch args[0] {
	case "version":
		fmt.Fprintf(out, "spark-agent %s\n", version)
		return nil
	case "enroll":
		return enroll(args[1:], out)
	case "run":
		return runAgent(args[1:], out)
	case "selfcheck":
		fs := flag.NewFlagSet("selfcheck", flag.ContinueOnError)
		stateDir := fs.String("state-dir", "/var/lib/spark-agent", "状态目录")
		if err := fs.Parse(args[1:]); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return selfcheck(context.Background(), cmdrun.Exec{}, *stateDir, out)
	}
	return errors.New(usage)
}

type agentConfig struct {
	Platform   string
	ServerName string
}

// loadAgentConfig 读 <dir>/config.yaml：只支持 `key: value` 行与 # 注释。
func loadAgentConfig(dir string) (agentConfig, error) {
	f, err := os.Open(filepath.Join(dir, "config.yaml"))
	if err != nil {
		return agentConfig{}, err
	}
	defer f.Close()
	var cfg agentConfig
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(f)
	for sc.Scan() {
		line := strings.TrimSpace(sc.Text())
		if line == "" || strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
			continue
		}
		k, v, ok := strings.Cut(line, ":")
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		v = strings.TrimSpace(v)
		switch strings.TrimSpace(k) {
		case "platform":
			cfg.Platform = v
		case "server_name":
			cfg.ServerName = v
		}
	}
	if cfg.Platform == "" {
		return agentConfig{}, errors.New("config.yaml 缺少 platform")
	}
	if cfg.ServerName == "" {
		cfg.ServerName, _, _ = net.SplitHostPort(cfg.Platform)
	}
	return cfg, sc.Err()
}

func enroll(args []string, out io.Writer) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("enroll", flag.ContinueOnError)
	platform := fs.String("platform", "", "平台地址 host:port")
	token := fs.String("token", "", "一次性接入令牌")
	caSHA := fs.String("ca-sha256", "", "平台 CA 证书指纹")
	dir := fs.String("dir", "/etc/spark-agent", "配置目录")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if *platform == "" || *token == "" || *caSHA == "" {
		return errors.New("--platform、--token、--ca-sha256 都必填\n" + usage)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(*dir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	keyPath := filepath.Join(*dir, "agent.key")
	key, err := loadOrCreateKey(keyPath)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	certDER, caDER, err := client.Enroll(context.Background(), *platform, strings.TrimSpace(*token), strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(*caSHA)), key)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("接入失败: %w", err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(*dir, "agent.crt"), pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: certDER}), 0o600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(*dir, "ca.crt"), pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "CERTIFICATE", Bytes: caDER}), 0o600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	host, _, _ := net.SplitHostPort(*platform)
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(*dir, "config.yaml"), []byte(fmt.Sprintf("platform: %s\nserver_name: %s\n", *platform, host)), 0o600); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	fmt.Fprintf(out, "已接入平台 %s，证书写入 %s\n", *platform, *dir)
	return nil
}

func loadOrCreateKey(path string) (*ecdsa.PrivateKey, error) {
	if raw, err := os.ReadFile(path); err == nil {
		block, _ := pem.Decode(raw)
		if block == nil {
			return nil, errors.New("agent.key 不是 PEM")
		}
		return x509.ParseECPrivateKey(block.Bytes)
	}
	key, err := ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	der, err := x509.MarshalECPrivateKey(key)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return key, os.WriteFile(path, pem.EncodeToMemory(&pem.Block{Type: "EC PRIVATE KEY", Bytes: der}), 0o600)
}

func runAgent(args []string, out io.Writer) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("run", flag.ContinueOnError)
	dir := fs.String("dir", "/etc/spark-agent", "配置目录")
	stateDir := fs.String("state-dir", "/var/lib/spark-agent", "状态目录")
	fake := fs.Bool("fake", false, "用内存 fake 运行时（本地开发，不需要 Docker / root）")
	if err := fs.Parse(args); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	log := slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(out, nil))
	cfg, err := loadAgentConfig(*dir)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	key, err := loadOrCreateKey(filepath.Join(*dir, "agent.key"))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	certDER, err := readPEM(filepath.Join(*dir, "agent.crt"))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	caDER, err := readPEM(filepath.Join(*dir, "ca.crt"))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	tc, err := client.TLSFor(key, certDER, caDER, cfg.ServerName)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(*stateDir, 0o700); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	st, err := state.Open(filepath.Join(*stateDir, "state.json"))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var rt runtime.Runtime
	var dk disk.Disk
	var nf netfilter.Netfilter
	runner := cmdrun.Exec{}
	if *fake {
		rt, dk, nf = rtfake.New(), diskfake.New(4000<<30), netfake.New()
	} else {
		rt = &docker.Runtime{Run: runner, Bridge: "spark0", Subnet: "10.200.0.0/24"}
		dk = &loop.Disk{Run: runner, Stat: os.Stat, StateDir: *stateDir, Log: log}
		nf = &iptables.Netfilter{Run: runner}
	}
	ex := executor.New(executor.Deps{Runtime: rt, Disk: dk, Net: nf, State: st, StateDir: *stateDir, Bridge: "spark0", LocalIPs: localIPs, Log: log}, false)
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()
	if err := ex.Recover(ctx); err != nil {
		log.Error("recover", "err", err) // 继续运行，StateReport 会报 missing_data
	}
	collector := &metrics.Collector{Run: runner, ReadFile: os.ReadFile}
	hostname, _ := os.Hostname()
	c := client.New(client.Config{Addr: cfg.Platform, TLS: tc, Version: version, Hostname: hostname, LocalIPs: localIPs},
		ex, func(ctx context.Context) metrics.Metrics { return collector.Collect(ctx, ex.DataMount()) }, log)
	go ex.Run(ctx)
	log.Info("spark-agent running", "platform", cfg.Platform, "version", version, "fake", *fake)
	return c.Run(ctx)
}

func readPEM(path string) ([]byte, error) {
	raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	block, _ := pem.Decode(raw)
	if block == nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%s 不是 PEM", path)
	}
	return block.Bytes, nil
}

// localIPs 返回本机全部非回环 IPv4 地址。
func localIPs() []string {
	var out []string
	addrs, err := net.InterfaceAddrs()
	if err != nil {
		return nil
	}
	for _, a := range addrs {
		if ipn, ok := a.(*net.IPNet); ok && ipn.IP.To4() != nil && !ipn.IP.IsLoopback() {
			out = append(out, ipn.IP.String())
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

`selfcheck.go`：

```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"strconv"
	"strings"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/cmdrun"
)

const minFreeBytes = uint64(100) << 30

// selfcheck 逐项检查并打印 ok/FAIL；任一失败返回错误（安装脚本据此中止）。
func selfcheck(ctx context.Context, run cmdrun.Runner, stateDir string, out io.Writer) error {
	failed := false
	report := func(name string, err error, detail string) {
		if err != nil {
			failed = true
			fmt.Fprintf(out, "FAIL: %s: %v\n", name, err)
			return
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(out, "ok: %s %s\n", name, detail)
	}
	v, err := run.Run(ctx, "docker", "version", "--format", "{{.Server.Version}}")
	report("docker", err, strings.TrimSpace(string(v)))
	info, err := run.Run(ctx, "docker", "info", "--format", "{{json .Runtimes}}")
	if err == nil && !strings.Contains(string(info), `"nvidia"`) {
		err = errors.New("docker 没有 nvidia 运行时（安装 nvidia-container-toolkit）")
	}
	report("nvidia runtime", err, "")
	_, err = run.Run(ctx, "iptables", "-w", "-S", "DOCKER-USER")
	report("iptables DOCKER-USER", err, "")
	_, err = run.Run(ctx, "tc", "-V")
	report("tc", err, "")
	_, err = run.Run(ctx, "losetup", "-V")
	report("losetup", err, "")
	ufw, err := run.Run(ctx, "ufw", "status")
	if err != nil {
		err = nil // 未安装即可
	} else if strings.Contains(string(ufw), "Status: active") {
		err = errors.New("ufw 已启用，会干扰 DOCKER-USER 规则，请禁用")
	}
	report("ufw", err, "")
	df, err := run.Run(ctx, "df", "-B1", "--output=avail", stateDir)
	var free uint64
	if err == nil {
		lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(df)), "\n")
		free, _ = strconv.ParseUint(strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]), 10, 64)
		if free < minFreeBytes {
			err = fmt.Errorf("剩余 %d GiB，少于 100 GiB", free>>30)
		}
	}
	report("disk free", err, fmt.Sprintf("%d GiB", free>>30))
	if failed {
		return errors.New("自检未通过")
	}
	return nil
}
```

（`selfcheck` 测试里 `docker version` 的 stub 前缀是 `docker version`，匹配带 `--format` 的调用。）

`internal/app/agentflow_test.go`：

```go
package app_test

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http/cookiejar"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/fakeagent"
	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"
	"spark-cloud/internal/app"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/clock"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/config"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

const flowTick = 20 * time.Millisecond

type flow struct {
	t    *testing.T
	ctx  context.Context
	pool *pgxpool.Pool
	clk  *clock.Fake
	a    *app.App
	addr string
	ts   *httptest.Server
	seq  int
}

func buildApp(t *testing.T, pool *pgxpool.Pool, clk *clock.Fake) (*app.App, string) {
	t.Helper()
	// 显式给上游密钥：否则 dev 下 Build 会在 os.TempDir() 生成一把永不清理的密钥
	keyFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "upstream.key")
	if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(keyFile); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg := config.Config{Env: "dev", UploadDir: t.TempDir(), Domain: "spark.test", SecretKey: dbtest.Key, SMSDriver: "mock", KYCDriver: "mock",
		AgentAddr: "127.0.0.1:0", AgentHosts: []string{"127.0.0.1"}, AgentDataSizeGiB: 2048, AgentBandwidthMbps: 200, AgentDNSServers: []string{"223.5.5.5"},
		UpstreamSSHKeyFile: keyFile}
	a, err := app.Build(cfg, pool, slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)), app.Options{Clock: clk, AgentTick: flowTick, DisableRateLimits: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(done); _ = a.Gateway.Serve(ctx, lis) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { cancel(); <-done })
	return a, lis.Addr().String()
}

func newFlow(t *testing.T) *flow {
	t.Helper()
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	clk := clock.NewFake(dbtest.T0)
	a, addr := buildApp(t, pool, clk)
	ts := httptest.NewServer(a.Handler)
	t.Cleanup(ts.Close)
	return &flow{t: t, ctx: context.Background(), pool: pool, clk: clk, a: a, addr: addr, ts: ts}
}

// enrollAgent 登记一台机器并接入一个 fake Agent；opts 可改 Options（例如顶替类用例给旧 Agent 设 NoReconnect）。
func (f *flow) enrollAgent(serial string, stepped bool, opts ...func(*fakeagent.Options)) (int64, *fakeagent.Agent) {
	f.t.Helper()
	mid := dbtest.CreateMachine(f.t, f.pool, serial)
	_, _ = f.pool.Exec(f.ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_online = false, last_seen_at = NULL WHERE id = $1`, mid)
	tok, err := f.a.Fleet.IssueEnrollToken(f.ctx, audit.Actor{}, mid)
	if err != nil {
		f.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	o := fakeagent.Options{Addr: f.addr, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Token: tok.Plain, Stepped: stepped,
		Metrics: metrics.Metrics{GPUTempC: 55, RootDiskTotal: 1000, RootDiskUsed: 400, MemTotal: 128 << 30}}
	for _, opt := range opts {
		opt(&o)
	}
	ag, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, o)
	if err != nil {
		f.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.t.Cleanup(ag.Stop)
	f.waitFor("first report", func() bool { return f.a.Gateway.ReportCount(mid) >= 1 })
	return mid, ag
}

func (f *flow) renter() *client {
	f.t.Helper()
	f.seq++
	jar, _ := cookiejar.New(nil)
	c := &client{t: f.t, base: f.ts.URL + "/api/v1", http: &http.Client{Jar: jar}}
	phone := fmt.Sprintf("138%08d", f.seq)
	c.do("POST", "/auth/sms-code", map[string]string{"phone": phone})
	c.do("POST", "/auth/login", map[string]string{"phone": phone, "code": "000000"})
	c.do("POST", "/kyc/personal", map[string]string{"name": "李四", "id_number": "110101199001011234"})
	rch := c.do("POST", "/recharges", map[string]any{"amount": 1000000, "channel": "wechat"})
	c.do("POST", "/dev/recharges/"+rch["id"].(string)+"/pay", map[string]any{})
	return c
}

func (f *flow) order(c *client) string {
	f.t.Helper()
	products := c.do("GET", "/products", nil)["items"].([]any)
	images := c.do("GET", "/images", nil)["items"].([]any)
	order := c.do("POST", "/orders", map[string]any{"product_id": products[0].(map[string]any)["id"], "duration": "1d", "image_id": images[0].(map[string]any)["id"]})
	return order["instance"].(map[string]any)["id"].(string)
}

func (f *flow) state(id string) rental.Instance {
	f.t.Helper()
	inst, err := f.a.Rental.Get(f.ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		f.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return inst
}

func (f *flow) waitState(id, want string) {
	f.t.Helper()
	f.waitFor("instance "+id+" → "+want, func() bool { return f.state(id).State == want })
}

func (f *flow) waitFor(what string, cond func() bool) {
	f.t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(10 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if cond() {
			return
		}
		time.Sleep(10 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	f.t.Fatalf("timeout waiting for %s", what)
}

func (f *flow) sweep() {
	f.t.Helper()
	if err := f.a.Rental.Sweep(f.ctx); err != nil {
		f.t.Fatal(err)
	}
}

// syncHeartbeat 推进假时钟后等机器的 last_seen_at 追上当前假时间（自动模式 Agent 每 100ms 心跳；步进模式自己发一次），
// 否则清扫器会把机器判离线、把实例暂停。
func (f *flow) syncHeartbeat(mid int64, ag *fakeagent.Agent, stepped bool) {
	f.t.Helper()
	if stepped {
		if err := ag.Client.SendHeartbeat(f.ctx); err != nil {
			f.t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	f.waitFor("heartbeat at current fake time", func() bool {
		m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid)
		return m.LastSeenAt != nil && m.LastSeenAt.Equal(f.clk.Now())
	})
}

func (f *flow) alertCount(kind string) int {
	var n int
	_ = f.pool.QueryRow(f.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM alerts WHERE kind = $1`, kind).Scan(&n)
	return n
}

// 自动模式 Agent：接入 → 下单 → 开通 → 到期停机 → 续费恢复 → 释放 → 洗机 → 机器回到库存。
func TestAgentFullLifecycle(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", false)
	m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid)
	if !m.Enrolled || !m.Connected || !m.AgentOnline {
		t.Fatalf("machine after enroll = %+v", m)
	}
	c := f.renter()
	id := f.order(c)
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateRunning)
	if ct := ag.Runtime.Containers()[id]; !ct.Running {
		t.Fatalf("container = %+v", ct)
	}
	if _, mounted := ag.Disk.State(id); !mounted {
		t.Fatal("data disk not mounted")
	}
	// 指标到库
	f.waitFor("metrics", func() bool { m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid); return len(m.LastMetrics) > 0 })

	f.clk.Advance(24*time.Hour + time.Second)
	f.syncHeartbeat(mid, ag, false)
	f.sweep()
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateStopped)
	f.waitFor("container stopped", func() bool { return !ag.Runtime.Containers()[id].Running })

	c.do("POST", "/instances/"+id+"/renew", map[string]string{"duration": "1d"})
	f.waitFor("container running again", func() bool { return ag.Runtime.Containers()[id].Running })
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateRunning)

	c.do("POST", "/instances/"+id+"/release", map[string]any{})
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateReleased)
	if ct := ag.Runtime.Containers()[id]; ct.Exists {
		t.Fatal("container not wiped")
	}
	if created, _ := ag.Disk.State(id); created {
		t.Fatal("disk not destroyed")
	}
	f.waitFor("machine idle", func() bool { m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid); return m.State == "idle" })
	var open int
	_ = f.pool.QueryRow(f.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM agent_commands WHERE status IN ('pending','sent')`).Scan(&open)
	if open != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("open commands = %d", open)
	}
}

// 断线重连补推：Agent 掉线期间入队的指令在重连后被投递执行。
func TestReconnectDeliversQueuedCommands(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", false)
	ag.Stop()
	f.waitFor("disconnected", func() bool { return !f.a.Gateway.Connected(mid) })
	c := f.renter()
	id := f.order(c) // 机器 5 分钟内仍 agent_online，可被分配（设计 §3.1 第 7 条）
	time.Sleep(5 * flowTick)
	if f.state(id).State != rental.StateCreating {
		t.Fatal("must wait for agent")
	}
	ag2, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: f.addr, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Creds: ag.Creds(), Dir: ag.Dir})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(ag2.Stop)
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateRunning)
}

// 迟到回报：指令被平台判超时后 Agent 的成功回报被忽略，对账再把实际建出来的容器清掉。
func TestLateResultIgnoredAndOrphanWiped(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", true)
	c := f.renter()
	id := f.order(c)
	f.waitFor("delivered", func() bool { return ag.Client.Received(f.commandID(id, "create_instance")) })
	f.clk.Advance(rental.CreateCommandTimeout + time.Second)
	f.syncHeartbeat(mid, ag, true) // 机器仍在线，指令超时才会计时
	f.sweep()
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateFailed)
	if _, err := ag.Client.Step(f.ctx, nil); err != nil { // Agent 现在才执行：容器真的建出来了
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitFor("orphan wipe enqueued", func() bool { return f.alertCount("orphan_container") == 1 })
	if f.state(id).State != rental.StateFailed {
		t.Fatal("late success revived instance")
	}
	f.waitFor("wipe delivered", func() bool { return ag.Client.Received(f.commandID(id, "wipe_instance")) })
	if _, err := ag.Client.Step(f.ctx, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitFor("container gone", func() bool { return !ag.Runtime.Containers()[id].Exists })
	m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid)
	if m.State != "faulty" { // 开通失败照旧隔离机器
		t.Fatalf("machine = %s", m.State)
	}
}

// 离线恢复：容器不在 → 保持暂停并告警；容器在 → 恢复计时。
func TestOfflineResumeDependsOnContainer(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", true)
	c := f.renter()
	id := f.order(c)
	f.waitFor("delivered", func() bool { return ag.Client.Received(f.commandID(id, "create_instance")) })
	if _, err := ag.Client.Step(f.ctx, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateRunning)
	f.clk.Advance(time.Hour)
	f.syncHeartbeat(mid, ag, true)
	f.clk.Advance(rental.OfflineTimeout + time.Minute)
	f.sweep()
	if inst := f.state(id); !inst.Suspended() {
		t.Fatalf("not suspended: %+v", inst)
	}
	ag.Runtime.Exit(id) // 失联期间容器退出了
	if err := ag.Client.SendHeartbeat(f.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := ag.Client.SendStateReport(f.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitFor("container_down alert", func() bool { return f.alertCount("container_down") == 1 })
	if !f.state(id).Suspended() {
		t.Fatal("resumed although container is down")
	}
	_ = ag.Runtime.Start(f.ctx, id) // 管理员手工拉起
	if err := ag.Client.SendStateReport(f.ctx); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitFor("resumed", func() bool { return !f.state(id).Suspended() })
}

// 新连接顶掉旧连接；指纹不符被拒。旧 Agent 用 NoReconnect，否则它会在退避后重新连上再把新 Agent 顶掉（乒乓）。
func TestReplaceAndRejectedFingerprint(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	noReconnect := func(o *fakeagent.Options) { o.NoReconnect = true }
	mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", true, noReconnect)
	ag2, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: f.addr, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Creds: ag.Creds(), Stepped: true, NoReconnect: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(ag2.Stop)
	f.waitFor("old stream replaced", func() bool { return !ag.Client.Connected() })
	f.waitFor("new stream active", func() bool { return ag2.Client.Connected() && f.a.Gateway.Connected(mid) })
	// 重新接入签出新证书：ag2 的流在下一个 tick 被断，旧凭据再连被拒
	tok, _ := f.a.Fleet.IssueEnrollToken(f.ctx, audit.Actor{}, mid)
	ag3, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: f.addr, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Token: tok.Plain, Stepped: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(ag3.Stop)
	f.waitFor("ag2 aborted", func() bool { return !ag2.Client.Connected() })
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(f.ctx, 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if _, err := fakeagent.Start(ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: f.addr, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Creds: ag.Creds(), Stepped: true, NoReconnect: true}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("old certificate must be rejected")
	}
	f.waitFor("ag3 still active", func() bool { return ag3.Client.Connected() && f.a.Gateway.Connected(mid) })
}

func (f *flow) commandID(instanceID, typ string) string {
	cmds, _ := f.a.Fleet.CommandsForInstance(f.ctx, instanceID, 20)
	for _, c := range cmds {
		if string(c.Type) == typ {
			return c.ID
		}
	}
	return ""
}
```

```go
// upstreamPublicKey 读取平台登录容器用的上游公钥；dev 下未配置时自动在临时目录生成并打印路径。
func upstreamPublicKey(cfg config.Config, log *slog.Logger) (string, error) {
	path := cfg.UpstreamSSHKeyFile
	if path == "" {
		if cfg.Env == "prod" {
			return "", errors.New("生产环境必须设置 SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE")
		}
		path = filepath.Join(os.TempDir(), "spark-upstream-"+randx.ID("", 8)+".key")
		if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(path); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
		log.Warn("SPARK_UPSTREAM_SSH_KEY_FILE 未设置，已生成临时上游 SSH 密钥（仅开发环境）", "path", path)
	}
	pub, err := pki.LoadUpstreamPublicKey(path)
	if err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("读取上游 SSH 密钥: %w", err)
	}
	return pub, nil
}
```

### Task 19: 集成测试：两个网关副本（顶替、撤销断流、A→B 切换 ≤2 tick）与并发 50 单抢 10 台

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/app/agentreplica_test.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 18 的 `flow` / `buildApp`、`fakeagent`、`agentgw.Server.Ticks / ReadyTick / DeliveryTick / Connected`。
- Produces: 无新代码；若测试暴露网关缺陷，在 `internal/agentgw` 内修复并补单测。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写测试**

`internal/app/agentreplica_test.go`：

```go
package app_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"spark-cloud/internal/agent/fakeagent"
	"spark-cloud/internal/app"
	"spark-cloud/internal/audit"
	"spark-cloud/internal/catalog"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/apperr"
	"spark-cloud/internal/platform/dbtest"
	"spark-cloud/internal/rental"
)

// twoReplicas 在同一个数据库上起两个 platform（两个网关监听）。
func twoReplicas(t *testing.T) (*flow, *app.App, string) {
	t.Helper()
	f := newFlow(t)
	b, addrB := buildApp(t, f.pool, f.clk)
	return f, b, addrB
}

// 旧 Agent（A 上）与被撤销的 Agent 都用 NoReconnect：否则它们退避后重连会把对方再顶掉，断言不稳定。
func noReconnect(o *fakeagent.Options) { o.NoReconnect = true }

func TestReplicaTakeoverAndRevoke(t *testing.T) {
	f, b, addrB := twoReplicas(t)
	mid, agA := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", true, noReconnect) // 连在 A
	agB, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: addrB, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Creds: agA.Creds(), Stepped: true, NoReconnect: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(agB.Stop)
	// 后连上的副本赢：A 在下一个 tick 发现归属不再是自己，关闭流
	f.waitFor("A aborted", func() bool { return !agA.Client.Connected() && !f.a.Gateway.Connected(mid) })
	if !b.Gateway.Connected(mid) {
		t.Fatal("B must own the stream")
	}
	m, _ := f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid)
	if !m.Connected {
		t.Fatal("A's detach cleared B's ownership")
	}
	// 撤销接入：B 上的旧流在下一个 tick 被断
	if err := f.a.Fleet.RevokeEnrollment(f.ctx, audit.Actor{AdminID: 1}, mid); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitFor("B aborted after revoke", func() bool { return !agB.Client.Connected() && !b.Gateway.Connected(mid) })
	m, _ = f.a.Fleet.Get(f.ctx, f.pool, mid)
	if m.Enrolled || m.Connected {
		t.Fatalf("after revoke = %+v", m)
	}
}

// Agent 从 A 切到 B：A 已认领为 sent 但 Agent 尚未执行的指令、以及切换后新入队的指令，都须在 B 处理完首个报告后 ≤2 个 tick 内由 B 投递。
func TestSwitchDeliversWithinTwoTicks(t *testing.T) {
	f, b, addrB := twoReplicas(t)
	mid, agA := f.enrollAgent("SN-001", true, noReconnect)
	c := f.renter()
	id := f.order(c)
	createID := f.commandID(id, "create_instance")
	f.waitFor("A delivered", func() bool { return agA.Client.Received(createID) }) // A 认领为 sent，步进式 Agent 不执行
	// 切到 B：A 的流被顶掉（agA 不重连），B 通过「sent 补推」投递同一条指令
	agB, err := fakeagent.Start(f.ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: addrB, CAPEM: f.a.Gateway.CAPEM(), Creds: agA.Creds(), Stepped: true})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(agB.Stop)
	f.waitFor("B delivered", func() bool { return agB.Client.Received(createID) })
	ready, ok := b.Gateway.ReadyTick(mid)
	delivered, ok2 := b.Gateway.DeliveryTick(createID)
	if !ok || !ok2 {
		t.Fatalf("ticks missing: ready=%v delivered=%v", ok, ok2)
	}
	if delivered-ready > 2 {
		t.Fatalf("re-push at tick %d, ready at %d: more than 2 ticks", delivered, ready)
	}
	if _, err := agB.Client.Step(f.ctx, nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	f.waitState(id, rental.StateRunning)
	if agA.Runtime.Containers()[id].Exists {
		t.Fatal("A's agent must not have executed")
	}
	// 切换后新入队的指令（到期停机）由 B 在 ≤2 tick 内认领投递
	f.clk.Advance(24*time.Hour + time.Second)
	f.syncHeartbeat(mid, agB, true)
	f.sweep()
	stopID := f.commandID(id, "stop_instance")
	before := b.Gateway.Ticks()
	f.waitFor("stop delivered by B", func() bool { return agB.Client.Received(stopID) })
	if tick, _ := b.Gateway.DeliveryTick(stopID); tick-before > 2 {
		t.Fatalf("stop delivered at tick %d, enqueued around %d", tick, before)
	}
	if _, ok := f.a.Gateway.DeliveryTick(stopID); ok {
		t.Fatal("A must not deliver after losing ownership")
	}
}

// 并发 50 单抢 10 台不超卖：恰好 10 单成功，其余 no_capacity；10 个 Agent 全部开通成功。
func TestConcurrentOrdersDoNotOversell(t *testing.T) {
	f := newFlow(t)
	agents := map[int64]*fakeagent.Agent{}
	for i := 0; i < 10; i++ {
		mid, ag := f.enrollAgent("SN-"+string(rune('A'+i)), false)
		agents[mid] = ag
	}
	users := make([]int64, 50)
	for i := range users {
		users[i] = dbtest.CreateUser(t, f.pool, fmt.Sprintf("1370000%04d", i), true)
		dbtest.Fund(t, f.pool, users[i], 100000)
	}
	productID, imageID := dbtest.ProductID(t, f.pool), dbtest.ImageID(t, f.pool)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	results := make(chan error, 50)
	for _, uid := range users {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(uid int64) {
			defer wg.Done()
			_, _, err := f.a.Rental.Place(f.ctx, rental.PlaceRequest{UserID: uid, ProductID: productID, ImageID: imageID, Duration: catalog.D1d})
			results <- err
		}(uid)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	close(results)
	okCount, capCount := 0, 0
	for err := range results {
		switch {
		case err == nil:
			okCount++
		case apperr.As(err) != nil && apperr.As(err).Code == apperr.ErrNoCapacity.Code:
			capCount++
		default:
			t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
		}
	}
	if okCount != 10 || capCount != 40 {
		t.Fatalf("ok=%d no_capacity=%d", okCount, capCount)
	}
	f.waitFor("all running", func() bool {
		var n int
		_ = f.pool.QueryRow(f.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM instances WHERE state = 'running'`).Scan(&n)
		return n == 10
	})
	var perMachine int
	_ = f.pool.QueryRow(f.ctx, `SELECT count(DISTINCT machine_id) FROM instances`).Scan(&perMachine)
	if perMachine != 10 {
		t.Fatalf("instances spread over %d machines", perMachine)
	}
}
```

```go
func TestEnrollCommandAndRevoke(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ops := e.loginAs("ops", admin.RoleOps)
	mid := e.machine("SN-ENR")
	path := fmt.Sprintf("/machines/%d/enroll-command", mid)
	if code, out := ops.do("POST", path, map[string]any{}); code != 403 || codeOf(out) != "totp_required" {
		t.Fatalf("without totp = %d %v", code, out)
	}
	code, out := ops.do("POST", path, map[string]any{"totp": ops.totp()})
	if code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("enroll-command = %d %v", code, out)
	}
	cmd := out["command"].(string)
	want := "curl -fsSL http://files.test/agent/install.sh | sudo sh -s -- --platform agent.test:9443 --token spk1_"
	if !strings.HasPrefix(cmd, want) || !strings.Contains(cmd, "--ca-sha256 "+e.gw.CAFingerprint()+" --binary-sha256 beef") {
		t.Fatalf("command = %s", cmd)
	}
	if out["token_id"].(float64) <= 0 || out["expires_at"] == nil {
		t.Fatalf("meta = %v", out)
	}
	// 审计不含令牌明文
	var diff string
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT diff::text FROM audit_logs WHERE action = 'machine.enroll_token'`).Scan(&diff)
	token := strings.Fields(strings.SplitN(cmd, "--token ", 2)[1])[0]
	if strings.Contains(diff, strings.TrimPrefix(token, "spk1_")) {
		t.Fatalf("audit leaks token: %s", diff)
	}
	// 未接入时撤销 → invalid_state；接入后撤销成功并清空
	if code, out := ops.do("POST", fmt.Sprintf("/machines/%d/revoke-enrollment", mid), map[string]any{}); code != 409 || codeOf(out) != "invalid_state" {
		t.Fatalf("revoke unenrolled = %d %v", code, out)
	}
	if _, err := e.pool.Exec(e.ctx, `UPDATE machines SET cert_fingerprint = repeat('ab', 32), agent_conn_id = 'c1', agent_version = '0.1.0', last_metrics = '{"gpu_temp_c":55}' WHERE id = $1`, mid); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	code, out = ops.do("GET", fmt.Sprintf("/machines/%d", mid), nil)
	m := out["machine"].(map[string]any)
	if m["enrolled"] != true || m["fingerprint_prefix"] != "abababababababab" || m["connected"] != true || m["agent_version"] != "0.1.0" || m["last_metrics"].(map[string]any)["gpu_temp_c"].(float64) != 55 {
		t.Fatalf("machine view = %v", m)
	}
	if _, has := m["cert_fingerprint"]; has {
		t.Fatal("full fingerprint must not be exposed")
	}
	code, out = ops.do("POST", fmt.Sprintf("/machines/%d/revoke-enrollment", mid), map[string]any{})
	if code != 200 || out["enrolled"] != false || out["connected"] != false {
		t.Fatalf("revoke = %d %v", code, out)
	}
	var n int
	_ = e.pool.QueryRow(e.ctx, `SELECT count(*) FROM audit_logs WHERE action = 'machine.revoke_enrollment'`).Scan(&n)
	if n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("revoke audit = %d", n)
	}
	fin := e.loginAs("fin", admin.RoleFinance)
	if code, out := fin.do("POST", path, map[string]any{}); code != 403 || codeOf(out) != "forbidden" {
		t.Fatalf("finance = %d %v", code, out)
	}
}
```

```go
func TestImageEnableBroadcastsPull(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	root := e.loginAs("root", admin.RoleSuper)
	online := e.machine("SN-ON")
	off := e.machine("SN-OFF")
	_, _ = e.pool.Exec(e.ctx, `UPDATE machines SET agent_online = false WHERE id = $1`, off)
	code, out := root.do("POST", "/images", map[string]any{"name": "cuda", "ref": "nvcr.io/x/cuda:13", "enabled": true, "sort": 1})
	if code != 201 {
		t.Fatalf("create = %d %v", code, out)
	}
	cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, online)
	if len(cmds) != 1 || cmds[0].Type != fleet.CmdPullImage || !strings.Contains(string(cmds[0].Payload), "nvcr.io/x/cuda:13") {
		t.Fatalf("pull = %+v", cmds)
	}
	if cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, off); len(cmds) != 0 {
		t.Fatal("offline machine got pull")
	}
	id := int64(out["id"].(float64))
	// 停用不广播；再启用广播
	if code, _ := root.do("PUT", fmt.Sprintf("/images/%d", id), map[string]any{"name": "cuda", "ref": "nvcr.io/x/cuda:13", "enabled": false, "sort": 1}); code != 200 {
		t.Fatal(code)
	}
	if cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, online); len(cmds) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("disable broadcast: %+v", cmds)
	}
	if code, _ := root.do("PUT", fmt.Sprintf("/images/%d", id), map[string]any{"name": "cuda", "ref": "nvcr.io/x/cuda:14", "enabled": true, "sort": 1}); code != 200 {
		t.Fatal(code)
	}
	if cmds, _ := e.fleet.PendingCommands(e.ctx, online); len(cmds) != 2 || !strings.Contains(string(cmds[1].Payload), "cuda:14") {
		t.Fatalf("enable broadcast: %+v", cmds)
	}
}
```

```go
// handleEnrollCommand 签发一次性接入令牌并返回安装命令（只显示一次；令牌明文不进日志与审计）。
func (s *Server) handleEnrollCommand(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, err := pathID(r, "id")
	if err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	var req struct {
		TOTP string `json:"totp"`
	}
	if err := httpx.DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	if err := s.stepUp(r, req.TOTP); err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	if s.Gateway == nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, errors.New("未配置 Agent 网关"))
		return
	}
	tok, err := s.Fleet.IssueEnrollToken(r.Context(), s.actor(r), id)
	if err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, map[string]any{"command": s.Gateway.InstallCommand(tok.Plain), "token_id": tok.ID, "expires_at": tok.ExpiresAt})
}

func (s *Server) handleRevokeEnrollment(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, err := pathID(r, "id")
	if err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	var req struct {
		TOTP string `json:"totp"`
	}
	if err := httpx.DecodeJSON(r, &req); err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	if err := s.stepUp(r, req.TOTP); err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	if err := s.Fleet.RevokeEnrollment(r.Context(), s.actor(r), id); err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	m, err := s.Fleet.Get(r.Context(), s.Fleet.Pool(), id)
	if err != nil {
		s.writeError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	httpx.WriteJSON(w, http.StatusOK, m)
}
```

```go
// broadcastPull 镜像上架或启用时向所有在线机器预拉取；失败不阻塞上架。
func (s *Server) broadcastPull(r *http.Request, img catalog.AdminImage) {
	if !img.Enabled {
		return
	}
	if n, err := s.Fleet.BroadcastPullImage(r.Context(), img.Ref); err != nil {
		s.Log.Warn("broadcast pull_image failed", "image", img.ID, "err", err)
	} else {
		s.Log.Info("pull_image broadcast", "image", img.ID, "machines", n)
	}
}
```

### Task 21: e2e-server 接入真实网关 + 步进式 fake Agent；钩子改造；现有 Playwright 全绿

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/e2ehooks/hooks.go`（`Server` 字段、`/hooks/machines`、`/hooks/agent`、`/hooks/sweep`）
- Modify: `internal/e2ehooks/hooks_test.go`（`newEnv` 起网关；用例断言改为新语义）
- Modify: `cmd/e2e-server/main.go`（网关监听 `127.0.0.1:18443`、`AgentTick: 50ms`、`AgentHosts`）
- Modify: `web/e2e/helpers/hooks.ts`（类型不变，加注释说明语义）

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 18 `app.App.Gateway` / `Options.AgentTick`、Task 17 `fakeagent`、Task 1 `Fleet.IssueEnrollToken` / `Get`。
- Produces:
  - `e2ehooks.Server` 新字段：`AgentAddr string`（网关监听地址，供 fake Agent 连接）；内部 `agents map[int64]*fakeagent.Agent` + `mu`。
  - `/hooks/machines {count, serial_prefix?, rack?}`（`rack` 缺省 `E2E`，供用例按机柜筛选）：登记 → 结束维护 → `agentFor(id)`（签发令牌、`fakeagent.Start(Stepped: true, IPs: [lan_ip], Metrics: 固定值 gpu_temp 55、root 40%、mem 128GiB）` → 等 `Gateway.ReportCount ≥ 1`）。
  - `/hooks/agent {machine_id?, fail?, result?}`：对每台目标机器（缺省全部**已有 fake Agent** 的机器 + 若指定 `machine_id` 而尚无 Agent 则先接入）：等该机器 `pending` 指令清零且 `sent` 指令全部 `Client.Received`（≤10 秒）→ `Client.Step(ctx, override)`（`fail` 为真时 `override = {OK:false, Message: result||"e2e: simulated failure"}`；否则 `nil`，真实执行 fake 运行时）→ 等 `ReportCount` 增加 → 累计 `completed`。
  - `/hooks/sweep {heartbeat?}`：`heartbeat != false` 时让每个 fake Agent `SendHeartbeat` 并等 `HeartbeatCount` 增加；然后 `Rental.Sweep` + `Billing.Sweep`。
  - 新增 `GET /hooks/agent/status?machine_id=` → `{connected: bool}`（前端用例断言撤销接入后连接断开；机器已被撤销接入时顺手停掉并移除它的 fake Agent）。
  - fake Agent 固定指标：`metrics.Metrics{GPUUtil: 12, GPUTempC: 55, GPUPowerW: 30, MemTotal: 128 << 30, MemUsed: 8 << 30, RootDiskTotal: 4000 << 30, RootDiskUsed: 1600 << 30, Load1: 0.5}`（列表显示 `55°C`、`40%`）。

`internal/e2ehooks/hooks_test.go` 的 `newEnv` 改为：

```go
func newEnv(t *testing.T) *env {
	t.Helper()
	pool := dbtest.New(t)
	clk := clock.NewFake(dbtest.T0)
	k := &e2ehooks.SwitchableKYC{}
	log := slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil))
	keyFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "upstream.key")
	if err := pki.GenerateUpstreamKey(keyFile); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cfg := config.Config{Env: "dev", Domain: "spark.test", SecretKey: dbtest.Key, SMSDriver: "mock", KYCDriver: "mock", UploadDir: t.TempDir(),
		AgentHosts: []string{"127.0.0.1"}, AgentDataSizeGiB: 2048, AgentBandwidthMbps: 200, AgentDNSServers: []string{"223.5.5.5"}, UpstreamSSHKeyFile: keyFile}
	a, err := app.Build(cfg, pool, log, app.Options{Clock: clk, KYC: k, DisableRateLimits: true, AgentTick: 20 * time.Millisecond})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	lis, err := net.Listen("tcp", "127.0.0.1:0")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	done := make(chan struct{})
	go func() { defer close(done); _ = a.Gateway.Serve(ctx, lis) }()
	hooks := &e2ehooks.Server{App: a, Pool: pool, Clock: clk, KYC: k, Log: log, AgentAddr: lis.Addr().String()}
	ts := httptest.NewServer(hooks.Handler())
	t.Cleanup(func() { ts.Close(); hooks.Close(); cancel(); <-done })
	return &env{t: t, a: a, clk: clk, ts: ts}
}
```

```go
	// 步进式：下单后不调 hooks/agent 就一直 creating（fake Agent 不自动执行）
	_, inst3, err := e.a.Rental.Place(ctx, rental.PlaceRequest{UserID: uid, ProductID: dbtest.ProductID(t, e.a.Fleet.Pool()),
```

5. 后续原有逻辑不变：`advance 86400 + sweep` 后 `inst` 与 `inst3` 都到期（各一条 `stop` pending）；随后「开通失败」段的 `inst2` 落在 T-3；`/hooks/agent {fail:true}` 会把两条 `stop` 按失败回报（两条 `stop_failed` 告警）并让 `inst2` 开通失败——这是预期行为，原有 `create_failed` 断言在第一页 20 条未处理告警里仍能找到。

新增用例：

```go
// 后台 API 登记（未接入）的机器调 hooks/agent 时自动接入。
func TestAgentHookEnrollsUnknownMachine(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	m, err := e.a.Fleet.Register(ctx, fleet.NewMachine{Serial: "REG-1", LanIP: "10.99.0.9", ProductID: dbtest.ProductID(t, e.a.Fleet.Pool())})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := e.a.Fleet.SetMaintenance(ctx, m.ID, false); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if out := e.must("POST", "/hooks/agent", map[string]any{"machine_id": m.ID}); out["completed"].(float64) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("agent = %v", out)
	}
	got, _ := e.a.Fleet.Get(ctx, e.a.Fleet.Pool(), m.ID)
	if !got.Enrolled || !got.Connected || !got.AgentOnline || got.LastSeenAt == nil {
		t.Fatalf("machine = %+v", got)
	}
}

// sweep 的 heartbeat 通过 fake Agent 发送：推进 6 分钟后 heartbeat=true 的 sweep 不会判离线。
func TestSweepHeartbeatGoesThroughAgent(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	ids := e.must("POST", "/hooks/machines", map[string]any{"count": 1})["ids"].([]any)
	mid := int64(ids[0].(float64))
	e.must("POST", "/hooks/clock/advance", map[string]int{"seconds": 360})
	e.must("POST", "/hooks/sweep", map[string]any{})
	m, _ := e.a.Fleet.Get(ctx, e.a.Fleet.Pool(), mid)
	if !m.AgentOnline || !m.LastSeenAt.Equal(e.clk.Now()) || len(m.LastMetrics) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("after sweep = %+v", m)
	}
	e.must("POST", "/hooks/clock/advance", map[string]int{"seconds": 360})
	e.must("POST", "/hooks/sweep", map[string]any{"heartbeat": false})
	if m, _ = e.a.Fleet.Get(ctx, e.a.Fleet.Pool(), mid); m.AgentOnline {
		t.Fatal("heartbeat=false must let the sweeper mark offline")
	}
}
```

`TestSweepWithoutHeartbeatSuspends` 不变（语义相同）。`TestLoopbackOnly` 等不变。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现 `internal/e2ehooks/hooks.go`**

`Server` 改为：

```go
type Server struct {
	App       *app.App
	Pool      *pgxpool.Pool
	Clock     *clock.Fake
	KYC       *SwitchableKYC
	Log       *slog.Logger
	AgentAddr string // 真实网关监听地址；fake Agent 连到这里

	mu     sync.Mutex
	agents map[int64]*fakeagent.Agent
}

// Close 停掉全部 fake Agent。
func (s *Server) Close() {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	for _, a := range s.agents {
		a.Stop()
	}
	s.agents = nil
}
```

`Handler` 加 `mux.HandleFunc("GET /hooks/agent/status", s.agentStatus)`。

fake Agent 管理：

```go
const hookWait = 10 * time.Second

var fakeMetrics = metrics.Metrics{GPUUtil: 12, GPUTempC: 55, GPUPowerW: 30, MemTotal: 128 << 30, MemUsed: 8 << 30, RootDiskTotal: 4000 << 30, RootDiskUsed: 1600 << 30, Load1: 0.5}

// agentFor 返回机器的步进式 fake Agent；没有（或机器已被撤销接入）时签发令牌、接入、建流并等首个 StateReport 被处理。
func (s *Server) agentFor(ctx context.Context, machineID int64) (*fakeagent.Agent, error) {
	m, err := s.App.Fleet.Get(ctx, s.Pool, machineID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	if s.agents == nil {
		s.agents = map[int64]*fakeagent.Agent{}
	}
	ag, ok := s.agents[machineID]
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if ok && m.Enrolled {
		return ag, nil
	}
	if ok {
		ag.Stop()
	}
	tok, err := s.App.Fleet.IssueEnrollToken(ctx, audit.Actor{}, machineID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	ag, err = fakeagent.Start(ctx, fakeagent.Options{Addr: s.AgentAddr, CAPEM: s.App.Gateway.CAPEM(), Token: tok.Plain, Stepped: true,
		IPs: []string{m.LanIP}, Metrics: fakeMetrics, Log: s.Log})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("fake agent for machine %d: %w", machineID, err)
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	s.agents[machineID] = ag
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if err := s.waitUntil(ctx, "first state report", func() bool { return s.App.Gateway.ReportCount(machineID) >= 1 }); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return ag, nil
}

func (s *Server) agentIDs() []int64 {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	ids := make([]int64, 0, len(s.agents))
	for id := range s.agents {
		ids = append(ids, id)
	}
	sort.Slice(ids, func(i, j int) bool { return ids[i] < ids[j] })
	return ids
}

func (s *Server) waitUntil(ctx context.Context, what string, cond func() bool) error {
	deadline := time.Now().Add(hookWait)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if cond() {
			return nil
		}
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		case <-time.After(10 * time.Millisecond):
		}
	}
	return fmt.Errorf("timeout waiting for %s", what)
}
```

`machines` 钩子：请求体加 `Rack string \`json:"rack"\``（空则 `"E2E"`），`Register` 时用它；把 `s.App.Fleet.Heartbeat(ctx, m.ID)` 换成 `if _, err := s.agentFor(ctx, m.ID); err != nil { return nil, err }`。`hooks_test.go` 的 300 次序列号循环改为 100 次（每次都是真实接入，约 30ms 一次）。

`heartbeatAll` 改为：

```go
// heartbeatAll 让每个仍连着的 fake Agent 发一次心跳并等网关处理完（心跳走真实流，但由钩子触发，假时钟保持确定）。
// 整轮 Playwright 下来进程里会累积几百个 fake Agent：先并发发送、再统一等计数增加，避免串行几百次往返。
func (s *Server) heartbeatAll(ctx context.Context) error {
	ids := s.agentIDs()
	before := make(map[int64]int64, len(ids))
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	errs := make(chan error, len(ids))
	for _, id := range ids {
		s.mu.Lock()
		ag := s.agents[id]
		s.mu.Unlock()
		if !ag.Client.Connected() { // 被撤销接入的 Agent 不再心跳
			continue
		}
		before[id] = s.App.Gateway.HeartbeatCount(id)
		wg.Add(1)
		go func(id int64) {
			defer wg.Done()
			if err := ag.Client.SendHeartbeat(ctx); err != nil {
				errs <- fmt.Errorf("heartbeat %d: %w", id, err)
			}
		}(id)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	close(errs)
	for err := range errs {
		return err
	}
	for id, n := range before {
		if err := s.waitUntil(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("heartbeat %d", id), func() bool { return s.App.Gateway.HeartbeatCount(id) > n }); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

`agent` 钩子：

```go
// agent 让步进式 fake Agent 执行当前队列：先等网关把 pending 指令投递到 Agent，再执行并上报，最后等网关处理完本次报告。
func (s *Server) agent(ctx context.Context, body json.RawMessage) (any, error) {
	req, err := decode[struct {
		MachineID *int64 `json:"machine_id"`
		Fail      bool   `json:"fail"`
		Result    string `json:"result"`
	}](body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	var ids []int64
	if req.MachineID != nil {
		ids = []int64{*req.MachineID}
	} else {
		ids = s.agentIDs()
	}
	var override *state.Result
	if req.Fail {
		msg := req.Result
		if msg == "" {
			msg = "e2e: simulated failure"
		}
		override = &state.Result{OK: false, Message: msg}
	}
	completed := 0
	for _, mid := range ids {
		ag, err := s.agentFor(ctx, mid)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := s.waitUntil(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("delivery to machine %d", mid), func() bool {
			cmds, err := s.App.Fleet.PendingCommands(ctx, mid)
			if err != nil {
				return false
			}
			for _, c := range cmds {
				if c.Status != "sent" || !ag.Client.Received(c.ID) {
					return false
				}
			}
			return true
		}); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		before := s.App.Gateway.ReportCount(mid)
		n, err := ag.Client.Step(ctx, override)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("step machine %d: %w", mid, err)
		}
		if err := s.waitUntil(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("report of machine %d", mid), func() bool { return s.App.Gateway.ReportCount(mid) > before }); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		completed += n
	}
	return map[string]int{"completed": completed}, nil
}

// agentStatus 报告 fake Agent 是否仍连着；机器已被撤销接入时顺手停掉它的 Agent（否则它会以 ≤1s 退避无限重试 Unauthenticated 刷日志）。
func (s *Server) agentStatus(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id, err := strconv.ParseInt(r.URL.Query().Get("machine_id"), 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		writeJSON(w, 400, map[string]string{"error": "machine_id 不正确"})
		return
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	ag := s.agents[id]
	s.mu.Unlock()
	if m, err := s.App.Fleet.Get(r.Context(), s.Pool, id); err == nil && !m.Enrolled && ag != nil {
		ag.Stop()
		s.mu.Lock()
		delete(s.agents, id)
		s.mu.Unlock()
		ag = nil
	}
	connected := ag != nil && ag.Client.Connected() && s.App.Gateway.Connected(id)
	writeJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"connected": connected})
}
```

旧的 `machineIDs` 若不再使用则删除。import 追加 `"sort"`、`"strconv"`、`"sync"`、`"spark-cloud/internal/agent/fakeagent"`、`"spark-cloud/internal/agent/metrics"`、`"spark-cloud/internal/agent/state"`。

原来 `HandleCommandResult` 之后的 Agent 结果现在走真实流，所以钩子返回时实例状态已更新（网关在处理 StateReport 之前已顺序处理了 CommandResult）。

`web/admin/src/logic/metrics.ts`：

```ts
import type { MachineMetrics } from '@spark/shared'

export function fmtTemp(m: MachineMetrics | null | undefined): string {
  return m ? `${Math.round(m.gpu_temp_c)}°C` : '—'
}

export function fmtPercent(used: number, total: number): string {
  if (!total) return '—'
  return `${Math.round((used / total) * 100)}%`
}

export function rootUsage(m: MachineMetrics | null | undefined): string {
  return m ? fmtPercent(m.root_disk_used, m.root_disk_total) : '—'
}

const units = ['B', 'KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB']
export function fmtBytes(n: number): string {
  if (!n) return '0 B'
  let i = 0
  let v = n
  while (v >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1) {
    v /= 1024
    i++
  }
  return i === 0 ? `${v} B` : `${v.toFixed(1)} ${units[i]}`
}
```

`MachineDetailView.vue` 的 `<script setup>` 追加：

```ts
import { fmtBytes, fmtPercent, fmtTemp } from '../logic/metrics'
import type { EnrollCommand } from '@spark/shared'

const enrollVisible = ref(false)
const enroll = ref<EnrollCommand | null>(null)
const revokeVisible = ref(false)

/** 生成接入命令：请求层遇到 totp_required 会弹动态码框；命令只显示一次，关闭后不再可见（需要重新生成）。 */
async function generateEnroll() {
  if (busy.value) return
  busy.value = true
  try {
    enroll.value = await api.post<EnrollCommand>(`/machines/${id.value}/enroll-command`, {})
    enrollVisible.value = true
  } catch (e) {
    notifyError(e)
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
async function copyEnroll() {
  if (!enroll.value) return
  await navigator.clipboard.writeText(enroll.value.command)
  ElMessage.success('已复制')
}
function closeEnroll() {
  enrollVisible.value = false
  enroll.value = null
}
async function revokeEnrollment() {
  if (busy.value) return
  busy.value = true
  try {
    await api.post(`/machines/${id.value}/revoke-enrollment`, {})
    ElMessage.success('已撤销接入')
    revokeVisible.value = false
    await load()
  } catch (e) {
    revokeVisible.value = false
    notifyError(e)
  } finally {
    busy.value = false
  }
}
```

```vue
          <el-button data-testid="mdetail-maintenance-btn" @click="maintenanceVisible = true">{{ inMaintenance ? '结束维护' : '进入维护' }}</el-button>
          <el-button type="danger" plain data-testid="mdetail-power-btn" @click="powerVisible = true">断电重启</el-button>
          <el-button type="primary" plain :loading="busy" data-testid="mdetail-enroll-btn" @click="generateEnroll">生成接入命令</el-button>
          <el-button type="danger" plain :disabled="!data.machine.enrolled" data-testid="mdetail-revoke-btn" @click="revokeVisible = true">撤销接入</el-button>
        </div>
```

（原 `.actions` 块替换为上面的四个按钮。）指标卡片放在「历史实例」卡片之前：
